第一章 绪论：齐鲁乡土建筑的生成土壤

Chapter 1 Introduction: The Ground of Qilu Vernacular Architecture

01. 山东古建筑的生成背景

Formation Background of Ancient Architecture in Shandong
概述 自然环境与人文环境 总论 山东省全域

山东古称齐鲁，简称鲁，是中华文明的重要发祥地之一、儒家文化的摇篮、黄河文明与海洋文明的交汇之地。

距今约8400年至7700年的后李文化、约7000年的北辛文化、约6400年至4500年的大汶口文化、约4500年至4000年的龙山文化，在齐鲁大地上依次展开，构成了中国东部史前文化最完整的序列。

龙山文化的城子崖遗址、桐林遗址、两城镇遗址与尧王城遗址，把山东早期城邑营建的历史推至距今4000年以上。

山东地形以山地丘陵与平原交错为特征，中部泰沂山脉隆起，鲁中山区面积占全省一半以上；西北与西部为黄河冲积平原；东部胶东半岛伸入黄海渤海之间，海岸线绵长。境内黄河自西向东横贯入海，京杭大运河纵贯鲁西，微山湖等湖泊星罗棋布。

这种山海河湖兼备的地理格局，从根本上决定了山东乡土建筑材料的多样性与类型的丰富性。气候上，山东属暖温带季风气候，夏季多雨炎热，冬季寒冷干燥，春季多风沙。鲁中与胶东冬季寒冷而夏季湿润，鲁西鲁北则冬冷夏热、降水集中。

严酷的冬季促成了厚墙、火炕、小窗的建筑取向，多雨的夏季则催生了陡坡顶与排水组织，风沙与海风又分别塑造了鲁北的封闭院落与胶东的低矮厚实海草屋顶。文化上，山东是儒家文化发源地，宗法礼制对聚落结构与院落秩序影响深远。

齐文化尚功利重工商，鲁文化重礼教崇农耕，两种文化传统叠加，加之运河商贸、海洋渔耕、山地农耕三种生计方式并存，共同塑造了山东民居「因地制宜、因脉成型」的多元格局。

— ENGLISH —

Shandong, anciently known as Qi-Lu and abbreviated as Lu, is one of the principal cradles of Chinese civilisation, the birthplace of Confucian culture and the meeting point of the Yellow River civilisation and maritime culture. The Houli culture (c.

8400-7700 BP), Beixin culture (c. 7000 BP), Dawenkou culture (c. 6400-4500 BP) and Longshan culture (c. 4500-4000 BP) unfolded in succession across this land, forming the most complete prehistoric cultural sequence in eastern China.

Longshan walled sites such as Chengziya, Tonglin, Liangchengzhen and Yaowangcheng push the history of early urban construction in Shandong back more than four millennia.Topographically Shandong is characterised by an interlacing of mountains, hills and plains.

The Tai-Yi mountain system rises in the centre; the central Shandong highlands account for more than half of the province.

Alluvial plains created by the Yellow River occupy the northwest and west, while the Jiaodong Peninsula reaches out between the Yellow Sea and the Bohai Sea with a long indented coastline.

The Yellow River crosses the province from west to east, the Grand Canal runs north-south through western Shandong, and lakes such as Weishan Lake are scattered across the land.

This combination of mountain, sea, river and lake fundamentally determined the diversity of building materials and the richness of dwelling types found in Shandong.Climatically Shandong belongs to the warm temperate monsoon zone: hot rainy summers, cold dry winters and windy, dusty springs.

Harsh winters encouraged thick walls, heated kang beds and small window openings; rainy summers produced steeply pitched roofs and carefully organised drainage; wind-blown sand and sea gales respectively shaped the closed courtyards of northern Shandong and the low, massive seaweed roofs of Jiaodong.Culturally, Shandong is the home of Confucianism, and patriarchal ritual deeply influenced settlement structure and courtyard order.

The Qi state valued practicality, industry and commerce; the Lu state stressed ritual, teaching and agriculture.

These two traditions, combined with three coexisting livelihoods - canal commerce, marine fishing and mountain farming - produced the plural pattern of Shandong vernacular architecture summed up as adapting to local conditions and taking shape from local veins.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 后李文化·北辛文化·大汶口文化·龙山文化完整序列
  • 山海河湖兼备的地理格局
  • 暖温带季风气候：夏热多雨、冬寒干燥、春多风沙
  • 中部泰沂山脉、西部平原、东部半岛三大地貌单元
  • 齐文化重工商、鲁文化重礼教的双源传统
  • 黄河、京杭大运河、微山湖三大水系
  • 厚墙·火炕·小窗的御寒取向
  • 儒家宗法礼制对院落秩序的深刻影响

02. 山东古建筑的历史沿革

Historical Evolution of Ancient Architecture in Shandong
新石器时代至近代 历史分期 总论 山东省全域

一、原始社会时期。山东是中国史前建筑遗存最丰富的地区之一。章丘西河遗址距今8400年至7700年，是后李文化的典型遗址，房址为半地穴式，面积多在30至50平方米，室内设有灶坑，柱洞排列规整。

大汶口文化时期出现地面式建筑，墙体采用木骨泥墙，并出现分间与多室房屋。龙山文化时期，夯筑技术成熟，城子崖、桐林、两城镇、尧王城等城址相继出现，房屋形式有半地穴式、地面式与土台式，并普遍使用白灰面防潮处理。二、夏商周时期。

岳石文化承接龙山文化，城址与夯土台基继续发展。商代山东为东夷之地，商文化向东推进，城址如济南大辛庄、桓台史家等显现出夯土城垣与大型建筑基址。西周分封齐、鲁，齐都临淄、鲁都曲阜成为东方两大都邑，夯土高台建筑发达。

春秋战国时期，齐国修筑齐长城，西起长清广里，东至青岛黄岛入海，全长618.9公里，翻越1518座山峰，比秦长城早400余年，堪称中国长城之父。

临淄齐国故城、曲阜鲁国故城、邹城邾国故城规模宏大，城垣、宫殿基址、排水系统、手工业作坊遗存俱全。三、秦汉魏晋南北朝时期。秦统一后山东设郡县，汉代山东经济繁荣，画像石墓与石祠盛行。长清孝堂山郭氏墓石祠建于东汉初年，是我国现存最早的地面房屋建筑；

嘉祥武氏祠、沂南北寨汉墓画像石，忠实再现了汉代山东民居、祠堂、阙楼与桥梁的形象。魏晋南北朝时期，佛教东传，朗公寺即今神通寺创建于前秦皇始元年（351年），是山东境内最早的佛教寺院。

北魏正兴元年开始重建的灵岩寺，至唐代与国清寺、栖霞寺、玉泉寺并称海内四大名刹。四、隋唐宋金元时期。隋大业七年（611年）建成的四门塔是中国现存最早的单层亭阁式石塔。

北宋嘉祐六年（1061年）登州郡守朱处约创建蓬莱阁。

宋代崇儒重道，曲阜孔庙与泰安岱庙屡加扩建，岱庙天贶殿创建于宋大中祥符二年（1009年），与北京故宫太和殿、曲阜孔庙大成殿并称中国古代三大宫殿。

金明昌二年（1191年）大修孔庙，三分其役而因旧完葺者居其一、增创者倍之，殿堂廊庑达三百六十余楹。五、明清时期。这是山东乡土建筑全面定型与繁荣的阶段。明洪武年间设成山、靖海等卫，沿海建寨筑城，屯田军户村落蜂起，胶东海草房居住区域随之扩大。

清道光《荣成县志》载：邑中罕见瓦房，砌以石头，覆以茅，苫以海草，仅蔽风雨。明清两代曲阜孔庙、孔府形成九进院落、三路布局的宏大规模；

聊城山陕会馆自清乾隆八年（1743年）起历时66年建成；

栖霞牟氏庄园自清雍正元年（1723年）起营建至1935年，形成三组六院、480余间的格局。六、近代。山东是中国北方开埠最早的省份之一，烟台、青岛先后开埠，西式建筑与本土形式交融。

青岛形成独特的里院建筑，烟台朝阳街、所城里与威海刘公岛留下大量近代建筑，济南商埠区、广智院、洪家楼天主教堂等构成山东近代建筑的多元图景。

— ENGLISH —

1. Prehistoric period. Shandong possesses some of the richest prehistoric architectural remains in China.

The Xihe site in Zhangqiu, dating to 8400-7700 BP, is a type site of the Houli culture; its houses were semi-subterranean, generally 30 to 50 square metres, with hearths inside and regularly arranged post holes.

In the Dawenkou period ground-level houses appeared, using wattle-and-daub walls and showing division into rooms.

By the Longshan period rammed-earth technology was mature: walled sites appeared at Chengziya, Tonglin, Liangchengzhen and Yaowangcheng, with semi-subterranean, ground-level and platform house forms, and lime-sand floors were widely used for damp-proofing.2. Xia, Shang and Zhou.

The Yueshi culture continued Longshan traditions of walled settlements and rammed-earth platforms. The Zhou enfeoffment created Qi and Lu, whose capitals Linzi and Qufu became the two greatest eastern cities, both noted for rammed-earth terrace architecture.

In the Spring and Autumn and Warring States periods the state of Qi built the Great Wall of Qi, running 618.9 km from Guangli in Changqing in the west to the sea at Huangdao, Qingdao, in the east, crossing 1518 peaks.

Built more than four hundred years before the Qin great wall, it is rightly called the father of Chinese great walls.3. Qin, Han, Wei, Jin and Northern and Southern dynasties. Han dynasty Shandong was prosperous, and stone-chambered tombs and stone shrines flourished.

The Guo family stone shrine at Xiaotangshan in Changqing, built in the early Eastern Han, is the earliest surviving above-ground building in China. The Wu family shrines in Jiaxiang and the Beizhai Han tomb reliefs in Yinan faithfully depict Han dwelling houses, shrines, gate towers and bridges.

Buddhism arrived in this period: Langgong Temple (later Shentong Temple) was founded in 351, the earliest Buddhist monastery in Shandong.4. Sui, Tang, Song, Jin and Yuan. The Four-Door Pagoda, completed in 611, is China's oldest surviving single-storey pavilion-type stone pagoda.

Penglai Pavilion was founded in 1061 by Zhu Chuyue, prefect of Dengzhou. The Song dynasty revered Confucianism and Daoism, and both the Confucius Temple at Qufu and the Dai Temple at Mount Tai were repeatedly enlarged.

The Tiankuang Hall of the Dai Temple, erected in 1009, ranks with the Hall of Supreme Harmony in Beijing and the Dacheng Hall at Qufu as one of the three great palatial halls of ancient China.5. Ming and Qing. This was the period when Shandong vernacular architecture took definitive shape.

Under the Ming, coastal guards such as Chengshan and Jinghai were established; military colonies spread and the area of seaweed-roofed houses in Jiaodong expanded.

The Qing Daoguang edition of the Rongcheng county gazetteer records that tiled houses were rarely seen in the district; walls were laid in stone and roofs thatched with straw and covered with seaweed, barely sufficient to keep out wind and rain.

The Confucius Temple and Confucius Mansion reached their nine-courtyard, three-axis form; the Shanshan Guild Hall at Liaocheng took sixty-six years to complete from 1743; and the Mou Family Manor at Qixia was built up from 1723 until 1935 into three groups and six compounds with over 480 rooms.6.

Modern period. Shandong was among the earliest provinces in north China to open treaty ports.

Yantai and Qingdao were opened in turn, and Western architecture mingled with local forms: Qingdao produced the distinctive Liyuan courtyard block, while Yantai's Chaoyang Street and Suochengli, Weihai's Liugong Island and Jinan's commercial port district complete a plural picture of modern Shandong architecture.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 后李文化半地穴房址（30-50平方米）
  • 龙山文化夯筑城址群
  • 齐长城全长618.9公里，翻越1518座山峰，比秦长城早400余年
  • 孝堂山郭氏墓石祠：中国现存最早的地面房屋建筑
  • 朗公寺（351年）：山东境内最早的佛教寺院
  • 四门塔（611年）：中国现存最早的单层亭阁式石塔
  • 岱庙天贶殿（1009年）：中国古代三大宫殿之一
  • 青岛里院、烟台所城里、济南商埠区等近代建筑群

03. 山东传统民居的类型分区

Regional Typology of Shandong Traditional Dwellings
总论 民居区划 总论 山东省六大风貌区

山东乡土建筑研究者通常将全省民居划分为六大风貌区，各区在材料、结构、屋顶形式与院落组织上差异显著。一、胶东沿海区。包括青岛、烟台、威海、日照东部，以海草房最具代表性。墙体采用海岸花岗岩块石垒砌，厚达半米以上；屋顶以大叶藻即俗称的海带草苫盖，坡度在50度以上，苫厚约40厘米，屋脊压脊或以旧渔网罩顶防风。

院落以一进三合院或四合院为主，坐北朝南，正房与厢房交错，形成六面带尖顶的山墙。

威海现存海草房4000多栋，分布于环翠、荣成、文登、乳山的23个乡镇、600多个村落；荣成沿海最为古老集中。二、鲁中山区。包括济南南部、泰安、淄博、莱芜及临沂北部，石材资源丰富，形成石砌民居体系。

墙体以青石、石灰岩、砂岩或当地称作十八行子的片岩分层干垒，不用灰浆，采用上石压下缝、大石撑四角、小石填缝隙的干摞法则，以石条搭梁、石板覆顶或木梁铺草覆瓦。房屋多依山就势，上下两层，上层住人保暖防潮，下层储物。代表村落有莱芜逯家岭、卧云铺、济南长清方峪、章丘朱家峪、淄博博山与淄川诸石寨。三、鲁中南与鲁南丘陵区。

包括枣庄、济宁东部、临沂大部，盛产页岩，形成以石板为瓦的石板房。墙可垒至2至5米高，以厚1至2厘米的不规则薄石板错缝沿茬叠压铺顶。

院落以四合院为基本单元，正房面阔三间或四间，配房略矮，大门多独立并建门楼，门内设影壁。枣庄山亭兴隆庄是鲁南规模最大、保存最完整的石板房建筑群。四、鲁西平原区。

包括聊城、菏泽、济宁西部，地势平坦，布局方正，多采用三合院，南北向，大门楼、囤坡顶，圆拱券门窗、方格窗棂，青砖垛、土坯墙、灰黄墙面。聊城、临清一带因运河商贸而兴，会馆、钞关、商铺林立。五、鲁北平原区。

包括德州、滨州、东营、济南北部，为黄河冲积平原，土坯与夯土民居为主，平顶房即囤顶分布广泛。房顶做法主要有三种：老檐出头，即以厚厚的秫秸平铺在架好的檩条上，再以麦秸泥抹平，厚度约50厘米，但每年需抹泥防雨；

八砖漫顶，即以面积约为今砖两倍、厚度约为今砖三分之二的近方形大砖铺满屋顶，砖缝撒谷粒使其生根盘结以加固防雨；石灰捶顶，即以石灰、炉渣、泥土按比例加水混合，平铺在数层芦苇席上，厚约十几厘米，众人持木板反复捶打至表面平滑坚实。

墙体分落地白即全土坯，与砖砌碱脚加土坯两类，碱脚又有五行、七行、九行、十一行高低之分。六、鲁西南区。包括菏泽、济宁、枣庄西部、临沂西部，山区随山就势、平原布局规整，以青砖、石材、粉墙、灰瓦为主，素雅古朴。

此外，微山湖渔民以船为家的连家船，是山东民居中极特殊的例子。

— ENGLISH —

Researchers usually divide Shandong vernacular dwellings into six regional style zones, each markedly different in material, structure, roof form and courtyard organisation.1. Jiaodong coastal zone, covering Qingdao, Yantai, Weihai and eastern Rizhao, is represented above all by the seaweed house.

Walls are laid in coastal granite blocks more than half a metre thick; roofs are thatched with eelgrass, locally called sea-strap grass, to a depth of about 40 cm on a pitch greater than 50 degrees, the ridge weighted or netted with old fishing nets against wind.

The plan is usually a single-bay three-side or four-side courtyard facing south, with principal and wing rooms interlocking to produce six gabled, pointed walls. More than 4,000 seaweed houses survive in Weihai across 23 townships and over 600 villages.2.

Central Shandong mountain zone, covering southern Jinan, Tai'an, Zibo, Laiwu and northern Linyi, is rich in stone and produced a complete stone-dwelling system.

Walls are dry-laid in coursed limestone, sandstone or the locally named eighteen-layer schist, with no mortar, following the rule that upper stones press the joints below, large stones brace the four corners and small stones fill the gaps.

Houses cling to the slope, often in two storeys, with living quarters above and storage below.3. South-central and southern Shandong hill zone, covering Zaozhuang, eastern Jining and most of Linyi, is rich in shale and produced stone-slab-roofed houses.

Walls rise 2 to 5 metres and the roof is laid in irregular slabs 1 to 2 cm thick, overlapped course by course. Xinglongzhuang in Shanting district, Zaozhuang, is the largest and best preserved group of slab-stone houses in southern Shandong.4.

Western Shandong plain zone, covering Liaocheng, Heze and western Jining, is flat and regular in layout, with three-side courtyards facing south, gate towers, flat or gently cambered roofs, arched openings and lattice windows, using grey brick piers, adobe walls and yellowish walls.

Thanks to Grand Canal commerce, Liaocheng and Linqing are rich in guild halls, customs houses and shops.5.

Northern Shandong plain zone, covering Dezhou, Binzhou, Dongying and northern Jinan, lies on the Yellow River alluvial plain; adobe and rammed earth predominate and flat trap roofs are widespread.

Three roof techniques are characteristic: the projecting-eave roof, in which thick sorghum stalks are laid over purlins and rendered with straw clay to some 50 cm, requiring annual renewal; the eight-brick paved roof, using near-square bricks about twice the area and two-thirds the thickness of modern bricks, with millet seed scattered into the joints so that sprouting roots bound them and improved rain resistance; and the lime-beaten roof, in which a mix of lime, slag and earth is laid over several layers of reed matting to a depth of some ten-odd centimetres and beaten with boards until smooth and solid.6.

South-western Shandong zone, covering Heze, Jining, western Zaozhuang and western Linyi, uses grey brick, stone, whitewashed walls and grey tiles. In addition, the linked-family boats of the Weishan Lake fishermen, in which families lived entirely aboard, are a highly unusual case in Shandong.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 胶东海草房：石墙+海草苫顶，坡度50度以上
  • 鲁中山地干摞石屋：无灰浆错缝咬合
  • 鲁南石板房：薄石板错缝叠压铺顶
  • 鲁西平原：三合院、囤坡顶、青砖土坯墙
  • 鲁北平顶房三法：老檐出头、八砖漫顶、石灰捶顶
  • 鲁西南：青砖粉墙灰瓦，素雅古朴
  • 微山湖连家船：以船为家的特殊居住形态
  • 威海现存海草房4000余栋，分布于23个乡镇600余村

04. 山东传统村落的保护名录与分布格局

Protection Registers and Distribution Pattern of Shandong Traditional Villages
当代 保护名录 省级名录体系 济南·淄博·烟台·潍坊·济宁·临沂等

自2012年住房城乡建设部等部门启动传统村落保护以来，山东省已构建起层次清晰的保护名录体系。

截至第六批名单公布，全省共有168个村落列入中国传统村落名录，其中第一批10个、第二批6个、第三批21个、第四批38个、第五批50个、第六批43个；

同时命名保护了385个省级传统村落，第六批省级名单公布后省级传统村落总数已达389个。

此外，全省拥有中国历史文化名镇4个、中国历史文化名村11个，省级历史文化名镇45个、省级历史文化名村60个。所有名镇名村和传统村落均已编制保护规划或方案，建立一镇一档、一村一档。

全省已有5个县入选国家传统村落集中连片保护利用示范县，6个县推进省级传统村落集中连片保护利用。从地域空间分布看，山东各类名镇名村和传统村落整体呈现大分散、小集中，山地丘陵多、平原少的总体特点。鲁中、胶东是全省集聚的核心区域，其中济南、淄博、烟台、潍坊、济宁、临沂六市资源富集度最高、分布最为集中、文化底蕴最为深厚。

从廊道分布看，整体布局贴合齐鲁历史地理脉络：沿黄河、运河水系形成临水聚落带；沿古官道驿路呈现串珠式点状分布；沿传统商贸要道集聚形成带状连片分布。山地丘陵区域以传统村落集群为主体，古官道、商贸要道沿线则以历史文化名镇名村居多，驿站型、商贸型名镇名村辨识度高。从区域风貌看，鲁中、鲁西南山地丘陵区域多依山就势、顺势而建，石砌民居、石路街巷风貌特征典型，集中承载了山地农耕文明、齐文化与传统儒家宗族文化；

胶东依托滨海区位优势，形成以临海古村、海防名镇名村为核心的资源格局，集中展现胶东半岛海洋渔耕文化与明清海防军事文化。

2025年，山东省住房城乡建设厅等五部门联合印发《关于加强传统村落保护和发展的实施意见》，从加强传统村落调查建档、科学开展规划编制、推进村落整体保护发展等方面明确工作要求、制定保障措施，有效遏制了传统村落衰败趋势，激发了乡村内生动力。

— ENGLISH —

Since the Ministry of Housing and Urban-Rural Development and other departments launched traditional village protection in 2012, Shandong has built a clearly layered protection register.

As of the announcement of the sixth batch, 168 villages in the province have been inscribed on the China Traditional Village list: ten in the first batch, six in the second, twenty-one in the third, thirty-eight in the fourth, fifty in the fifth and forty-three in the sixth.

At the same time 385 provincial-level traditional villages had been designated, a figure that reached 389 with the sixth provincial batch.

The province also has four China Historical and Cultural Towns, eleven China Historical and Cultural Villages, forty-five provincial historical and cultural towns and sixty provincial historical and cultural villages.

Conservation plans or schemes have been prepared for every one of them under a one town one file, one village one file system.Five counties have been selected as national demonstration counties for concentrated and contiguous protection and utilisation of traditional villages, and six counties are advancing provincial-level concentrated protection.In spatial terms the distribution is broadly dispersed but locally clustered, with many sites in mountains and hills and few on plains.

Central Shandong and Jiaodong are the two core clusters; Jinan, Zibo, Yantai, Weifang, Jining and Linyi are the six cities with the richest and most concentrated resources.

Along corridors the pattern follows the historical geography of Qi-Lu: waterfront settlement belts along the Yellow River and the Grand Canal; bead-like point distribution along old official roads and post roads; and belt-like clusters along traditional trade routes.In the central and south-western hill areas, villages follow the contours of the slope, with stone dwellings and stone-paved lanes that carry mountain farming civilisation, Qi culture and Confucian clan culture.

Jiaodong, relying on its coastal position, has a resource pattern centred on seaside old villages and coastal defence towns and villages, expressing the maritime fishing culture of the peninsula and the Ming-Qing coastal defence military culture.In 2025 five provincial departments jointly issued the Implementing Opinions on Strengthening the Protection and Development of Traditional Villages, setting out requirements for survey and documentation, plan preparation and holistic village protection, with supporting measures that have effectively checked the decline of traditional villages and stimulated endogenous rural vitality.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 中国传统村落168个（第一批10·第二批6·第三批21·第四批38·第五批50·第六批43）
  • 省级传统村落389个
  • 中国历史文化名镇4个、名村11个
  • 省级历史文化名镇45个、名村60个
  • 大分散、小集中，山地丘陵多、平原少
  • 济南·淄博·烟台·潍坊·济宁·临沂六市最富集
  • 5个国家集中连片保护利用示范县、6个省级
  • 2025年《关于加强传统村落保护和发展的实施意见》

05. 山东民居的气候适应性与生态智慧

Climatic Adaptability and Ecological Wisdom of Shandong Dwellings
总论 生态建筑技术 总论 山东省全域

山东民居的每一种形式，几乎都能在气候与资源条件中找到直接的技术解释，构成了一套完整的低技术生态建筑经验。在御寒方面，山东各地普遍采用厚重围护结构。胶东海草房半米多厚的石墙与厚达40厘米的海草顶共同形成保温层，实现冬暖夏凉；鲁西鲁北的土坯墙厚约60厘米，平顶房顶厚可达50厘米，冬天保温、夏天防晒效果显著。

室内普遍设置火炕，胶东火炕横接房间东西两墙，一进门即可上炕；冬季在正房屋内灶台做饭，热气经炕洞加热炕体，夏季则移至厢房灶中做饭，火炕转为凉炕，铺篾席以度暑。这种季节性用灶的转换，是极低成本的被动式调节策略。

在防水方面，海草房屋顶保持约50度的坡度，使雨水迅速沥出，避免积水导致海草腐烂；同时海草中的硅藻附着使叶片耐腐蚀，加之含盐分与胶质，防虫蛀、不易霉烂，也不易燃烧，用打火机点燃海草只起暗烟而不起明火。鲁中石板顶与鲁南薄石板顶同样依靠坡度与叠压组织排水，山区聚落则利用天然沟壑与石板路构成完整的排水系统。在防风方面，胶东渔民在屋顶覆盖废弃渔网并以石块作坠子垂脚，起到防风、防鸟的作用；

鲁北平顶房的囤顶形式降低了迎风面，封闭的院落与高院墙则阻挡风沙。在防潮方面，鲁中山地石屋多设二层，上层住人、下层储物，避免地面湿气；山区院落普遍采用高台阶、青石根基，以防雨水与洪水冲刷。

在材料循环方面，山东民居几乎全部就地取材：沿海取石与海草，山区取青石与片岩，平原取土制坯、取麦草和泥。材料可完全回归自然，拆除后无不可降解残留，被研究者称为生态民居活标本。当代研究进一步引入定量方法。针对寒冷地区农村住宅，学界提出附加阳光间与节能火墙耦合供暖的技术方案，显著改善冬季室内热环境；对鲁西地区村镇住宅的节能研究表明，传统厚重围护结构的蓄热性能与现代轻质材料相比仍具优势。

2006年，荣成海草房建筑技艺被列入山东省非物质文化遗产名录；2021年，莱芜和庄石头屋子干摞技艺同样入选省级非遗代表性项目名录。

— ENGLISH —

Almost every form found in Shandong vernacular architecture can be explained directly by climate and available resources, constituting a complete body of low-technology ecological building experience.For winter protection, heavy envelopes are universal.

In Jiaodong, walls over half a metre thick and seaweed roofs 40 cm deep form a combined insulating layer producing the celebrated cool in summer and warm in winter effect.

In western and northern Shandong, adobe walls are about 60 cm thick and flat roofs can reach 50 cm, giving excellent winter insulation and summer shading.

Heated kang beds are standard; in Jiaodong the kang spans the east-west walls so that one steps onto it on entering.

In winter cooking is done on the stove in the principal room so that hot flue gases heat the kang; in summer cooking moves to the wing-room stove so that the kang becomes a cool platform covered with a woven mat.

This seasonal switching of stoves is an extremely low-cost passive strategy.For rain protection, the seaweed roof keeps a pitch of about 50 degrees so that rainwater drains rapidly, preventing ponding that would rot the thatch.

Diatoms adhering to the leaves make the seaweed corrosion resistant, while its salt and gum content resists insects and mould; it also resists fire, a lighter held to a strand producing only dull smoke rather than an open flame.

Stone-slab roofs in central and southern Shandong rely similarly on pitch and overlapping, and mountain settlements use natural gullies and flagged lanes as an integrated drainage system.For wind protection, Jiaodong fishermen spread disused nets over the roof weighted with stones, guarding against wind and birds; the flat trap roofs of northern Shandong reduce the windward surface, while enclosed courtyards and high walls block blown sand.For damp protection, mountain stone houses are often two-storeyed with living quarters above and storage below; courtyards use high steps and stone footings to resist rain and flash floods.In material terms, Shandong dwellings are almost entirely built from local sources: stone and seaweed on the coast, limestone and schist in the hills, earth for adobe and straw for clay render on the plains.

Materials return wholly to nature with no non-degradable residue, which is why researchers call the seaweed house a living specimen of ecological dwelling.Contemporary research has added quantitative methods.

For cold-region rural housing, scholars have proposed coupled heating combining a sunspace with an energy-saving hot wall, markedly improving winter indoor thermal conditions; studies of energy use in western Shandong villages show that the thermal mass of traditional heavy envelopes still outperforms that of modern lightweight materials.

In 2006 the craft of Rongcheng seaweed houses was inscribed on the Shandong provincial intangible cultural heritage list, and in 2021 the dry-stacking craft of stone houses at Hezhuang, Laiwu, was likewise inscribed.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 厚重围护结构：石墙半米、海草顶40厘米、土坯墙60厘米
  • 火炕与季节性换灶的被动式调节
  • 海草含硅藻、盐分与胶质：防腐防虫不易燃
  • 海草顶50度坡度利于快速排水
  • 渔网罩顶以石块垂脚防风防鸟
  • 二层石屋：上层住人、下层储物防潮
  • 就地取材、可完全降解的生态民居活标本
  • 2006年海草房技艺、2021年干摞技艺入选省级非遗

第二章 聚落源流：城址、长城与运河古镇

Chapter 2 Settlements: City Sites, the Great Wall of Qi and Canal Towns

06. 大汶口遗址

Dawenkou Site
新石器时代（距今6400-4500年） 史前聚落遗址 全国重点文物保护单位（1982年第二批） 泰安市岱岳区大汶口镇，大汶河两岸

大汶口遗址位于泰山南麓泰安市大汶口镇，大汶河自东向西贯穿，将遗址分为南北两片。遗址总面积80余万平方米，文化层堆积2至3米，是大汶口文化的发现地与命名地。

遗址包括了大汶口文化发展的全过程，距今约6400年至4500年，1982年被国务院公布为第二批全国重点文物保护单位。

三次发掘显示，遗址内涵极为丰富，包含墓葬、房址与窖穴等遗存。出土的陶、石、玉、骨、牙器等不同质料的生产工具、生活用具和装饰品都异常精美。生活用具主要有鼎、豆、壶、罐、钵、盘、杯等器皿，分彩陶、红陶、白陶、灰陶、黑陶几种，特别是彩陶器皿，花纹精细匀称，几何形图案规整。生产工具有磨制精致的石斧、石锛、石凿和磨制骨器，其中骨针磨制之精细，几可与今针媲美。

从建筑史的角度看，大汶口遗址的意义在于它完整呈现了黄河下游地区从母系氏族社会末期向父系氏族社会过渡阶段的居住形态演变。早期房址以半地穴式为主，面积较小，室内设灶坑，反映出以单个家庭为单位的生活方式；中晚期出现地面式建筑与分间房屋，墙体采用木骨泥墙做法，即以木柱为骨架、编织枝条后内外抹泥，部分房址还采用烧烤使地面与墙面陶化的防潮技术。

窖穴与房屋的组合关系显示出明确的储藏与居住功能分区，这是山东地区聚落规划意识最早的实物证据之一。墓葬以仰卧伸直葬为主，普遍随葬獐牙，有的还随葬猪头、猪骨以象征财富。随葬品的多寡差异，说明当时已出现明显的社会分层；这种分层随后在建筑上表现为房屋规模与区位等级的差别，并在数千年后的山东传统村落中，以台阶级数、正厢等级的方式被反复重演。

大汶口文化的发现，为山东地区的龙山文化找到了渊源，也为研究黄淮流域及山东、江浙沿海地区原始文化提供了重要线索。

— ENGLISH —

The Dawenkou site lies at the southern foot of Mount Tai in Dawenkou town, Tai'an, with the Dawen River running east to west through it and dividing the site into northern and southern halves.

Covering more than 800,000 square metres with cultural deposits 2 to 3 metres deep, it is the discovery site and type site of the Dawenkou culture.

It spans the entire development of that culture, roughly 6400 to 4500 years ago, and was listed in 1982 in the second batch of National Key Cultural Heritage Sites.Three excavation campaigns revealed an exceptionally rich site containing tombs, house foundations and storage pits.

Objects of pottery, stone, jade, bone and ivory - tools, vessels and ornaments - are all of extraordinary refinement.

Vessels include ding tripods, dou stemmed bowls, hu jars, guan jars, bo bowls, pan dishes and bei cups, in painted, red, white, grey and black wares; the painted pottery in particular carries fine, well-balanced geometric designs.

Production tools include finely ground stone axes, adzes and chisels and worked bone implements; the bone needles are so finely ground that they rival modern needles.For architectural history the significance of Dawenkou lies in the way it documents the evolution of dwelling form in the lower Yellow River region during the transition from the late matrilineal to the patrilineal stage.

Early houses were mainly semi-subterranean and small, with hearths inside, reflecting household-scale living.

In the middle and late phases ground-level and multi-room houses appeared, using wattle-and-daub walls with timber frames and woven branches rendered with clay inside and out; some floors and wall faces were fired to a ceramic hardness for damp-proofing.

The relationship between storage pits and houses shows a clear functional zoning of storage and dwelling among the earliest physical evidence of settlement planning consciousness in Shandong.Burials are predominantly extended supine inhumations, commonly accompanied by roe-deer teeth, and in some cases by pig skulls or bones symbolising wealth.

Differences in grave goods indicate marked social stratification, which was subsequently expressed architecturally in house size and location - a hierarchy that would be re-enacted thousands of years later in Shandong villages through the number of steps at a doorway and the grading of principal versus wing rooms.The discovery of the Dawenkou culture traced the origin of the Longshan culture in Shandong and provided important clues for the study of primitive cultures in the Huang-Huai basin and the coastal regions of Shandong, Jiangsu and Zhejiang.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 大汶口文化命名地，距今6400-4500年
  • 总面积80余万平方米，文化层厚2至3米
  • 半地穴式房址向地面式、分间房屋演变
  • 木骨泥墙与烧烤陶化防潮技术
  • 窖穴与房屋的功能分区：山东聚落规划意识的最早证据
  • 随葬獐牙与猪头的财富象征
  • 骨针磨制精细，几可与今针媲美
  • 为龙山文化找到渊源

07. 城子崖遗址

Chengziya Site
新石器时代龙山文化（距今4500-4000年） 史前城址 全国重点文物保护单位（1961年第一批） 济南市章丘区龙山街道龙山村东北

城子崖遗址位于济南市章丘龙山镇龙山村东北，总面积22万平方米，年代大约在公元前2500年至公元前2100年。

该遗址于1928年由吴金鼎发现，1930年至1931年由中央研究院历史语言研究所与山东省教育厅联合发掘，首次发现磨光黑陶。

城子崖遗址包括龙山文化、岳石文化等遗存及部分商代遗物，1961年国务院公布为第一批全国重点文物保护单位。城子崖的发现具有划时代意义。

它首次揭示出一种以磨光黑陶为特征、独立于仰韶文化之外的史前文化，被命名为龙山文化，从根本上动摇了中国文化西来说，被称为中国考古圣地。从建筑史角度看，城子崖是中国早期城邑营建技术的重要标本。遗址发现的城墙采用夯筑技术修筑，墙体依台地边缘而建，形成近似长方形的城圈。夯层清晰、夯窝密集，说明当时已掌握较为成熟的版筑工艺。

岳石文化时期的城址在龙山城址基础上继续修筑与利用，展现出城垣营建的连续传统。遗址内发现的房址包括半地穴式、地面式与土台式三种形式。地面式房屋多在垫土台基上建造，墙基采用夯土或土坯，室内地面普遍敷设有白灰面，即以石灰质材料抹面并经压实打磨，形成坚硬、防潮、易清洁的居住面。

这一做法在此后的山东乃至华北地区民居中延续数千年，是白灰面防潮工艺的最早源头之一。城子崖所在的章丘，同时也是后李文化西河遗址的所在地与齐长城的起点区域，构成从距今8400年延续至春秋战国的完整建筑史序列。

今日龙山文化博物馆与考古体验区已对外开放，使这一中国考古圣地成为公众理解山东建筑源流的重要现场。

— ENGLISH —

The Chengziya site lies northeast of Longshan village in Zhangqiu district, Jinan, covering some 220,000 square metres and dating roughly from 2500 to 2100 BC.

It was discovered by Wu Jinding in 1928 and excavated jointly in 1930-31 by the Institute of History and Philology of the Academia Sinica and the Shandong provincial education department, when polished black pottery was identified for the first time.

The site contains Longshan and Yueshi culture remains together with some Shang material, and was listed in 1961 in the first batch of National Key Cultural Heritage Sites.The discovery of Chengziya was epoch-making.

It revealed for the first time a prehistoric culture characterised by polished black pottery and distinct from Yangshao, duly named the Longshan culture, and fundamentally undermined the theory that Chinese culture came from the west.

The site is honoured as a holy place of Chinese archaeology.For architectural history, Chengziya is an important specimen of early Chinese urban construction. The city wall was built of rammed earth along the edge of the terrace, forming a roughly rectangular enclosure.

The rammed layers are clearly visible and the tamping pits dense, showing that a fairly mature formwork-and-tamping technique had been mastered.

The Yueshi phase city continued to build upon and reuse the Longshan walls, demonstrating a continuous tradition of city-wall construction.House foundations at the site fall into three types: semi-subterranean, ground-level and platform.

Ground-level houses were generally raised on made-up earth platforms, with rammed-earth or adobe footings, and their interior floors were commonly finished with a lime-sand surface - a calcareous render compacted and polished to produce a hard, damp-proof and easily cleaned living surface.

This technique persisted for millennia in the dwellings of Shandong and north China, and Chengziya is one of its earliest sources.Zhangqiu, where the site lies, also contains the Houli culture site at Xihe and the western starting point of the Great Wall of Qi, forming a continuous architectural sequence from 8400 years ago through to the Spring and Autumn period.

The Longshan Culture Museum and a hands-on archaeology area are now open to the public, making this holy place of archaeology an important venue for understanding the origins of Shandong architecture.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 1928年吴金鼎发现，1930-1931年联合发掘
  • 首次发现磨光黑陶，命名为龙山文化
  • 总面积22万平方米，年代约公元前2500-2100年
  • 夯筑城垣：版筑工艺的早期标本
  • 半地穴式、地面式、土台式三种房址并存
  • 白灰面防潮工艺的最早源头之一
  • 龙山文化、岳石文化、商代遗存叠压
  • 中国考古圣地，动摇中国文化西来说

08. 桐林遗址与两城镇遗址

Tonglin Site and Liangchengzhen Site
新石器时代龙山文化 史前城址与聚落 全国重点文物保护单位 淄博市临淄区桐林村 / 日照市东港区两城镇

山东龙山文化城址并非孤例。除城子崖外，鲁北的桐林遗址与鲁东南的两城镇遗址、尧王城遗址，共同构成山东史前城址群，把中国早期城邑的分布格局由单点扩展为网络。桐林遗址又称桐林田旺遗址，位于淄博市临淄区，是鲁北地区规模较大的龙山文化城址之一。

考古调查与发掘显示，城址平面近方形，城墙夯筑，城外有壕沟环绕，构成城垣与壕沟双重防御体系。城内发现有夯土台基、房址、窖穴与陶窑，显示出居住、储藏与手工业的明确分区。学者在山东龙山文化城墙研究中指出，鲁北地区龙山城址的兴起与当时人口增长、资源竞争加剧以及社会组织复杂化直接相关；城垣不仅是防御设施，更是社会权力空间化的物质表现。

这一论断把山东早期建筑史从技术史推进到社会史层面。两城镇遗址位于日照市东港区，是鲁东南地区规模最大的龙山文化遗址之一，面积逾百万平方米。该遗址是中美联合考古长期合作的田野基地，通过系统的区域系统调查，揭示出围绕中心聚落的多层级聚落结构。

在房址复原研究方面，学者以藤花落与两城镇遗址的典型房址为例，对龙山文化建筑技艺进行过系统探讨：房址多为地面式，平面以长方形为主，开间由单间向双间、多间发展；墙体以木骨泥墙为主，重要房屋使用夯土台基并铺设白灰面；屋顶形态推定为两面坡或四面坡的草顶，以木柱承檩、檩上铺椽、椽上铺草束并以泥背压固。

这套构造逻辑与后世山东民居的抬梁式木构架一脉相承。两城镇与桐林的研究还表明，龙山时期山东已存在明显的聚落等级：区域中心、次级中心与普通村落的规模差异显著，这种层级结构在此后三千年的山东城乡体系中反复重现，构成齐鲁聚落形态的长时段底色。

— ENGLISH —

Longshan walled sites in Shandong are not isolated cases.

Besides Chengziya, the Tonglin site in northern Shandong together with the Liangchengzhen and Yaowangcheng sites in the south-east form a network of prehistoric walled towns, expanding the pattern of early Chinese urbanism from a single point to a system.The Tonglin site, also known as Tonglin-Tianwang, lies in Linzi district, Zibo, and is one of the larger Longshan walled sites of northern Shandong.

Survey and excavation show a near-square plan with rammed-earth walls enclosed by an outer ditch, forming a dual defensive system of rampart and moat.

Inside the city, rammed-earth platforms, house foundations, storage pits and pottery kilns have been found, indicating a clear zoning of dwelling, storage and craft production.Scholars working on the city walls of the Shandong Longshan culture argue that the rise of walled sites in northern Shandong was directly related to population growth, intensifying competition for resources and increasing social complexity: the wall was not merely a defensive work but the material expression of social power in space.

This judgement moves the architectural history of early Shandong from a technical to a social plane.The Liangchengzhen site in Donggang district, Rizhao, is one of the largest Longshan sites in south-eastern Shandong, covering over a million square metres.

It has long served as a field base for Sino-American collaborative archaeology; systematic regional survey has revealed a multi-tiered settlement structure organised around a central place.Research on house reconstruction has examined Longshan building technique through typical houses at Tenghualuo and Liangchengzhen: houses were mainly ground-level and rectangular in plan, developing from single to double and multiple bays; walls were largely wattle-and-daub; important buildings used rammed-earth platforms with lime-sand floors; and roofs are reconstructed as double- or four-pitched thatch, with posts carrying purlins, purlins carrying rafters, and rafters carrying bundles of grass weighted with a clay bed.

This constructional logic is directly ancestral to the post-and-lintel timber frames of later Shandong dwellings.Studies at Liangchengzhen and Tonglin also show that clear settlement hierarchies existed in Longshan Shandong, with marked differences in scale between regional centres, secondary centres and ordinary villages - a hierarchy that recurs throughout the following three millennia of Shandong's urban-rural system and forms the long-term ground-tone of Qilu settlement morphology.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 鲁北桐林、鲁东南两城镇与尧王城构成史前城址群
  • 桐林城址近方形，城墙夯筑、外围壕沟
  • 两城镇遗址面积逾百万平方米，中美联合考古基地
  • 区域系统调查揭示多层级聚落结构
  • 龙山房址：地面式、长方形、单间向多间发展
  • 木骨泥墙+夯土台基+白灰面
  • 屋顶推定：两面坡或四面坡草顶，泥背压固
  • 城垣是社会权力空间化的物质表现

09. 齐长城

The Great Wall of Qi
春秋至战国 军事防御工程 全国重点文物保护单位 西起济南长清孝里街道广里村，东至青岛黄岛区东于家河村入海

齐长城横亘于齐鲁大地，始建于春秋时期，距今已2500余年。它最早载于《管子》：长城之阳，鲁也；长城之阴，齐也。司马迁《史记·楚世家》中载：乘山岭之上筑长城，东至海，西至济洲，千余里以备楚。

齐长城源于济南市长清区孝里街道广里村北、古济水河东岸，从大峰山山顶通过，蜿蜒千里，直达青岛市黄岛区东于家河村北入海，全长618.9公里，共翻越1518座山峰。长清县境内有两条齐长城，即主线和复线。

主线西起孝里，经双泉、马山、万德与泰山相连；复线均在主线以南长清与肥城、泰安交界线上。两道长城之间为屯兵营房和石砌通道。城墙一般高5米，厚2.5米；城墙内侧有守城马道，高4米，宽2米；马道下依墙建石屋。

长清境内齐长城共约97.8公里，翻越山头294座。章丘境内长约70公里，是章丘年代最早的古建筑遗迹。齐长城在中国历史上占有重要地位。

它比欧洲人公元前459年修建的79公里长雅典壁垒早200余年，比秦长城早400余年，堪称中国长城之父、世界壁垒之最。从建造技术看，齐长城充分体现了因地制宜的原则：山地段以块石干垒，利用自然山脊与陡崖；

丘陵与平地段则夯土版筑，局部外包石块。沿线设关隘、烽火台与屯兵堡，形成完整的预警与防御体系。其中莱芜和庄镇的青石关是齐长城规模最大的关隘，被称为齐鲁第一关，历来为兵家必争之地。齐长城不仅是一道军事工程，更是一条深刻影响了山东聚落分布的文化地理界线。长城以南是鲁国的礼乐农耕世界，以北是齐国的工商渔盐之利；

沿线形成的关隘型聚落、驿站型聚落与屯兵型村落，构成了山东古村落中最具军事色彩的一支。

— ENGLISH —

Stretching across the land of Qi and Lu, the Great Wall of Qi was first built in the Spring and Autumn period, more than 2,500 years ago. It is recorded earliest in the Guanzi: south of the long wall is Lu, north of the long wall is Qi.

Sima Qian's Shiji, in the Hereditary House of Chu, records that a long wall was built along the mountain ridges, reaching east to the sea and west to Jizhou, over a thousand li, as a defence against Chu.The wall began north of Guangli village in Xiaoli subdistrict, Changqing district, Jinan, on the east bank of the ancient Ji River, crossed the summit of Mount Dafeng and wound for a thousand li to enter the sea north of Dongyuhe village in Huangdao district, Qingdao - a total length of 618.9 km across 1,518 peaks.Within Changqing there are two lines, a main line and a secondary line.

The main line starts at Xiaoli in the west and runs through Shuangquan, Mashan and Wande to connect with Mount Tai; the secondary line lies south of the main line along the Changqing-Feicheng and Tai'an boundaries. Between the two lines were barracks and stone-paved passages.

The wall generally stands 5 metres high and 2.5 metres thick; inside it runs a patrol path 4 metres high and 2 metres wide, beneath which stone huts were built against the wall.

Within Changqing the wall extends about 97.8 km over 294 peaks; within Zhangqiu about 70 km, the oldest architectural remains in that district.The Great Wall of Qi occupies an important place in Chinese history.

Built more than two hundred years before the 79 km Athenian Long Walls of 459 BC and more than four hundred years before the Qin great wall, it deserves its titles of father of Chinese great walls and foremost rampart of the world.In construction technique it fully embodies the principle of adapting to local conditions: in the mountains it is dry-laid in stone blocks, following natural ridges and cliffs; in hills and plains it is of rammed earth with local stone facing.

Passes, beacon towers and garrison forts along the line formed a complete warning and defence system.

Among them Qingshi Pass at Hezhuang in Laiwu is the largest pass on the wall, known as the first pass of Qi-Lu and long contested by strategists.The wall was more than a military work; it was a cultural-geographical line that profoundly shaped settlement distribution in Shandong.

South of it lay the ritual and agrarian world of Lu, north of it the commercial, fishing and salt wealth of Qi. Pass settlements, post settlements and garrison villages formed along its course, constituting the most strongly military strand among Shandong's old villages.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 始建于春秋时期，距今2500余年，全长618.9公里
  • 翻越1518座山峰
  • 比雅典壁垒早200余年，比秦长城早400余年
  • 中国长城之父、世界壁垒之最
  • 城墙高5米、厚2.5米，内侧马道高4米宽2米
  • 山地段干垒块石，平地段夯土版筑
  • 青石关：齐长城规模最大的关隘，齐鲁第一关
  • 长清境内97.8公里、翻越294座山头

10. 曲阜明故城与鲁国故城

Qufu Ming Walled City and the Ancient Capital of Lu
周至明清 都城与府城 全国重点文物保护单位 / 世界文化遗产 济宁市曲阜市明故城中心

曲阜是鲁国都城所在地与孔子故里，其城市史跨越三千年。周代鲁国故城规模宏大，城垣、宫殿基址、排水系统、手工业作坊遗存俱全，是周代东方最重要的都邑之一。今日所见的曲阜明故城，则成型于明代迁城卫庙之举。

明正德年间，因孔庙孔府屡遭兵燹与盗匪侵扰，明武宗下令移城卫庙，即以孔庙、孔府为中心修筑一座新城。

工程历时十年，建成周长约4.8公里的砖砌城墙，外有深邃的护城河，孔府、孔庙居于城中，奠定了今日曲阜的城市格局。这是中国历史上极为罕见的为一处祭祀建筑群而迁建整座城市的案例，深刻体现了礼制建筑在古代城市中的核心地位。明故城平面近似方形，设城门五座，各门均建城楼与瓮城。

城市结构以孔庙、孔府为双核心：孔庙居城中偏东，孔府紧邻其东；城内的街道、水系、庙宇、衙署均围绕这一核心展开。这种以礼制建筑为城市原点的布局，与以官署或市肆为中心的一般府县城形成鲜明对比。孔庙位于曲阜明故城中心，是我国历代封建王朝祭祀孔子的地方。

它占地约14万平方米，南北长0.7公里，共有九进院落，殿阁庑堂祠466间、门坊54座、古树1700余株、碑碣1200余块。

因其典型的东方建筑特色和宏伟规模，与北京故宫、河北承德避暑山庄并称为中国三大古建筑群。1994年，孔庙、孔府、孔林被联合国教科文组织列入世界遗产名录；

2007年，明故城三孔旅游区被批准为国家5A级旅游景区。

近年来的保护工作引入了现代技术：2015年起实施三孔古建筑彩绘保护工程，2021年启动孔庙及孔府文物建筑预防性保护试点项目，使这处世界遗产由抢救性修复转向预防性保护。

— ENGLISH —

Qufu was the capital of the state of Lu and the home of Confucius; its urban history spans three millennia. The Zhou capital of Lu was vast, with city walls, palace platforms, drainage systems and craft workshops all preserved, making it one of the most important cities of the eastern Zhou world.

The Ming walled city visible today took shape when the city was moved to protect the temple.During the Zhengde reign, because the Confucius Temple and Mansion had repeatedly suffered from war and banditry, the Ming court ordered the city to be relocated so as to shelter the temple.

The works took ten years and produced a brick-walled city some 4.8 km in circumference, surrounded by a deep moat, with the Confucius Mansion and Temple at its heart - establishing the urban pattern of Qufu that survives today.

This is an extremely rare case in Chinese history of an entire city relocated for the sake of a single ritual complex, powerfully illustrating the centrality of ritual architecture in the traditional Chinese city.The Ming city is roughly square in plan with five gates, each carrying a gate tower and barbican.

Its structure has a twin core: the Confucius Temple slightly east of centre with the Mansion immediately to its east; streets, watercourses, temples and government offices all unfold around this core.

Such a layout, with a ritual complex as the urban origin point, contrasts sharply with ordinary prefectural and county cities centred on government offices or markets.The Confucius Temple, occupying the centre of the Ming city, was where successive dynasties sacrificed to Confucius.

It covers some 140,000 square metres and extends 0.7 km from north to south, with nine successive courtyards, 466 halls, chambers and side rooms, 54 gate-archways, over 1,700 ancient trees and more than 1,200 stelae.

For its typical East Asian architectural character and immense scale it is ranked with the Beijing imperial palace and the Chengde Mountain Resort as one of China's three greatest ancient building complexes.In 1994 the temple, mansion and cemetery were inscribed on the UNESCO World Heritage List, and in 2007 the Ming city Three Confucian Sites scenic area was approved as a national 5A tourist attraction.

Conservation has recently adopted modern approaches: a polychrome painting conservation project began in 2015, and in 2021 a preventive conservation pilot for the temple and mansion buildings was launched, shifting this World Heritage site from rescue repair to preventive care.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 明代移城卫庙：为保护孔庙而迁建整城，历时十年
  • 明故城周长约4.8公里，设五座城门
  • 以孔庙孔府为城市双核心的礼制城市布局
  • 孔庙占地约14万平方米、九进院落、466间殿庑
  • 与北京故宫、承德避暑山庄并称中国三大古建筑群
  • 1994年列入世界遗产名录
  • 2015年三孔古建筑彩绘保护工程
  • 2021年孔庙孔府文物建筑预防性保护试点

11. 聊城东昌古城（水上古城）

Dongchang Ancient City, Liaocheng (the Water City)
宋至明清 府城与运河商城 国家历史文化名城 聊城市东昌府区古城中央，东昌湖环绕

聊城古称东昌，是京杭大运河沿线的重要商城。今日所见的东昌古城平面近似方形，被面积广阔的东昌湖环抱，形成城在水中、水在城中的独特格局，故又称水上古城或凤凰城。现存城池格局可追溯至宋代，明洪武五年东昌卫守御指挥佥事陈镛将土城改筑为砖城，城周设四门，各门建城楼与瓮城，四角设角楼，城墙外为护城河，水面宽阔，构成城防与水利双重体系。

古城中央即明代建造的光岳楼，四条大街自楼下的十字拱门向四方延伸，形成以楼为心的方正路网，是中国古代城市规划中以高大体量建筑统率全城格局的典型实例。聊城因运河而兴。明清时期，东关运河沿岸商贾云集，先后建有山陕、江西、苏州、赣江、武林等八大会馆，其中山陕会馆是唯一保存至今者。

城内有县署、七贤祠、绮园、地委专署旧址等建筑群，反映出运河商贸对城市空间结构的深刻塑造：商业沿运河外溢，行政与礼制居中，居住则填充其间。这种由一条运河重新组织城市功能的模式，在临清、济宁、台儿庄等山东运河城市中被反复复制，构成山东城镇体系中极具辨识度的一支。东昌湖是聊城城市景观的核心。湖面由护城河扩展而成，是中国北方少见的大型城内湖泊。

湖与城的关系并非单纯的景观营造，而是防洪、供水、航运、养殖与军事防御多重功能的叠加，体现了山东平原城市在水利条件约束下的适应性智慧。

— ENGLISH —

Liaocheng, anciently called Dongchang, was an important commercial city on the Grand Canal.

The old city visible today is roughly square in plan and encircled by the broad Dongchang Lake, producing the distinctive pattern of a city standing in water and water lying within the city; it is therefore also called the Water City or the Phoenix City.The layout of the existing city can be traced to the Song dynasty.

In the fifth year of the Hongwu reign, Chen Yong, commander of the Dongchang guard, rebuilt the earthen city in brick, with four gates each carrying a tower and barbican, corner towers at the four angles, and a wide moat outside - a dual system of defence and water management.At the exact centre stands the Guangyue Tower, built in the Ming; four main streets radiate from the crossed archways under it, forming a square road network governed by a single dominant structure - a classic example of the Chinese city plan in which one massive building orders the whole.Liaocheng prospered because of the canal.

In Ming and Qing times merchants gathered along the canal at the east suburb and built eight guild halls, including those of Shanxi-Shaanxi, Jiangxi, Suzhou, Ganjiang and Wulin, of which only the Shanshan Guild Hall survives.

Within the city are the county government office, the Seven Worthies Shrine, Qiyuan Garden and the former prefectural party committee offices, reflecting the way canal commerce reshaped urban space: commerce spilled outward along the canal, administration and ritual occupied the centre, and housing filled the intervals.

This model of a city reorganised by a canal was repeated at Linqing, Jining and Taierzhuang, forming a highly recognisable strand in Shandong's urban system.Dongchang Lake is the core of the city's landscape. Formed by expanding the moat, it is a rare large intra-urban lake in north China.

The relationship between lake and city is not merely scenic: flood control, water supply, navigation, fish farming and military defence all overlap, demonstrating the adaptive wisdom of Shandong plain cities under hydraulic constraints.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 城在水中、水在城中的水上古城格局
  • 明洪武五年陈镛改土城为砖城
  • 光岳楼居城中央，四条大街由十字拱门放射
  • 明清运河沿岸八大会馆，山陕会馆为唯一幸存
  • 运河重塑城市功能：商业外溢、行政居中
  • 东昌湖：防洪·供水·航运·养殖·防御多功能叠加
  • 国家历史文化名城
  • 临清、济宁、台儿庄共享同一运河城市模式

12. 蓬莱水城

Penglai Water Castle
宋至明清 古代军港与海防城 全国重点文物保护单位（1982年第二批） 烟台市蓬莱区丹崖山下，蓬莱阁景区内

蓬莱水城位于蓬莱阁下，沿丹崖绝壁向南筑起，是我国现存古代海军基地之一，又名备倭城，原为宋代边防水寨刀鱼寨旧址。明洪武九年（1376年），依山势构筑城墙，引海水入内，以停泊船舰、操练水师。

1982年，蓬莱水城及蓬莱阁被国务院公布为第二批全国重点文物保护单位。水城周长约1.8公里，面积约25万平方米，整个城池只有南北两门。

北门叫水门，又称关门口，门上建有栅闸，以控制船只出入，是水师战船进出港湾的通道；南门为振阳门，与陆地相连，供车马行人出入。城内港湾俗称小海，为人工开凿的椭圆形水域，是停泊战船与操练水师的场所。港湾东北、西北两侧各有一段码头，可供船只停靠装卸；港湾周围设有灯楼、炮台与库房。水城的防御体系由城墙、水门栅闸、炮台与港湾共同构成：平时战船入港避风补给，战时水门下栅，港内水师可依托城防坚守，形成可以自守、可以出击的海防要塞。

水城的建造技术体现了对山海地形的精妙利用。城墙沿丹崖山绝壁与海岸线走势修筑，北临大海，东、南、西三面依山就势，墙体随地形起伏，既节省工料，又使城防与山崖连为一体，难以攀越。引海水入城的做法，则利用潮汐涨落维持港内水深，是中国古代军港工程中的杰作。

蓬莱水城与丹崖山上的蓬莱阁建筑群共同构成了山、海、城、阁一体的完整景观，既是明清海防体系的实物见证，也是胶东海防型聚落最具代表性的范例。

— ENGLISH —

Penglai Water Castle lies below Penglai Pavilion, built southwards along the cliffs of Dan-yai Mountain.

It is one of China's surviving ancient naval bases, also known as the Anti-Japanese-Pirate Castle, on the site of the Song dynasty frontier water fortress called Daoyu Zhai.

In the ninth year of the Hongwu reign (1376) walls were raised following the contours of the hill and seawater was let in to berth warships and train naval forces.

In 1982 the water castle together with Penglai Pavilion was listed in the second batch of National Key Cultural Heritage Sites.The castle is about 1.8 km in circumference and some 250,000 square metres in area, with only two gates.

The north gate, called the water gate, carried a grille that controlled the passage of vessels and served as the channel by which warships entered and left the harbour.

The south gate, Zhenyang Gate, connected to the land and served carts, horses and pedestrians.The harbour inside, known locally as the Small Sea, is an artificially excavated oval basin for mooring warships and naval drill.

Quays on the north-east and north-west sides allowed vessels to berth and load, while beacon towers, gun platforms and storehouses were arranged around the basin.The defensive system combined walls, the grilled water gate, gun platforms and the harbour itself: in peacetime ships sheltered and resupplied in the basin; in wartime the grille was lowered and the fleet could hold out behind the defences - a coastal fortress able both to defend and to strike out.Construction technique shows a masterly use of mountain and sea topography.

The walls follow the cliffs of Dan-yai and the coastline; the north faces the open sea while the east, south and west sides follow the slope, the wall rising and falling with the terrain - saving labour and material while fusing the defences with the cliff so as to be almost unclimbable.

Letting seawater into the castle used the rise and fall of the tide to maintain depth in the harbour, a masterpiece of ancient Chinese naval engineering.Together with the Penglai Pavilion complex on Dan-yai Mountain, the water castle forms an integrated landscape of mountain, sea, city and pavilion - both material witness to the Ming-Qing coastal defence system and the most representative example of a Jiaodong coastal-defence settlement.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 我国现存古代海军基地之一，又名备倭城
  • 明洪武九年（1376年）依山筑城、引海水入内
  • 周长约1.8公里，面积约25万平方米，仅南北两门
  • 北水门设栅闸控船，南振阳门通陆路
  • 小海：人工开凿的椭圆形军港
  • 城墙沿绝壁与海岸走势修筑，与山崖连为一体
  • 利用潮汐涨落维持港内水深
  • 1982年与蓬莱阁同列第二批全国重点文物保护单位

13. 即墨雄崖所村

Xiongyasuo Village, Jimo
明代 海防卫所聚落 中国传统村落 / 中国历史文化名村 青岛市即墨区田横镇

雄崖所村位于青岛市即墨区田横镇，是明代海防卫所雄崖守御千户所的所在地，也是山东现存最完整的卫所之一，兼具军事防御与胶东渔村的双重性格。明初为防御倭寇，在山东沿海广建卫所。洪武年间设鳌山卫，下辖雄崖守御千户所与浮山守御千户所。雄崖所因所城东侧临海处有一处赭褐色巨崖，巍峨雄健，故名雄崖。所城平面近方形，城墙以块石垒砌，外设城壕；

设四门，各门建城楼，城门内侧设瓮城。城内街道呈十字或井字形布局，便于兵力快速调度；城的中央偏北设所署衙门，为千户所的指挥机构；城隍庙、关帝庙等礼制建筑分布于城内主要街道两侧，构成军政合一的聚落结构。这种平面规整、功能齐备、防御严密的卫所城，与一般自然生长的传统村落形成鲜明对比，代表了中国古代由国家主导、按军事规制一次性规划建成的聚落类型。

随着清代海防体系的调整与卫所制度的消亡，雄崖所逐渐由军城转为渔村。此后数百年间，居民在所城内部加建民居，形成所城外壳与渔村内核叠合的独特形态：城墙、城门、衙署遗迹与胶东海草房、石砌民居共处一地，军事规整性与生活自发性并存。这种叠合，恰恰是山东沿海聚落六百年变迁最直观的切片。

今日雄崖所村已列入中国传统村落名录与中国历史文化名村，所城的城墙、城门、衙署与城隍庙遗存，是研究明代海防制度与胶东聚落形态演变的珍贵实物资料。

— ENGLISH —

Xiongyasuo village in Tianheng town, Jimo district, Qingdao, is the site of the Ming coastal defence unit known as the Xiongya Defence Thousand-Household Battalion.

It is one of the most completely surviving guard stations in Shandong and combines a military character with that of a Jiaodong fishing village.In the early Ming, to guard against Japanese pirates, guard stations were built extensively along the Shandong coast.

The Aoshan Guard was established in the Hongwu reign with the Xiongya and Fushan defence battalions under it.

The place takes its name from a towering russet cliff above the sea east of the fort.The fort is nearly square in plan, its walls dry-laid in stone blocks with an outer moat, four gates each with a tower and a barbican behind.

Streets inside follow a cross or well-field pattern allowing rapid troop movement; the battalion headquarters sits slightly north of centre, while the City God temple and Guandi temple line the main streets, producing a settlement structure fusing military and civil administration.Such a regularly planned, fully equipped and tightly defended fort contrasts sharply with organically grown villages, and represents the Chinese type of state-led settlement planned and built in one operation to a military specification.With the adjustment of the Qing coastal defence system and the disappearance of the guard system, Xiongyasuo gradually turned from a military town into a fishing village.

Over the following centuries residents inserted dwellings inside the fort, creating a distinctive composite: a fort shell with a fishing-village core, where walls, gates and the headquarters ruins coexist with Jiaodong seaweed-roofed and stone houses, military regularity blended with vernacular spontaneity.

This layering is the most direct cross-section of six centuries of change in Shandong's coastal settlements.Xiongyasuo is today listed as a China Traditional Village and a China Historical and Cultural Village; its walls, gates, headquarters site and City God temple are precious material evidence for the study of the Ming coastal defence system and the evolution of Jiaodong settlement form.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 明代鳌山卫下辖雄崖守御千户所
  • 所城近方形，块石城墙、外设城壕、四门带瓮城
  • 十字或井字形街道便于兵力调度
  • 所署衙门与城隍庙、关帝庙构成军政合一结构
  • 国家主导、按军事规制一次性规划建成的聚落类型
  • 由军城转为渔村：所城外壳与渔村内核叠合
  • 城墙衙署遗迹与胶东海草房共处一地
  • 中国传统村落、中国历史文化名村

14. 青石关村与齐长城关隘聚落

Qingshiguan Village and the Pass Settlements of the Great Wall of Qi
春秋至明清 关隘型村落 中国传统村落 济南市莱芜区和庄镇

青石关村位于济南市莱芜区和庄镇，是齐长城规模最大的关隘所在地，素有齐鲁第一关之盛名，历来为兵家必争之地。村庄东北部即是齐长城青石关，该关形似城堡，长宽各百米，有南、北、西三座城门楼。西门通往今博山区的樵岭前村；南门上方曾经镶嵌着青石关石匾，现保存在关门东侧的民屋墙上；

北门顶原建有重檐的玄帝阁，现仅存阁基，阁基下即是北门洞。青石关之所以被称为齐鲁第一关，不仅因其规模，更因其地形：此处两山夹峙，关口狭窄，素有一夫当关万夫莫开之势，是齐国腹地通往鲁地、南下江淮的必经孔道。关隘的功能决定了聚落的形态。青石关村沿古道两侧展开，呈带状布局，沿街多为供往来客商歇脚的车马店、客栈与商铺，形成典型的驿站商贸型村落。

村内民居就地取材，以青石垒砌墙体、石板覆顶，坚固厚重，与关隘的军事气质一脉相承。青石关村最著名的实物遗存，是关门内外坚硬岩石上被南来北往车辆碾出的三道深深车辙。当地人称这种岩石为青石，硬度极大，然而千百年的车轮竟在石面上刻下深槽，成为这条古道上商旅往来密度最直观的度量，也是山东古代交通史最动人的实物证据之一。与青石关同类的关隘型、驿站型聚落，在山东还有章丘锦阳关、莱芜风门道关等。

它们沿古官道与商贸要道呈串珠式点状分布，与沿黄河、运河流域分布的临水聚落带，以及山地丘陵中的传统村落集群，共同构成山东聚落分布的三大空间脉络。

— ENGLISH —

Qingshiguan village in Hezhuang town, Laiwu district, Jinan, is the site of the largest pass on the Great Wall of Qi, long famed as the first pass of Qi-Lu and a place of permanent strategic contest.

To the north-east stands the Qingshi Pass, castle-like in form, some 100 metres each way, with gate towers on the south, north and west.

The west gate leads to Qiaolingqian village in present-day Boshan; above the south gate was once set a stone plaque reading Qingshi Pass, now preserved on the wall of a dwelling beside the gate; the north gate was crowned by a double-eave Xuandi Pavilion of which only the platform survives, the gate tunnel passing beneath it.The pass earned its title not only through scale but through terrain: two mountains press in on either side and the opening is narrow, so that one man holding the pass could hold off ten thousand - the unavoidable route from the Qi heartland into Lu and south towards the Huai and Yangtze.The function of the pass determined the form of the settlement.

Qingshiguan extends along both sides of the old road in a linear plan, lined with inns, caravanserais and shops serving passing merchants - a classic post-and-trade village.

Houses are built of local stone with slab roofs, solid and heavy, in keeping with the military character of the pass.The most celebrated physical remains at Qingshiguan are the three deep wheel ruts ground into the hard rock inside and outside the gate by centuries of passing carts.

The locals call this rock blue stone, and it is extremely hard; yet the wheels of a thousand years cut deep grooves into its surface - the most direct measure of traffic density on this ancient road and one of the most moving pieces of material evidence for the transport history of Shandong.Comparable pass and post settlements include Jinyang Pass in Zhangqiu and Fengmendao Pass in Laiwu.

Distributed like beads along old official and trade routes, they join the waterfront settlement belts of the Yellow River and Grand Canal and the village clusters of the hills to form the three great spatial threads of settlement distribution in Shandong.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 齐长城规模最大的关隘，齐鲁第一关
  • 关口长宽各百米，设南、北、西三座城门楼
  • 两山夹峙、一夫当关万夫莫开的险要地形
  • 沿古道带状展开的驿站商贸型村落
  • 青石上车辙深槽：古代交通密度的实物度量
  • 就地取材的青石墙体与石板覆顶民居
  • 同类关隘：章丘锦阳关、莱芜风门道关
  • 沿古官道串珠式点状分布的聚落脉络

15. 微山县南阳古镇

Nanyang Ancient Town, Weishan County
明清 运河岛屿型古镇 中国传统村落（第六批） 济宁市微山县南阳镇，南阳湖中

南阳古镇位于济宁市微山县南阳镇，坐落在南阳湖中的一座狭长岛屿上，是京杭大运河沿线极具特色的岛屿型古镇。古镇因运河而兴。元明清三代，京杭大运河穿镇而过，南阳成为南北漕运的重要码头与商埠，明清时期与夏镇、扬州、镇江并称运河四大名镇。南阳古镇的空间形态完全由运河与湖水共同塑造。

镇子沿运河堤岸呈带状展开，主街与运河平行，长约2公里，两侧为密集的商铺、民居与会馆建筑；垂直于主街的巷弄向下延伸至水边，形成家家临水、户户通舟的水乡格局。由于四面环水、用地紧张，镇内建筑密度极高，院落进深浅、面阔窄，屋顶多为硬山两坡，出檐深远以遮雨防晒，呈现出与山东内陆民居显著不同的水乡性格。

镇内保留了大量明清与民国建筑，包括清真寺、河神庙、钱庄、商铺与会馆旧址，反映出运河商贸带来的多元信仰与多元人群。建筑结构上，临水建筑多以砖木混合，临街为两层，下层开店、上层住人，与青岛里院、江南沿河商住建筑有着功能上的共通性。

南阳古镇的居民长期以渔业与航运为生，与微山湖上的连家船渔民共同构成湖区特有的水居社会。近年随着南水北调东线工程的实施与运河航运的恢复，南阳古镇被纳入大运河文化带保护体系，2023年入选第六批中国传统村落名录。

作为山东唯一的岛屿型运河古镇，南阳填补了山东聚落类型中临水岛居这一关键环节，与鲁中山地石寨、胶东滨海渔村、鲁西平原合院共同构成完整的齐鲁聚落谱系。

— ENGLISH —

Nanyang ancient town lies in Nanyang township, Weishan county, Jining, on a long narrow island in Nanyang Lake - a distinctive island-type town on the Grand Canal. It rose with the canal.

Under the Yuan, Ming and Qing the Grand Canal ran straight through it, making Nanyang an important port and commercial centre on the north-south grain route; in Ming-Qing times it was counted with Xiazhen, Yangzhou and Zhenjiang among the four great canal towns.Its spatial form was shaped entirely by canal and lake.

The town extends linearly along the canal embankment, its main street parallel to the water for some 2 km, densely lined with shops, dwellings and guild halls; lanes running perpendicular to it descend to the water's edge, producing the water-town pattern of every house facing water and every household owning a boat.Surrounded by water and short of land, the town is extremely dense: courtyards are shallow and narrow, roofs are mostly flush-gable with deep overhangs for rain and sun, giving it a water-town character markedly different from inland Shandong dwellings.Many Ming, Qing and Republican buildings survive, including a mosque, a river god temple, money shops, commercial premises and guild hall sites, reflecting the plural faiths and plural populations brought by canal commerce.

Structurally, waterfront buildings are mostly brick-and-timber, two storeys to the street, with shops below and dwellings above - functionally akin to the Qingdao Liyuan and to riverside commercial dwellings of the Yangtze delta.Townspeople long lived by fishing and shipping, together with the linked-boat fishermen forming the distinctive aquatic society of the lake region.

With the eastern route of the South-North Water Transfer Project and the revival of canal navigation, Nanyang has been brought into the Grand Canal Cultural Belt conservation system, and in 2023 it was inscribed in the sixth batch of China Traditional Villages.As Shandong's only island-type canal town, Nanyang fills the island-dwelling niche in the province's settlement typology and completes, together with the central Shandong stone hamlets, the Jiaodong coastal fishing villages and the western Shandong plain courtyards, the full Qilu settlement spectrum.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 京杭大运河穿镇而过的岛屿型古镇
  • 明清运河四大名镇之一
  • 主街与运河平行，长约2公里
  • 家家临水、户户通舟的水乡格局
  • 用地紧张：院落进深浅、面阔窄、建筑密度极高
  • 临街两层，下层开店、上层住人
  • 清真寺、河神庙、钱庄、会馆反映多元信仰与人群
  • 2023年入选第六批中国传统村落

16. 阳谷七级镇

Qiji Town, Yanggu County
明清 运河码头型古镇 中国传统村落（第六批） 聊城市阳谷县七级镇七级村

七级镇位于聊城市阳谷县，是京杭大运河沿岸因码头而兴的商贸集镇，2023年入选第六批中国传统村落名录。镇名七级，相传因运河码头原有七级石阶而得名，形象地记录了聚落起源与水工设施的直接关系。

山东运河沿线聚落的名称多与水利设施相关，如七级、阿城、张秋、魏湾等，这些地名本身就是一部缩微的运河工程史。七级镇的格局是典型的河埠型：运河自镇中穿过，码头、渡口沿岸分布，主街垂直于运河向内延伸，两侧为商铺、粮行、酒坊与民居。由于漕运与商贸的推动，镇内曾建有山西会馆等商业会馆，是客商联络乡谊、议定商规、祭祀神灵的场所。

民居建筑反映出鲁西与运河商贸的双重影响：院落以三合院为主，正房三间、厢房两间，墙体为青砖碱脚加土坯，屋顶为囤坡顶，门窗起圆拱券，窗棂为简洁方格。

临街房屋多为前店后宅或下店上宅，是运河商镇的通用做法。学者对山东段大运河沿线乡村聚落空间演变及其影响因素的研究表明，运河的开通、改道与废弃，是沿线聚落兴衰的决定性变量：运河通航期聚落沿河呈带状快速扩张，形成前店后宅的高密度商业街巷；运河停航后，聚落的发展重心重新转向陆路交通或农业生产，原有的滨水商业街逐渐衰落，留下大量形制完整但功能空置的传统建筑。

七级镇正是这一过程的典型样本，其价值在于完整保留了运河商贸聚落在失去运输功能之后的空间化石状态。

— ENGLISH —

Qiji town in Yanggu county, Liaocheng, is a commercial market town that grew around a quay on the Grand Canal; it was inscribed in the sixth batch of China Traditional Villages in 2023.

The name Qiji, seven steps, is said to derive from the seven stone steps of the old canal quay - vividly recording the direct relationship between the origin of a settlement and a hydraulic structure.

Many settlements along the Shandong canal are named after such works - Qiji, Acheng, Zhangqiu, Weiwang - and these place names are in themselves a miniature history of canal engineering.The layout is that of a classic river-port town: the canal runs through the middle, quays and ferries line the banks, and the main street runs inland perpendicular to the water, lined with shops, grain houses, distilleries and dwellings.

Grain transport and commerce brought merchant guild halls such as the Shanxi Guild Hall, where sojourning traders maintained hometown ties, agreed commercial rules and performed sacrifices.The dwellings show a dual influence of western Shandong tradition and canal commerce: courtyards are mostly three-side, with a three-bay principal room and two-bay wings; walls are adobe on a grey-brick damp-proof course; roofs are flat or gently cambered; openings are arched and windows have simple lattice.

Street-front buildings follow the common canal-town formula of shop in front and dwelling behind, or shop below and dwelling above.Research on the spatial evolution of rural settlements along the Shandong section of the Grand Canal shows that the opening, realignment and abandonment of the canal were the decisive variables in the rise and fall of settlements along it.

During navigation periods settlements expanded rapidly in a linear form along the water, producing high-density commercial streets of shop-front-dwelling-behind.

After navigation ceased, growth shifted back towards road transport or agriculture, the former waterfront commercial streets declined, and many structurally complete but functionally vacant traditional buildings were left behind.

Qiji is a textbook example of this process, its value lying in the way it preserves the spatial fossil of a canal trading settlement that has lost its transport function.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 因运河码头七级石阶得名
  • 京杭大运河穿镇而过的河埠型格局
  • 主街垂直于运河向内延伸，商铺粮行酒坊林立
  • 山西会馆：客商联络乡谊、议定商规的场所
  • 鲁西三合院：青砖碱脚+土坯墙+囤坡顶+圆拱券方格窗
  • 前店后宅、下店上宅的商镇通用做法
  • 运河通航与停航决定沿线聚落兴衰
  • 2023年入选第六批中国传统村落

17. 临清中洲运河古城

Linqing Zhongzhou Canal City
明清 运河商城与钞关 省级历史文化名城 聊城市临清市，运河与卫河交汇处

临清位于京杭大运河与卫河交汇处，因漕运而崛起为明清时期山东最大的商业都会之一。中洲古城即临清运河城区的核心，被运河与卫河环抱，形如洲渚，是运河城市中最典型的洲城形态。临清的兴起始于运河。明永乐年间会通河疏浚通航后，临清成为南北漕运的咽喉，商船云集，百货辐辏，设有临清钞关，是明清两代全国规模最大的运河税收机构之一。钞关的设立使临清不仅是货物集散地，更成为国家财政网络的关键节点。

城市格局上，中洲古城由运河、卫河与城墙共同界定，形成水环城、城抱水的形态。城内街巷沿运河走势展开，形成竹竿巷、箍桶巷、马市街等以手工业与商业命名的专业街巷，反映出高度专业化的城市分工。城内的标志性建筑包括鳌头矶、舍利宝塔、清真寺与大宁寺。鳌头矶建于明代，位于运河与卫河交汇处，是一组临水的楼阁式建筑，取独占鳌头之意，既是漕运航标的观望处，也是临清的城市象征。临清的建筑与工艺享有盛名。

临清砖是明清皇家建筑用砖的重要产地，北京故宫、明十三陵所用城砖多出自临清，所谓临清砖、北京城之说即由此而来。临清的贡砖烧制技艺、临清架鼓、临清时调等非物质文化遗产，共同构成运河商贸城市的文化底色。

2023年，临清市魏湾镇镇中联合村入选第六批中国传统村落名录，使临清的运河聚落体系在村落层面也获得了应有的认定。

— ENGLISH —

Linqing lies at the confluence of the Grand Canal and the Wei River and rose through grain transport to become one of the greatest commercial cities of Ming-Qing Shandong.

The Zhongzhou old city is the core of the canal district, embraced by the canal and the Wei River like an islet - the most typical islet-city form among canal cities.The city's rise began with the canal.

After the Huitong canal was dredged and opened in the Yongle reign, Linqing became the throat of north-south grain transport: ships gathered and goods converged, and the Linqing customs house was established, one of the largest canal tax offices of the Ming and Qing.

Its presence made Linqing not merely an entrepot but a key node in the state fiscal network.In urban form, the Zhongzhou city is defined by canal, Wei River and wall together, creating a pattern of water girdling the city and city holding water.

Streets follow the canal, forming specialised lanes named after trades - Bamboo-Pole Lane, Barrel-Hoop Lane, Horse-Market Street - reflecting a highly developed urban division of labour.

Landmarks include Aotou Ji, the Sarira Pagoda, the mosque and Daning Temple.Aotou Ji, built in the Ming at the confluence of canal and Wei River, is a group of waterfront pavilion-type buildings whose name means seizing the turtle's head - coming first.

It served both as a lookout for grain fleets and as the city's emblem.Linqing's architecture and crafts are famous.

Linqing bricks were a major source of brick for Ming-Qing imperial construction; much of the brick used in the Beijing imperial palace and the Ming tombs came from Linqing, giving rise to the saying Linqing brick, Beijing city.

The craft of tribute-brick firing, together with Linqing frame drums and Linqing folk tunes, forms the cultural ground of a canal commercial city.In 2023 Zhenzhong United Village in Weiwang town, Linqing, was inscribed in the sixth batch of China Traditional Villages, extending due recognition to Linqing's canal settlement system at village level.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 京杭大运河与卫河交汇处的洲城形态
  • 明清全国规模最大的运河税收机构之一临清钞关
  • 竹竿巷、箍桶巷、马市街等专业街巷
  • 鳌头矶：明代临水楼阁，城市象征
  • 临清砖：明清皇家建筑用砖的重要产地
  • 水环城、城抱水的城市格局
  • 贡砖烧制技艺等非物质文化遗产
  • 2023年魏湾镇镇中联合村入选第六批中国传统村落

18. 周村古商城

Zhoucun Ancient Commercial City
明清至民国 陆路商贸城镇 国家历史文化名镇 淄博市周村区大街、丝市街、银子市街一带

周村古商城位于淄博市周村区，是明清至民国时期山东重要的陆路商贸集镇，素有旱码头、金周村、丝绸之乡之称，是中国北方为数不多保存完整的商业城镇标本。与临清、南阳、七级等因运河而兴的城镇不同，周村的兴起主要依托陆路：它地处济南与青州、烟台之间的陆路要冲，是鲁中山区与胶东半岛之间物资交换的枢纽。

明清时期，周村的丝织业与丝绸贸易极为发达，成为山东丝绸的集散中心，大街、丝市街、银子市街三条主街分别承担商业零售、丝绸批发与金融汇兑功能，形成高度专业化的商业街区结构。周村商业建筑的最大特点是前店后坊、下店上宅的商住混合模式，沿街建筑多为两层，下层为铺面，上层为居住或仓储，进深较大，院内往往串联作坊、库房与住宅，形成商、工、居三位一体的院落组织。

建筑立面采用青砖砌筑，设木质槅扇门板，可整体拆卸以敞开铺面；门楣、墀头、影壁处多施砖雕与木雕，题材以福禄寿、吉祥花草为主，既显示财力，又寄托商人对经营平安的祈愿。

周村古商城内保存有千佛寺、魁星阁、三益堂书局旧址与大量老字号商号建筑，其中瑞蚨祥、谦祥益等山东商帮的老字号，在全国范围内具有深远影响。从建筑史看，周村代表了山东聚落类型中与运河商贸、海洋渔耕、山地农耕并列的第四支，即以陆路商道与手工业专业化为基础的商贸城镇。周村周边的李家疃村、万家村等传统村落，则是商帮财富回流乡村、营建大型宅院的直接产物，构成商城与乡居相互支撑的完整体系。

— ENGLISH —

The Zhoucun ancient commercial city in Zhoucun district, Zibo, was an important overland trading town in Shandong from the Ming and Qing into the Republican period, known as the dry port, golden Zhoucun and home of silk, and is one of the few well-preserved specimens of a commercial town in north China.Unlike Linqing, Nanyang and Qiji, which rose through the canal, Zhoucun grew chiefly from overland routes: it stood at the junction of the roads between Jinan, Qingzhou and Yantai, acting as the hub for exchange between the central Shandong mountains and the Jiaodong Peninsula.

In Ming-Qing times its silk weaving and silk trade were highly developed and it became the distribution centre for Shandong silk.

The three main streets - Great Street, Silk Market Street and Silver Market Street - respectively handled retail, silk wholesale and financial exchange, forming a highly specialised commercial district.The defining feature of Zhoucun commercial architecture is the mixed shop-workshop-dwelling model: street-front buildings are generally two storeys, with the shop below and living or storage above, considerable in depth, and the courtyard linking workshops, warehouses and residence - a three-in-one organisation of commerce, manufacture and dwelling.

Facades are of grey brick with timber screen doors that could be entirely removed to open up the shop; brick and wood carving decorate lintels, gable shoulders and screen walls with motifs of fortune, emolument and longevity and auspicious flowers, displaying wealth and expressing merchants' prayers for safe trade.Within the old city survive the Thousand-Buddha Temple, the Kuixing Pavilion, the former premises of the Sanyitang bookshop and many old merchant houses.

Shandong merchant houses such as Ruifuxiang and Qianxiangyi exerted influence nationwide.In architectural terms Zhoucun represents a fourth strand of Shandong settlement, alongside canal commerce, marine fishing and mountain farming - the trading town based on overland routes and specialised handicraft.

Nearby traditional villages such as Lijiatuan and Wanjia, with their large courtyard houses, are direct products of mercantile wealth flowing back into the countryside, forming a complete system in which the commercial town and rural dwelling supported each other.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 旱码头、金周村、丝绸之乡
  • 依托陆路商道而非运河兴起
  • 大街、丝市街、银子市街：零售·丝绸批发·金融汇兑三条专业街
  • 前店后坊、下店上宅的商住混合模式
  • 可整体拆卸的木质槅扇铺面门
  • 砖雕木雕题材：福禄寿与吉祥花草
  • 瑞蚨祥、谦祥益等山东商帮老字号
  • 与李家疃、万家等村落构成商城与乡居互撑体系

19. 台儿庄古城

Taierzhuang Ancient City
明清 运河商贸与军事重镇 国家历史文化名镇 枣庄市台儿庄区，京杭大运河沿岸

台儿庄位于枣庄市最南端、京杭大运河畔，是明清时期运河沿线重要的商贸与军事重镇，有天下第一庄之誉。它同时承载着运河商贸文化与近代抗战记忆的双重价值。台儿庄因河而兴。明万历年间泇河开通后，京杭大运河改道经台儿庄，此地成为山东段运河南端的咽喉，商船昼夜往来，形成商贾迤逦、一河渔火、歌声十里的繁盛景象。

清代康乾时期，台儿庄设有巡检司、守备署与闸官署，兼具税收、军事与水利管理三重职能。从聚落形态看，台儿庄是典型的水街相依格局：运河主道穿城而过，城内另有多条水街与水巷，形成河、街、巷、院逐层递进的空间序列。沿河设码头，临水建筑多为前店后宅，二层设临水挑廊，货运与人居共用一条水岸。台儿庄的建筑风格极具包容性。

由于客商来自全国各地，城内曾建有山西会馆、江西会馆、福建会馆、浙人会馆等八大会馆，以及清真寺、天主教堂、关帝庙、妈祖庙等不同信仰的建筑，形成南北交融、中西合璧、多元信仰并存的独特城市景观，被学者称为运河文化的活态博物馆。1938年的台儿庄战役使古城几乎化为废墟。

2008年起，枣庄市启动台儿庄古城重建工程，依据历史文献、老照片与考古发掘进行复原性重建，恢复了明清与民国时期的街巷格局、会馆建筑与临水民居，是当代中国规模最大的古城复建工程之一，也引发了关于真实性、复原与遗产伦理的持续讨论。

从技术层面看，重建严格遵循原材料、原工艺、原形制的原则，大量使用当地青砖、木材与传统榫卯与砖石工艺，使传统营造技艺在当代得以延续与检验。

— ENGLISH —

Taierzhuang, at the southern tip of Zaozhuang on the Grand Canal, was an important commercial and military town on the canal in Ming-Qing times and is honoured as the foremost town under heaven.

It carries a double value: canal commercial culture and the memory of modern resistance.Taierzhuang rose with the river.

After the Jia River was opened in the Wanli reign, the Grand Canal was realigned through Taierzhuang, making it the throat of the southern end of the Shandong section; ships passed day and night in a scene of continuous merchants, fishing lights along the river and songs for ten li.

In the Kangxi and Qianlong reigns it hosted a patrol office, a garrison office and a sluice office, combining tax, military and water-management functions.In settlement form, Taierzhuang is a classic water-and-street interdependency: the main canal runs through the town and several water streets and lanes branch within, producing a spatial sequence of river, street, lane and courtyard.

Quays line the water and waterfront buildings are typically shop in front and dwelling behind, with overhanging waterfront balconies at first-floor level; freight and daily life share a single bank.Its architecture is remarkably inclusive.

Because merchants came from all over China, the town once contained eight guild halls - Shanxi, Jiangxi, Fujian and Zhejiang among them - alongside a mosque, a Catholic church, a Guandi temple and a Mazu temple, producing a unique urban landscape in which north and south, China and the West, and multiple faiths coexisted.

Scholars have called it a living museum of canal culture.The battle of Taierzhuang in 1938 reduced the old city almost to ruins.

From 2008 Zaozhuang launched a reconstruction project, rebuilding on the basis of historical documents, old photographs and archaeological excavation, restoring the street pattern, guild halls and waterfront dwellings of the Ming-Qing and Republican periods.

It is one of the largest urban reconstruction projects in contemporary China and has provoked continuing debate about authenticity, reconstruction and heritage ethics.Technically the reconstruction strictly followed the principles of original materials, original crafts and original forms, using large quantities of local grey brick and timber with traditional mortise-and-tenon and masonry work, allowing traditional building crafts to be continued and tested in the present.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 天下第一庄，泇河开通后成为运河南端咽喉
  • 清代设巡检司、守备署、闸官署三重职能
  • 河、街、巷、院逐层递进的空间序列
  • 八大会馆与清真寺、天主教堂、妈祖庙并存
  • 南北交融、中西合璧的运河文化活态博物馆
  • 1938年台儿庄战役使古城化为废墟
  • 2008年起依据文献、老照片与考古进行复原性重建
  • 原材料、原工艺、原形制的重建原则

20. 邹城邾国故城

The Ancient Capital of Zhu, Zoucheng
周至汉代 都城遗址 全国重点文物保护单位 济宁市邹城市峄山镇纪王城村一带

邾国故城位于济宁市邹城市峄山镇，是周代邾国的都城所在地。邾国为曹姓诸侯国，与鲁国毗邻，长期在齐鲁两大国之间存续，直至战国晚期为楚所灭。故城遗址平面近似长方形，面积约6平方公里，城墙夯筑，周长约9公里，现存城墙残高多处仍达3至5米，是山东保存状况较好的周代城址之一。

城址的营建充分利用了地形：北倚峄山，南临平原，城墙沿台地边缘修筑，形成易守难攻的态势；城内中部偏北为宫殿区，发现有大型夯土台基；城外分布有手工业作坊区、墓葬区与居住区，功能分区明确。

2017年，山东大学对邾国故城遗址J3等地点进行了系统发掘，出土了大批战国至汉代的建筑构件与生活器具，为研究周代东方城市的建造技术提供了重要材料。

邾国故城的建筑史价值，在于它展示了周代东方诸侯国都城的典型规制：夯土城垣、高台建筑、明确的功能分区，以及依托自然山体构筑防御的选址原则。这些原则在此后两千余年的山东城池营建中被反复沿用，与齐长城的干垒石墙、明清砖城的包砖做法一起，构成山东筑城技术的完整谱系。

邹城同时是孟子故里，境内孟庙、孟府、明鲁王陵等建筑遗存密集，使这一区域成为山东礼制建筑与都城遗址最为集中的文化地理单元之一。

— ENGLISH —

The ancient capital of Zhu lies in Yishan town, Zoucheng, Jining, and was the capital of the Zhou state of Zhu.

Zhu, a Cao-surnamed vassal state adjacent to Lu, survived between the two great states of Qi and Lu until it was destroyed by Chu in the late Warring States period.The site is roughly rectangular in plan, covering some 6 square kilometres, with rammed-earth walls about 9 km in circumference; surviving stretches still stand 3 to 5 metres high, making it one of the better-preserved Zhou city sites in Shandong.Construction made full use of terrain: with Mount Yi to the north and plain to the south, the walls follow the edge of a terrace, easy to defend and hard to attack.

The palace area occupies the north of centre, where large rammed-earth platforms have been found; outside the walls are craft workshop, burial and residential zones, with clear functional zoning.In 2017 Shandong University carried out systematic excavation at loci including J3, recovering large quantities of architectural elements and domestic vessels of the Warring States and Han periods, providing important material for the study of building technique in the eastern Zhou cities.The architectural-historical value of the site lies in the way it displays the typical regulations of an eastern Zhou vassal capital: rammed-earth walls, terrace architecture, clear functional zoning and a siting principle that relied on natural mountains for defence.

These principles were applied again and again in Shandong city building over the following two millennia, and together with the dry-stone walls of the Great Wall of Qi and the brick facing of Ming-Qing cities form a complete genealogy of Shandong fortification technique.Zoucheng is also the home of Mencius, and the density of surviving architecture in the district - the Mencius Temple, the Mencius Mansion and the Ming tomb of the Prince of Lu - makes it one of the most concentrated cultural-geographical units of ritual architecture and capital sites in Shandong.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 周代曹姓邾国都城，战国晚期为楚所灭
  • 城址约6平方公里，夯土城墙周长约9公里
  • 现存城墙残高3至5米，山东保存较好的周代城址
  • 北倚峄山、南临平原，沿台地边缘筑城
  • 城内宫殿区存大型夯土台基，城外分区明确
  • 2017年J3地点系统发掘
  • 周代东方诸侯国都城的典型规制
  • 与孟庙、孟府、明鲁王陵构成密集文化地理单元

21. 孝堂山郭氏墓石祠

The Guo Family Stone Shrine at Xiaotangshan
东汉初年 石祠建筑 全国重点文物保护单位（1961年第一批） 济南市长清区孝里街道孝堂山顶

孝堂山郭氏墓石祠位于济南市长清区孝里街道孝堂山顶，建于东汉初年，历经两千年，石祠至今完好无损，是我国现存最早的地面房屋建筑，1961年国务院公布为第一批全国重点文物保护单位。

石祠全部用青石构件装配而成，面阔两间，进深一间，单檐悬山顶，正面设两根八角形石柱，柱上承斗栱与檐枋，屋顶由数块石板拼合，完整地模仿了当时木构房屋的梁柱、斗栱、屋顶与墙体体系。

这种以石材仿真木构的做法，是研究汉代木构建筑形制最可靠的实物证据，因为汉代木构建筑实物无一存世，石祠与画像石、明器陶楼共同构成认识汉代建筑的三大材料来源。

祠内石壁和石梁上遍布精美的线刻图画，内容包括车马出行、楼阁拜谒、庖厨宴饮、百戏乐舞以及历史故事与神话传说，人物车马刻画生动，楼阁斗栱描绘清晰，具有极高的历史与艺术价值，也是研究汉代山东民居形象与城市生活最直观的图像资料。

祠内还保存有大量汉唐以来的题记与游人刻辞，其中最早的为东汉永建四年（129年）的题记，为石祠的断代提供了确切依据。从建筑史角度看，郭氏墓石祠的意义远超其体量：它证明了至迟在东汉初年，中国木构建筑体系中的柱、枋、斗栱、举折、出檐等要素已经成熟定型，此后两千年的山东乃至中国木构建筑，都是在这一框架内演进与细化。

石祠所在的长清孝里，同时也是齐长城起点所在地与方峪村等石头传统村落的分布区，构成山东建筑史上从汉代石构到明清石砌民居的完整链条。

— ENGLISH —

The Guo family stone shrine at Xiaotangshan, on the summit of Xiaotang hill in Xiaoli subdistrict, Changqing district, Jinan, was built in the early Eastern Han.

After two thousand years it remains intact, the earliest surviving above-ground building in China; it was listed in 1961 in the first batch of National Key Cultural Heritage Sites.The shrine is entirely assembled from blue-stone members: two bays wide and one bay deep, with a single-eave overhanging gable roof, two octagonal stone columns at the front carrying brackets and eave beams, and a roof made up of several stone slabs - a complete imitation in stone of the post, beam, bracket, roof and wall system of contemporary timber construction.This simulation of timber structure in stone is the most reliable material evidence for the forms of Han timber architecture, since no Han timber building survives; stone shrines, pictorial reliefs and pottery tower models together form the three principal sources for understanding Han building.The interior walls and beams are covered with fine incised pictures: processions of chariots and horses, audiences in multistorey halls, kitchens and banquets, acrobatics and dance, together with historical tales and myths.

Figures and vehicles are vivid and the brackets and storeys of the halls are clearly depicted - of the highest historical and artistic value and the most direct visual source for the appearance of Han dwellings and urban life in Shandong.The shrine also preserves numerous inscriptions and visitors' notes from the Han onwards, the earliest dated to the fourth year of the Yongjian era (AD 129), providing secure evidence for its dating.In architectural history, far beyond its small size, the shrine proves that by the early Eastern Han the elements of the Chinese timber system - columns, beams, brackets, roof pitch and eave projection - were already mature and fixed; the timber architecture of Shandong and of China over the following two millennia evolved and refined itself within that framework.Xiaoli in Changqing, where the shrine stands, is also the site of the western starting point of the Great Wall of Qi and of stone traditional villages such as Fangyu, forming a complete chain in Shandong architectural history from Han stone construction to Ming-Qing stone dwellings.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 我国现存最早的地面房屋建筑，东汉初年建成
  • 1961年第一批全国重点文物保护单位
  • 全部青石构件装配，面阔两间、单檐悬山顶
  • 八角形石柱承斗栱檐枋，以石材仿真木构
  • 研究汉代木构建筑形制最可靠的实物证据
  • 祠内壁梁遍布车马出行、楼阁拜谒等线刻图画
  • 东汉永建四年（129年）题记提供确切断代
  • 长清孝里：齐长城起点与方峪石头村同域

第三章 传统村落：山地石寨、海草渔村与平原聚落

Chapter 3 Traditional Villages: Mountain Stone Hamlets, Seaweed-Roof Fishing Villages and Plain Settlements

22. 朱家峪村

Zhu Jiayu Village
明代至今（明洪武四年，1371年） 山村型古村落 中国历史文化名村 / 中国传统村落 济南市章丘区官庄街道，明水城东南5公里，胡山东北脚下

朱家峪位于明水城东南5公里、胡山东北脚下，距省会济南约45公里。原名城角峪，后改为富山峪；朱氏于明洪武四年（1371年）入村，因朱系国姓，又将富山峪改名朱家峪。

据省内外专家考证，古村土台子出土陶器残片和斑鹿角化石，夏商时期即有先民栖息于此，距今3800年以上。

2002年6月章丘市人民政府将朱家峪评定为历史文化名村，2003年11月被山东省建设厅评定为省历史文化名村，2005年被国家建设部和国家文物局授予中国历史文化名村，是山东省唯一入选第二批名录的村落，并被专家誉为齐鲁第一古村、江北聚落标本。

古村为梯形聚落，三面环山，上下盘道，高低参差，错落有致。村南头文峰独秀，庄北首文昌阁楼，笔架山偎依着村西首，东崖下清泉长流，村四周尚有九山不露头。朱家峪有大小古建筑近二百处，大小石桥三十余座，井泉二十余处，庙宇十余处，是中国北方地区典型的山村型古村落，虽历经六百年沧桑巨变，仍完整地保存了原来的古门、古哨、古桥、古道、古祠、古庙、古宅、古校。村内最具代表性的建筑是康熙立交桥。桥分东西两座，相距约十米；

东桥建于康熙九年（1670年），西桥建于康熙二十七年（1688年），距今三百余年。上下行人通车，十分方便。桥身全用青石砌成，虽不用灰泥，历经风雨雪霜，依旧坚固，被专家誉为现代立交桥的雏形。其功能是桥上走人、桥下泄洪，桥下宽敞的泄洪道同整个村落的街道排水系统完美连接。

明代中叶，因山间道路拥挤，富人乘车马进出，旧有窄路不敷使用，全村动员修建了一条可上下交错行驶的双向车马道，即双轨古道，建于明代、复修于清代，长约三百米，双轨即指上行与下行。文昌阁原名危阁连云，建于清道光十八年（1838年），由江南人朱霞设计。

上建阁楼，下筑阁洞，造型古雅宏伟，楼洞一体，全用大青方石筑成，历尽沧桑而坚不可摧。朱氏家祠建于清光绪八年（1882年），民国二十六年（1937年）复修，分里外两院，系细凿大青方石、青砖、木椽、小瓦、翘檐、出厦之结构。

祠门正上方有七星图，被称为文运图腾。此外还有关帝庙（建于明代，清嘉庆十三年即1808年重修）、圣水灵泉（建于清康熙六十年即1721年）、山阴小学与圩门圩墙等。鸦片战争以后盗贼蜂起，村民在周边山上修建了防御性围墙。

朱家峪的房屋多为高台阶、青石根基、山字顶，依山就势而建，为防止雨水与洪水冲刷，显示出北方山区古村落的基本特征。作为电视剧《闯关东》的取景地，朱家峪还承载着山东人三百年闯关东的集体记忆，村内设有闯关东文化展览馆。

— ENGLISH —

Zhu Jiayu lies 5 km south-east of Mingshui town at the north-east foot of Mount Hu, some 45 km from provincial capital Jinan. First named Chengjiaoyu, then Fushanyu, it was renamed Zhu Jiayu when the Zhu family settled there in the fourth year of the Hongwu reign (1371).

Experts have confirmed from pottery sherds and sambar antler fossils unearthed on the village platform that people lived here in the Xia and Shang periods, more than 3,800 years ago.Zhangqiu city designated it a historical and cultural village in June 2002, Shandong province followed in November 2003, and in 2005 the Ministry of Construction and the State Administration of Cultural Heritage named it a China Historical and Cultural Village - the only village in Shandong on the second batch.

Experts hail it as the foremost ancient village of Qi-Lu and a specimen of northern Jiangnan settlement.The old village is a trapezoidal settlement ringed by hills on three sides, with paths climbing and descending, high and low, in pleasing disorder.

It contains nearly 200 old buildings of various sizes, more than 30 stone bridges, over 20 wells and springs and a dozen temples, and has preserved intact its old gates, watch posts, bridges, roads, ancestral halls, temples, dwellings and schools.Its most celebrated structure is the Kangxi flyover: two bridges about ten metres apart, the east built in the ninth year of the Kangxi reign (1670) and the west in the twenty-seventh year (1688), both over three centuries old.

Both are built entirely of blue stone without mortar and remain sound after wind, rain and frost; experts call them the prototype of the modern interchange.

People pass on top while floodwater drains below, and the broad channel beneath connects perfectly with the village street drainage system.In the mid-Ming, as mountain paths became congested and wealthy households travelled by ox-cart and carriage, the whole village was mobilised to build a double-track carriageway with up and down lanes, built in the Ming and rebuilt in the Qing, about 300 metres long.The Wenchang Pavilion, originally named Wei-ge-lian-yun, was built in the eighteenth year of the Daoguang reign (1838) to a design by the southerner Zhu Xia.

A pavilion above and an arched passage below form a single body of large squared blue stone, elegant, grand and enduring.The Zhu ancestral hall was built in the eighth year of the Guangxu reign (1882) and restored in 1937; it has inner and outer courts and is built of finely dressed squared blue stone, grey brick, timber rafters, small tiles, upturned eaves and a projecting porch.

Above the gate is a seven-star diagram honoured as a totem of literary fortune.Also surviving are the Guandi temple (Ming, rebuilt in 1808), the Shengshui Numinous Spring (1721), the Shanyin primary school and the defensive wall and gates erected after the Opium Wars when banditry spread.Houses generally have high steps, blue-stone footings and gabled roofs, built to follow the slope so as to resist rain and flash floods - the basic character of a northern mountain village.

As the location for the television series Chuang Guandong, Zhu Jiayu also carries the collective memory of three centuries of Shandong migration into Manchuria, and houses a Chuang Guandong culture exhibition hall.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 齐鲁第一古村、江北聚落标本
  • 明洪武四年（1371年）朱氏入村，夏商即有人居
  • 梯形聚落，三面环山，上下盘道
  • 康熙立交桥（1670、1688）：现代立交桥的雏形
  • 双轨古道：明代建的上下行双向车马道
  • 文昌阁（1838）：上阁楼下阁洞，全青方石筑成
  • 朱氏家祠（1882）：七星图文运图腾
  • 《闯关东》取景地，村内设闯关东文化展览馆

23. 三德范村

Sandefan Village
明清 山地街巷型古村落 中国传统村落 济南市章丘区文祖街道

三德范村位于济南市章丘区文祖街道，是章丘南部山区规模较大、街巷格局保存最为完整的传统村落之一，已列入中国传统村落名录。村名三德范，取三德为范之意，体现儒家伦理对村落精神秩序的塑造。村落坐落在锦屏山南麓的缓坡地带，整体沿山势呈团块状展开，内部街巷随等高线自由延伸，形成主干道、支巷、宅前小道三级路网结构。

这种非规整的有机路网，与平原地区棋盘式村落形成鲜明对比，是山地聚落适应地形的直接结果。三德范村最具价值的部分是其完整的石砌建筑体系。村内民居、院墙、街巷、台阶、排水沟全部采用当地青石与石灰岩，墙体采用干垒与灰浆砌筑两种做法：重要建筑与临街墙体多用方整料石、勾灰缝；一般院墙与后墙则采用不规则块石干垒或灰浆坐砌，形成粗粝而统一的材料肌理。

屋顶多为硬山两坡，以石板或青瓦覆顶，出檐较浅，山墙高耸，这种高墙小窗的形式既利于冬季保温，也具备一定的防御功能。公共建筑方面，村内保存有古庙、古桥、古井与多处门楼，其中巷道交汇处的过街门楼与券门，既是空间节点，也是历史上夜间闭户防盗的设施。

村内还保留有多处清代至民国的四合院，正房三间、厢房两间，门楼设在东南或正南，门内设影壁，遵循北方民居的一般礼制格局。

从区域视角看，三德范与同属章丘的朱家峪、石子口、石匣、博平、袭家等村，共同构成章丘南部山区传统村落集群，是济南六市中资源最富集、分布最集中的片区之一。

— ENGLISH —

Sandefan village in Wenzu subdistrict, Zhangqiu district, Jinan, is one of the larger and best preserved traditional villages of the southern Zhangqiu hills and has been inscribed on the China Traditional Village list.

Its name, Three Virtues as Model, reflects the shaping of village moral order by Confucian ethics.The village sits on a gentle slope at the southern foot of Mount Jinping, spreading as a clustered mass along the contour.

Its lanes extend freely along contour lines, forming a three-tier network of main routes, branch lanes and door-yard paths.

Such an irregular organic network contrasts sharply with the chessboard plans of plain villages and is the direct result of mountain settlement adapting to terrain.The most valuable aspect of Sandefan is its complete stone building system.

Dwellings, courtyard walls, lanes, steps and drainage channels are all made of local blue stone and limestone.

Walls use both dry-stacking and mortar-laid techniques: important buildings and street-facing walls generally use squared ashlar with struck mortar joints, while ordinary courtyard and rear walls use irregular blocks dry-stacked or bedded in mortar, producing a coarse but unified material texture.Roofs are mostly flush-gable double-pitch, covered with stone slabs or grey tiles, with shallow overhangs and tall gable walls; this high-wall, small-window form aids winter insulation and provides a measure of defence.Public buildings include old temples, bridges, wells and several gate towers.
