The gate towers and archways at lane intersections served both as spatial nodes and as installations for closing the lanes at night against theft.Several courtyard houses of the Qing and Republican periods survive, with three-bay principal rooms and two-bay wings, the gate placed to the south-east or due south and a screen wall inside, following the general ritual layout of northern dwellings.Regionally, Sandefan together with Zhu Jiayu, Shizikou, Shixia, Boping and Xijia forms the traditional village cluster of the southern Zhangqiu hills - one of the richest and most concentrated pockets among the six resource-richest cities of Shandong.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 章丘南部山区规模较大、街巷格局保存最完整的传统村落
  • 团块状沿等高线展开的有机路网
  • 主干道·支巷·宅前小道三级路网
  • 民居、院墙、街巷、台阶、排水沟全用当地青石
  • 整料石勾灰缝与不规则块石干垒并存的砌筑体系
  • 高墙小窗、浅出檐的硬山石屋
  • 过街门楼与券门：空间节点兼夜间防盗设施
  • 与朱家峪、石子口等构成章丘南部山区村落集群

24. 逯家岭村

Lujialing Village
明代（明永乐末年） 悬崖山地石寨 中国传统村落（第六批） 济南市莱芜区茶业口镇，章丘、莱芜、博山三县交界处，海拔800余米

逯家岭村位于济南市莱芜区茶业口镇，居章丘、莱芜、博山三县交界处，海拔800多米，是莱芜市境内海拔最高的村庄，始建于明永乐末年，至今已走过500多年的岁月。

2023年入选第六批中国传统村落名录。村名与地形的关系在当地谚语中被表达得淋漓尽致：冲了泰山顶，冲不了逯家岭。村子依山势而建，最南端的房子几乎就建在悬崖之上，因此被称为悬崖上的村庄。

村内有著名的齐长城遗址风门道关，登高远望，峰峦叠嶂，齐长城蜿蜒盘旋雄踞山巅。逯家岭是一座明代的石头城堡。村头一潭池水、几棵老树、石板路、石头桥，村舍均以石头垒砌，且多为两层，依山势而建，高低错落，曲折蜿蜒，如同走进一座迷宫般的石头城堡。

老宅基本都是石头垒砌而成，不用混凝土，不用石灰浆抹缝，只把大大小小的石头有序地挤在一块，即便是悬在空中的石头也掉不下来，而且一待就是几百年。这种干垒工艺与莱芜和庄的石头屋子干摞技艺同源，依靠层层错缝、上下咬合形成自稳结构，兼具稳固抗震与保温、隔热、防潮的特性。

逯家岭四合院的修建很讲究辈分和长幼排行：父母住正房，三个儿子按照长幼分住东、西、南三个厢房；每座房子的样式差不多，但等级分明，等级的区分就在台阶上——父母住的正房最高，门前有五级台阶；老大门前四级台阶；老二老三分别是三级和两级。

这种以台阶级数表达伦理秩序的做法，是山东民居礼制内涵最直观、最可度量的体现。村内石雕技艺非凡，从村里的一些石雕作品即可看出简朴山野生活中的大美艺术。村里立村时栽了五棵古槐，现仅存一株，即昔日街中那棵称作老三的古槐，粗达三围，虽历400多年风雨，依然枝繁叶茂，是村子古老的见证。

— ENGLISH —

Lujialing village in Chayekou town, Laiwu district, Jinan, sits at the meeting point of Zhangqiu, Laiwu and Boshan at over 800 metres above sea level - the highest village in Laiwu.

Founded in the late Yongle reign of the Ming, it has passed more than five centuries and was inscribed in the sixth batch of China Traditional Villages in 2023.A local saying expresses the relationship between village and terrain perfectly: the top of Mount Tai may be swept away, but never Lujialing.

The village follows the mountain, its southernmost houses standing almost on the cliff edge, hence its name as a village on the precipice.

The Great Wall of Qi runs past nearby at Fengmendao Pass; from a height one sees ridge upon ridge with the wall coiling along the summits.Lujialing is a Ming stone castle.

At the village entrance are a pool, a few old trees, a flagstone path and a stone bridge; the houses are all of stone, many of two storeys, rising and falling with the slope, winding like a labyrinthine stone fortress.The old houses are built of stone without concrete and without mortar pointing: stones of different sizes are simply pressed together in order, and even those apparently hanging in the air do not fall, remaining in place for centuries.

This dry-stacking craft is cognate with the Hezhuang stone-house technique of Laiwu; courses are staggered and interlocked to form a self-stabilising structure that is earthquake resistant, thermally insulating and damp-proof.Courtyard layout carefully reflects generation and seniority: parents occupy the principal room and the three sons occupy the east, west and south wings by age.

The houses look much alike but are strictly graded, and the grading is expressed in the steps - five at the parents' principal room, four at the eldest son's, three and two for the second and third.

This use of step count to express ethical hierarchy is the most direct and measurable expression of the ritual content of Shandong dwellings.The village's stone carving is exceptional, showing great beauty within a plain mountain life.

Five pagoda trees were planted at its founding; one survives, the one called the third elder in the old street, three arm-spans in girth and still luxuriant after more than four hundred years - a witness to the village's age.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 莱芜境内海拔最高的村庄，海拔800余米
  • 明永乐末年建村，500余年历史
  • 冲了泰山顶，冲不了逯家岭
  • 齐长城风门道关遗址近在村侧
  • 不用灰浆的干垒石屋，与和庄干摞技艺同源
  • 两层石屋依山而建，如迷宫般的石头城堡
  • 台阶级数表达长幼伦理：五级、四级、三级、两级
  • 400余年古槐，粗达三围

25. 卧云铺村

Woyunpu Village
明代（明永乐年间） 崖顶石寨聚落 中国传统村落 济南市莱芜区茶业口镇，齐长城风门道关下

卧云铺位于济南市莱芜区茶业口镇，地处莱芜区与淄博市博山区交界处的齐长城风门道关下，因地势高、常年云雾缭绕，故名卧云铺。据史料记载，该村始建于明朝永乐年间，至今保存有完好的明清民居，已列入中国传统村落名录。沿村内一条石板道缓步而上，俨然走进一座石头城堡。

路两边随地势而建的一座座四合院，虽大小不一、构造各异，但都匠心独运。房子全部用石头垒成，且多为上下两层：上层住人，保暖又防潮；下层放东西，方便又节省空间。除了石头铺成的街道、石头盖起的房子，还有石头砌起的墙壁以及石桌、石凳、石碾、石磨、石桥、石栏、石井、石缸、石槽、石臼等等，构成名副其实的石制聚落。这些建筑石料均来自一种被村里人称为十八行子的石头。

明永乐年间，一位名叫王三栗的河北枣强人逃荒来到这里。王三栗本是石匠，他发现这里的山石层次分明、厚薄不一、色彩各异，共有十八行，当地读作xìng，意思是层，俗称十八行子。每层石头的材质都很好，可以根据厚薄加工成盖房用的石料和居家使用的各种器具。于是王三栗在此搭起窝铺住下，开山凿石，建起一座小院，把家人搬来，这座院子被称为王家大院。

明嘉靖年间，淄博龙堂村一位姓刘的年轻人路过王家大院门前讨水喝，王家人热情让座、烧水下茶并留下吃饭，还主动提出提供土地助其安家。年轻人便留了下来，挨着王家建起自己的庭院，即刘家大院。此后又有其他姓氏陆续来此安家，各姓世代繁衍，和睦相处。卧云铺最动人的建筑细节，是街巷胡同拐弯处的拐弯抹角。靠近里边的人家房屋一角，不是九十度直角，而是有意把棱角变成一个平面，高度从地面往上差不多两米左右。

这样大大方便了行人，特别是推车的、抬轿的，不用拐急弯，更不会与墙角碰撞；类似于现在道路拐弯处安装的反光镜，可以扩大视野范围。虽然自家屋内空间小了一些，也不那么规整美观，却为别人带来了方便。村民们说，这叫上不让天，下不让地，中间让和气，是老祖宗留下来的传统。卧云铺各姓人家皆依水而居，村内水系与石板路共同构成完整的雨水组织系统。

— ENGLISH —

Woyunpu lies in Chayekou town, Laiwu district, below the Fengmendao Pass of the Great Wall of Qi on the border between Laiwu and Boshan district, Zibo. High and often wrapped in cloud, it is named the bed of reclining clouds.

Records indicate that it was founded in the Yongle reign of the Ming, and it preserves intact Ming-Qing dwellings; it has been inscribed on the China Traditional Village list.Walking up the flagged path one seems to enter a stone castle.

Courtyard houses rise on either side following the slope, differing in size and construction but all ingeniously made.

All are built of stone, often in two storeys: living quarters above for warmth and dryness, storage below for convenience and economy of space.Besides stone-paved lanes and stone-built houses there are stone walls and stone tables, benches, rollers, mills, bridges, railings, wells, vats, troughs and mortars - a veritable stone settlement.The material comes from a rock the villagers call the eighteen-layer stone.

In the Yongle reign a stonemason named Wang Sanli fled famine from Zaoqiang in Hebei and arrived here. He found the mountain stone clearly bedded, varying in thickness and colour, in eighteen layers - hang pronounced xing locally, meaning layer - and known as the eighteen-layer stone.

Each layer was of good quality and could be worked according to its thickness into building stone or household implements.

Wang put up a shelter, cut stone, built a courtyard and brought his family; it is called the Wang family compound.In the Jiajing reign a young man named Liu from Longtang village in Zibo passed the gate of the Wang compound and asked for water.

The Wangs welcomed him in, boiled water for tea and gave him a meal, and even offered him land to settle. He stayed and built his own courtyard beside theirs, the Liu family compound.

Other surnames followed, and the families multiplied together in harmony.The most touching architectural detail at Woyunpu is the cut corner. Where lanes turn, the corner of the inner house is not a right angle but deliberately flattened from the ground up to about two metres.

This greatly eased passage for pedestrians and especially for those pushing carts or carrying sedan chairs, allowing a gentler turn and avoiding collision - rather like the convex mirror installed at a modern bend, widening the field of view.

The household loses a little interior space and some regularity, but others gain convenience.

Villagers call it yielding neither to heaven above nor earth below, but yielding harmony in between, a tradition handed down from their ancestors.All the families of Woyunpu live beside water, and the village water system together with its flagged lanes forms a complete system of rainwater management.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 齐长城风门道关下的云雾石寨，明永乐年间建村
  • 房子全部用石头垒成，多为上下两层
  • 上层住人保暖防潮，下层储物节省空间
  • 十八行子片岩：层次分明、厚薄不一、色彩各异
  • 王家大院与刘家大院：石匠开山与邻里相助的建村史
  • 拐弯抹角：上不让天、下不让地、中间让和气
  • 石桌石凳石碾石磨石桥石栏石井石缸石槽石臼俱全
  • 村内水系与石板路构成完整雨水组织

26. 方峪村

Fangyu Village
明代至今，约500年历史 齐长城脚下石头古村 中国传统村落（第四批） 济南市长清区孝里街道，大峰山齐长城西南的山峪里

方峪村位于济南市长清区孝里街道，在大峰山齐长城西南的一个山峪里，距今已有500年左右的历史，列入第四批中国传统村落名单。

村名方峪，即方正的山峪，形象地说明了村落的选址特征：三面山体围合，向南敞开，既避冬季寒风，又获得充足日照，是山东山地聚落典型的负阴抱阳格局。这个村至今还比较完整地保留着众多建于100年前的石头房子，还有古庙、古井、古槐、石碾，以及散发着悠悠光泽的青石板路。村中除了鸡鸣狗吠外，几乎没有其他声响，安静得让人一下子回到了百年前。

方峪村的建筑全部采用当地青石，墙体以不规则块石干垒或灰浆坐砌，厚实厚重；屋顶以薄石板层层叠压铺设，出檐极浅，山墙高耸。

院落格局以三合院为主，正房依山就势坐北朝南，厢房分列东西，大门多设在东南角，门内设影壁，院内以石板铺地并设石碾、石磨等生活设施。由于地处山峪，用地紧张，院落规模普遍较小，但布局紧凑完整。

村内的古庙与古井是公共生活的中心。古井为全村饮水来源，庙宇则承载祭祀与议事功能，两者共同构成山地村落中以水源与信仰为双核心的公共空间结构。

方峪村所在的孝里街道，同时是齐长城起点广里村所在地与汉代孝堂山郭氏墓石祠的所在地，这一区域内从春秋战国的长城、汉代的石祠到明清的石头村落，构成山东建筑史上一部连续两千余年的实物编年史，在全国范围内亦属罕见。

— ENGLISH —

Fangyu village lies in Xiaoli subdistrict, Changqing district, Jinan, in a valley south-west of the Great Wall of Qi on Mount Dafeng. It has a history of some 500 years and was inscribed in the fourth batch of China Traditional Villages.

Its name, square valley, describes its siting precisely: enclosed by hills on three sides and opening to the south, sheltered from winter winds yet receiving ample sun - a classic Shandong mountain settlement pattern of embracing the sun with the shade at its back.The village still preserves quite completely many stone houses built a century ago, together with an old temple, an old well, an old pagoda tree, stone rollers and flagstone lanes polished to a soft sheen.

Apart from cocks crowing and dogs barking there is almost no sound; the quiet carries one back a hundred years.All building uses local blue stone: walls are dry-stacked or mortar-bedded in irregular blocks, thick and massive; roofs are laid in overlapping courses of thin stone slabs with very shallow overhangs and tall gable walls.The predominant plan is the three-side courtyard, the principal room facing south against the slope with wings east and west; the gate is usually at the south-east corner with a screen wall inside, and the yard is paved in stone slabs and equipped with rollers and mills.

Because the valley offers little level ground, compounds are generally small but compactly and completely laid out.The old temple and old well are the centres of public life: the well supplied drinking water for the whole village, while the temple hosted sacrifice and deliberation.

Together they form the dual-core public space structure of a mountain settlement, built on water and belief.Xiaoli subdistrict, where Fangyu lies, is also the site of Guangli village, the western starting point of the Great Wall of Qi, and of the Guo family stone shrine at Xiaotangshan from the Han.

Within this single area, the wall of the Spring and Autumn period, the Han stone shrine and the Ming-Qing stone villages compose a physical chronicle of architectural history continuous over more than two millennia - rare anywhere in China.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 齐长城大峰山西南山峪中的石头古村，500年历史
  • 三面围合、向南敞开的负阴抱阳格局
  • 众多建于100年前的石头房子完整保留
  • 不规则块石干垒墙体+薄石板叠压屋顶
  • 三合院为主，大门东南角、门内影壁、石板铺院
  • 古井与古庙：以水源与信仰为双核心的公共空间
  • 同域内有齐长城起点广里与汉代孝堂山石祠
  • 两千余年连续的建筑史实物编年

27. 莱芜茶业口镇石头村群

The Stone Village Cluster of Chayekou Town, Laiwu
明清 山地石砌聚落群 中国传统村落（第六批） 济南市莱芜区茶业口镇：卧铺村、逯家岭村、中法山村、上王庄村、中茶业村、潘家崖村

济南市莱芜区茶业口镇是山东传统村落密度最高的乡镇之一，2023年第六批中国传统村落名录中，该镇卧铺村、逯家岭村、中法山村、上王庄村、中茶业村、潘家崖村一次性集中入选，加上和庄镇的马杓湾村、青石关村，莱芜区成为全省山地石头村落集群最具代表性的区域。

茶业口镇地处莱芜东北部山区，属泰沂山脉余脉，地势险峻，石材资源丰富，是鲁中山区石砌民居体系的核心分布区。这一区域的村落具有高度一致的共性：选址上多位于山腰或岭顶的缓坡地带，背山面谷，既避风防洪，又便于耕作；布局上沿等高线自由展开，街巷随地形蜿蜒，无明确轴线；材料上全部就地取用当地青石、石灰岩与片岩；

结构上以石墙承重，配合木梁檩或石条梁，石板覆顶；形态上多为两层，上层住人、下层储物，院落紧凑。各村的差异则主要体现在选址的高差处理与院落组合方式上：陡坡地段的村落（如逯家岭、卧云铺）采用错层跌落、院落叠院的做法，上下两户的屋顶互为庭院；缓坡地段的村落则采用并列院落、街巷串联的方式。

2021年，莱芜和庄石头屋子干摞技艺被正式列入山东省非物质文化遗产代表性项目名录。

该技艺是济南市莱芜区和庄镇独有的省级非物质文化遗产，流传数百年，明洪武年间当地先民为适应山区地形、抵御风雨侵袭，就地取材摸索出这套干摞营造技法，清代至民国时期技艺愈发成熟，1800至1940年进入鼎盛阶段，全镇石屋超千座，形成极具特色的石头村落景观。

其核心工艺全程不使用水泥、石灰、泥土等任何黏合剂，依托当地质地坚硬、耐风化的青石与麻石，匠人凭借精湛眼力与丰富经验，遵循上石压下缝、大石撑四角、小石填缝隙的法则，采用层层错缝、上下咬合的方式垒砌墙体，再搭配石条搭梁、石板覆顶或木梁铺草覆瓦的石木结合结构，打造出的石屋稳固抗震，兼具保温、隔热、防潮特性，完美适配山区气候。

如今这项技艺被视为保留明清北方山区石构建筑智慧的建筑活化石，只是在现代建筑冲击下，传统匠人仅剩不足十人，技艺濒临失传。

— ENGLISH —

Chayekou town in Laiwu district, Jinan, has one of the highest densities of traditional villages in Shandong.

In the sixth batch of China Traditional Villages announced in 2023, Wopu, Lujialing, Zhongfashan, Shangwangzhuang, Zhongchaye and Panjiaya were all inscribed at once; together with Mashaowan and Qingshiguan in Hezhuang town, Laiwu district has become the most representative area of mountain stone village clusters in the province.Chayekou lies in the mountainous north-east of Laiwu, on a spur of the Tai-Yi range, with steep terrain and abundant stone - the core distribution area of the central Shandong stone dwelling system.The villages share strong common features.

In siting they occupy gentle slopes on hillsides or ridge tops, backed by mountain and facing a valley, sheltered from wind and safe from flood yet convenient for farming. In layout they spread freely along contours with lanes winding according to terrain and no dominant axis.

In material they use only local blue stone, limestone and schist. Structurally the stone walls are load-bearing, combined with timber beams and purlins or stone lintels, and roofs are of stone slabs.

In form they are often two-storeyed, living above and storage below, with compact courtyards.Differences between villages lie mainly in how they handle slope and combine courtyards.

Villages on steep ground such as Lujialing and Woyunpu use split levels and stacked courtyards, the roof of one household serving as the yard of the one above; villages on gentler slopes place courtyards side by side linked by lanes.In 2021 the dry-stacking craft of stone houses at Hezhuang, Laiwu, was formally inscribed on the Shandong provincial intangible cultural heritage list.

Unique to Hezhuang town, the craft has circulated for centuries: in the Hongwu reign local settlers, adapting to mountain terrain and sheltering from wind and rain, worked out this technique from local materials; it matured in the Qing and Republican periods, reaching its height between 1800 and 1940 when the township had over a thousand stone houses and a highly distinctive stone village landscape.Its core process uses no cement, lime or earth whatsoever.

Working with hard, weather-resistant local blue stone and granite, and relying on trained eyes and long experience, craftsmen follow the rule that upper stones press the joints below, large stones brace the four corners and small stones fill the gaps, laying walls in staggered, interlocking courses.

Stone-slab beams and roofs, or timber beams with thatch or tiles, complete a stone-and-timber structure that is stable and earthquake resistant as well as thermally insulating and damp-proof, perfectly suited to the mountain climate.Today the craft is regarded as a living fossil preserving the wisdom of Ming-Qing stone construction in the northern mountains.

Under the impact of modern building, however, fewer than ten traditional craftsmen remain and the skill is on the verge of being lost.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 第六批名录中茶业口镇六村一次性集中入选
  • 鲁中山区石砌民居体系的核心分布区
  • 选址背山面谷、布局沿等高线、材料全用当地石
  • 陡坡错层跌落、缓坡并列串联的两种院落组合
  • 2021年和庄石头屋子干摞技艺入选省级非遗
  • 全程不使用任何黏合剂的干摞工艺
  • 上石压下缝、大石撑四角、小石填缝隙
  • 传统匠人仅剩不足十人，技艺濒临失传

28. 博山石寨村落群

The Stone Fortress Villages of Boshan
明清 山地石砌聚落群 中国传统村落 淄博市博山区域城镇：黄连峪村、蝴蝶峪村、龙堂村、东流泉村、上恶石坞村、西流泉村、青龙湾村、镇门峪村

淄博市博山区地处鲁中山区腹地，是山东石砌民居保存最为集中的区域之一。域城镇的黄连峪村、蝴蝶峪村、龙堂村、东流泉村、上恶石坞村、西流泉村、青龙湾村、镇门峪村，以及源泉镇的南崮山北村等，先后列入中国传统村落名录，构成著名的博山石寨村落群。博山石寨的形成，与当地的自然环境与产业历史密切相关。

博山自明清以来即是山东重要的陶瓷与琉璃产地，陶琉产业需要大量燃料与原料，促使人口向山区扩散，形成密集的山地聚落；同时，明末清初社会动荡，山区村落普遍修筑寨墙、寨门与望楼，形成兼具居住与防御功能的石寨。镇门峪村、青龙湾村、西流泉村等村名中的峪、湾、泉，直观记录了聚落与自然地形、水源的关系。这些村落多选址于两山夹峙的沟峪之中，村口以寨门扼守，村内街巷狭窄曲折，房屋沿沟谷两侧层层叠建，远望如悬挂在山间的石堡。

建筑上，博山石屋以当地青石为主要材料，采用干垒或灰浆坐砌，墙体厚重，有的厚达60至80厘米；屋顶以薄石板或青瓦覆盖，出檐浅，坡度平缓；门窗洞口较小，窗棂简洁，有的还保留了传统的漆木窗棂。

博山尖古堆村是远近闻名的八秀山庄，历史上曾出过八个秀才，村内石头房子全部采用本地青石垒砌，不使用水泥，采用干垒错缝工艺，墙体的山石大小不一，随形堆叠，层层错叠，依山就势修建，房屋顺着山坡抬升，屋高于路，路亦高于屋，石缝之间野草藤蔓自然生长，充满沧桑古旧气息。

博山石寨村落群与相邻淄川区的梦泉村、上端士村、柏树村、永泉村、罗圈村、纱帽村、土泉村、鲁子峪村、池板村等，以及沂源县的姚南峪村，共同构成鲁中山区规模最大的传统村落连绵带，是全省传统村落大分散、小集中格局中最典型的集中片区。

— ENGLISH —

Boshan district, Zibo, lies in the heart of the central Shandong mountains and is one of the areas where stone dwellings are most densely preserved.

Huanglianyu, Hudieyu, Longtang, Dongliuquan, Shang'eshihu, Xiliuquan, Qinglongwan and Zhenmenyu in Yucheng town, together with Nangushanbei in Yuanquan town, have all been inscribed on the China Traditional Village list, forming the celebrated Boshan stone fortress village group.Their formation was closely linked to local environment and industrial history.

Since the Ming and Qing, Boshan has been an important centre of ceramic and glass production in Shandong; the potteries and glassworks needed large quantities of fuel and raw material, pushing population into the hills and creating dense mountain settlements.

At the same time, social unrest in the late Ming and early Qing led mountain villages to build fortress walls, gates and watchtowers, producing stone strongholds that were both dwellings and defences.Village names such as Zhenmenyu, Qinglongwan and Xiliuquan - valley, bay and spring - record directly the relationship between settlement, landform and water.

These villages generally occupy ravines between two hills, the entrance controlled by a fortress gate; lanes inside are narrow and winding and houses rise in tiers along both sides of the gully, so that from a distance they look like stone castles hanging on the mountainside.In construction, Boshan stone houses use local blue stone, dry-stacked or mortar-bedded, with heavy walls sometimes 60 to 80 cm thick; roofs are covered in thin slabs or grey tiles with shallow overhangs and gentle pitches; openings are small, with simple lattice, and some retain traditional lacquered timber window grilles.Jiangu Dui village in Boshan, known locally as the Eight Scholars' Villa, produced eight successful candidates in the imperial examinations.

Its stone houses are all built of local blue stone without cement, using dry-stacked staggered courses; the stones vary in size, stacked freely and layer upon layer following the slope.

Houses rise with the hillside, house above path and path above house, with grass and vines growing naturally in the joints, full of an air of age.Together with neighbouring villages in Zichuan - Mengquan, Shangduanshi, Baishu, Yongquan, Luoquan, Shamao, Tuquan, Luziyu and Chiban - and Yaonanyu in Yiyuan county, the Boshan group forms the largest continuous belt of traditional villages in the central Shandong mountains and the most typical concentrated cluster in the province's pattern of broad dispersal with local concentration.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 博山域城镇八村集中入选中国传统村落
  • 明清陶瓷琉璃产业推动山区聚落密集化
  • 两山夹峙的沟峪选址，寨门扼守、街巷狭窄曲折
  • 博山石屋墙体厚达60至80厘米
  • 尖古堆村：八秀山庄，干垒错缝、随形堆叠
  • 屋高于路、路亦高于屋的立体聚落形态
  • 与淄川、沂源村落构成鲁中最大传统村落连绵带
  • 全省大分散、小集中格局的最典型片区

29. 梦泉村

Mengquan Village
明清 淄河山谷石寨 中国传统村落 淄博市淄川区太河镇

梦泉村位于淄博市淄川区太河镇，地处淄河上游的山谷之中，是淄川区传统村落群的代表之一，已列入中国传统村落名录。村名梦泉，源于村中一眼常年不竭的山泉，体现出山地聚落对水源的绝对依赖——在鲁中山区，泉眼往往是村落选址的第一决定因素。

淄川区是淄博市传统村落资源最富集的区，太河镇一镇之内即有梦泉村、上端士村、柏树村、永泉村、罗圈村、纱帽村、土泉村、鲁子峪村、池板村九个中国传统村落，加上昆仑镇的张李村、磁村村、刘瓦村，洪山镇的蒲家庄，寨里镇的南峪村，罗村镇的大窎桥村与西河镇的田庄村，构成全省罕见的镇域级传统村落密集区。

梦泉村的建筑延续了鲁中山区石砌民居的基本范式：墙体以当地青石与石灰岩干垒或灰浆坐砌，屋顶以薄石板或青瓦覆盖，院落以三合院为主，正房依山就势，厢房分列，大门多设东南，门内影壁。

由于地处淄河谷地，村落沿河岸与山坡两个方向展开，形成临河的带状部分与山坡上的团状部分的复合形态。学者以淄川区为例，运用GIS技术对传统村落旅游资源进行过系统分析，指出该区域村落的空间分布与高程、坡度、水系距离与道路可达性四个因子高度相关，其中水系距离的影响最为显著——绝大多数村落分布在距常年有水河道500米以内的范围内。

这一结论为理解鲁中山区村落的选址逻辑提供了定量依据，也对传统村落的保护与旅游开发具有直接指导意义。同时，针对鲁中山区乡村聚落空间演变特征与模式的研究表明，该区域聚落在城镇化进程中普遍呈现人口减少与用地扩张并存的反常现象，即所谓人口减少、用地扩张，这一人地互动的特殊模式对传统村落保护提出了严峻挑战。

— ENGLISH —

Mengquan village in Taihe town, Zichuan district, Zibo, lies in a valley of the upper Zi River and is one of the representative traditional villages of Zichuan; it has been inscribed on the China Traditional Village list.

Its name, Dream Spring, comes from a never-failing spring in the village, expressing the absolute dependence of mountain settlements on water - in the central Shandong hills a spring is often the first determinant of village siting.Zichuan is the district of Zibo richest in traditional villages.

Taihe town alone contains nine China Traditional Villages: Mengquan, Shangduanshi, Baishu, Yongquan, Luoquan, Shamao, Tuquan, Luziyu and Chiban.

Adding Zhangli, Cicum and Liwa in Kunlun town, Pujiazhuang in Hongshan town, Nanyu in Zhaili town, Dadiaoqiao in Luocun town and Tianzhuang in Xihe town produces a township-level concentration of traditional villages rare in the province.Mengquan's buildings continue the basic paradigm of central Shandong stone dwellings: walls of local blue stone and limestone, dry-stacked or mortar-bedded; roofs of thin slabs or grey tiles; three-side courtyards with the principal room set against the slope, wings to either side, the gate usually to the south-east and a screen wall within.

Because it lies in the Zi River valley the village extends in two directions, along the bank and up the slope, producing a composite form that is linear by the river and clustered on the hill.Scholars have used GIS to analyse the tourism resources of traditional villages with Zichuan district as a case study, showing that the spatial distribution of villages correlates strongly with four factors - elevation, slope, distance to water and road accessibility - of which distance to water is the most significant, with the great majority lying within 500 metres of a perennial stream.

This provides a quantitative basis for understanding the siting logic of central Shandong villages and directly informs both conservation and tourism development.Research on the spatial evolution and models of rural settlements in the central Shandong mountains further shows that during urbanisation these settlements have commonly displayed the paradoxical pattern known as population decline and land expansion - people decreasing while built-up area grows - a distinctive mode of human-land interaction that poses a severe challenge to traditional village conservation.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 村名源于常年不竭的山泉，水源决定选址
  • 太河镇一镇有九个中国传统村落
  • 淄川区是淄博传统村落最富集的区
  • 墙体青石干垒，屋顶薄石板或青瓦
  • 沿河带状与山坡团状的复合形态
  • GIS分析：水系距离是村落分布的最显著因子
  • 绝大多数村落距常年有水河道500米以内
  • 人口减少、用地扩张的人地互动挑战

30. 李家疃村

Lijiatuan Village
明清 商帮望族古村 中国传统村落 淄博市周村区王村镇

李家疃村位于淄博市周村区王村镇，是明清时期依托周村商埠而兴的望族聚落，已列入中国传统村落名录。与周村区同批入选的还有王村镇万家村与北郊镇大七村，三村共同构成周村商城财富回流乡村、营建大型宅院的实物见证。李家疃村的历史与周村的丝绸贸易直接相关。

明清时期，周村是山东丝绸的集散中心，李氏家族以经营丝绸、杂货起家，积累了巨额财富，随后在故乡大规模营建宅院，形成多座三进四合院并列的大型建筑群。

村落格局规整，主街东西向贯穿，两侧排列深宅大院，各院落沿轴线依次布置大门、影壁、前院、过厅、内宅与后楼，进深可达三进甚至四进，规模远超一般民居。这种由商业资本支撑、按礼制规制营建的乡居建筑，在山东传统村落中是极为特殊的一支，兼具北方民居的厚重与商宅的考究。

建筑装饰是李家疃村最突出的价值所在。砖雕、木雕、石雕三雕俱全，门楼、影壁、墀头、檐枋、门窗槅扇处均有精美雕饰，题材包括福禄寿三星、吉祥花草、博古器物与戏曲故事，工艺细腻精湛。

村内保留有完整的家族祠堂，是宗族议事、祭祀与惩戒的场所，体现出宗法制度对商业家族内部秩序的维系作用。从区域体系看，李家疃村与周村古商城构成商与居的完整链条：商城是资本的生成地，村落是资本的存储与展示地，两者相距仅十余公里，共同记录了明清山东商品经济对乡村聚落形态的深刻改造。

这一模式与胶东牟氏庄园、鲁北魏氏庄园殊途同归，都是财富由商业或农业积累后向建筑转化的结果，只是李家疃以村落形态呈现，牟氏、魏氏则以庄园形态呈现。

— ENGLISH —

Lijiatuan village in Wangcun town, Zhoucun district, Zibo, is a distinguished-lineage settlement that rose in Ming-Qing times on the strength of the Zhoucun commercial port; it has been inscribed on the China Traditional Village list.

Wanjia village in the same town and Daqi village in Beijiao subdistrict were inscribed in the same batch, the three together bearing material witness to the return flow of Zhoucun mercantile wealth into the countryside in the form of large houses.The history of Lijiatuan is directly tied to the Zhoucun silk trade.

In Ming-Qing times Zhoucun was the distribution centre for Shandong silk; the Li family made its fortune in silk and general merchandise and then built extensively in its home village, producing a large complex of adjacent three-courtyard-deep mansion compounds.The village plan is regular: a main street runs east-west with deep mansion compounds on either side.

Each compound follows an axial sequence of gate, screen wall, front court, passage hall, inner quarters and rear building, reaching three or even four bays in depth - far beyond the scale of ordinary dwellings.Such rural residences, financed by commercial capital yet built to ritual norms, are a very special strand among Shandong's traditional villages, combining the solidity of northern dwellings with the refinement of merchant houses.Decoration is the village's outstanding value.

Brick, wood and stone carving are all present, on gate towers, screen walls, gable shoulders, eave beams and window screens, with motifs of the three stellar gods of fortune, emolument and longevity, auspicious flowers, antique vessels and opera scenes, executed with delicacy and skill.A complete lineage ancestral hall survives - the place where the clan deliberated, sacrificed and disciplined - showing how the patriarchal system maintained order within a commercial family.In regional terms Lijiatuan and the Zhoucun ancient commercial city form a complete chain of commerce and residence: the town generated capital, the village stored and displayed it.

Only some ten kilometres apart, they record together the profound transformation of rural settlement form by the commodity economy of Ming-Qing Shandong.

This pattern converges with the Mou Family Manor in Jiaodong and the Wei Family Manor in northern Shandong: all are the conversion of accumulated wealth into architecture, differing only in that Lijiatuan took village form while the Mou and Wei took manor form.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 依托周村丝绸贸易兴起的商帮望族聚落
  • 与万家村、大七村同批入选中国传统村落
  • 多座三进四合院并列的大型建筑群
  • 轴线序列：大门·影壁·前院·过厅·内宅·后楼
  • 砖雕、木雕、石雕三雕俱全
  • 完整的家族祠堂维系宗族秩序
  • 与周村古商城构成商与居的完整链条
  • 商城生成资本、村落存储展示资本

31. 蒲家庄与蒲松龄故居

Pujiazhuang Village and the Former Residence of Pu Songling
清代 名人故居村落 中国传统村落 / 全国重点文物保护单位 淄博市淄川区洪山镇蒲家庄村

蒲家庄位于淄博市淄川区洪山镇，是清代著名文学家、《聊斋志异》作者蒲松龄的故乡，已列入中国传统村落名录。

蒲松龄（1640-1715年），字留仙，一字剑臣，号柳泉居士，世称聊斋先生，淄川人，一生科举失意，以教书为业，积数十年之功写成《聊斋志异》，成为中国文言短篇小说的高峰，其笔下的狐鬼世界深刻反映了明清之际山东乡村的社会生活与民间信仰。

蒲松龄故居是一处典型的鲁中山区普通农家院落，现存正房三间、东西厢房各两间，院落规模不大，建筑朴素，青砖土坯墙体、麦草或青瓦覆顶，与村内其他民居并无二致。

这种平凡与伟大的强烈反差，正是故居最动人的地方：中国最伟大的志怪小说，诞生于鲁中山村一座最普通的石头院落之中。故居内保存有蒲松龄使用过的桌椅、砚台与手稿复制品，正房内设柳泉先生塑像，厢房陈列其生平与著作版本。院内有著名的柳泉，相传蒲松龄曾在此设茶待客、搜集民间故事，聊斋之名即由此而来。

蒲家庄本身也是一处完整的鲁中山地村落。村内街巷以青石铺砌，民居以当地青石垒砌墙体、石板或青瓦覆顶，院落紧凑，格局与淄川区其他传统村落一致。村外有蒲松龄墓园，园内古柏参天，碑碣林立。

从建筑史角度看，蒲家庄的价值在于它保存了清代鲁中山区一个中等村落的全部物质形态：民居、街巷、水井、庙宇、墓地俱全，未经过度商业化改造，是观察清代山东乡村居住状况最真实的样本之一。

今天，蒲家庄既是文学朝圣地，也是山东传统村落中文人故居类型的重要代表，与曲阜孔府、邹城孟府、临沂王羲之故居等共同构成山东名人故居建筑序列。

— ENGLISH —

Pujiazhuang in Hongshan town, Zichuan district, Zibo, is the home of Pu Songling (1640-1715), the great Qing writer and author of Strange Tales from a Chinese Studio, and has been inscribed on the China Traditional Village list.

Pu, courtesy name Liuxian, also Jianchen, styled himself the Layman of Willow Spring and is known as the Master of the Liaozhai Studio.

Failing repeatedly in the examinations, he made his living as a tutor and spent decades composing his collection, which stands as the summit of Chinese classical tales; its world of foxes and ghosts profoundly reflects the social life and popular belief of Shandong villages in the Ming-Qing transition.The former residence is a typical ordinary farm compound of the central Shandong hills: a three-bay principal room and two-bay wings east and west, small in scale and plain in construction, with grey-brick and adobe walls and thatch or grey-tile roofs, no different from the other houses of the village.

This stark contrast between ordinariness and greatness is the most moving thing about it: China's greatest collection of strange tales was born in the most ordinary stone compound of a central Shandong village.Inside are preserved the desk, chair and inkstone used by Pu together with facsimiles of his manuscripts; the principal room holds a statue of the Master of Willow Spring and the wings display his life and editions of his works.

In the yard is the famous Willow Spring, where Pu is said to have served tea to passers-by and collected folk tales - the origin of the name Liaozhai.Pujiazhuang itself is a complete central Shandong mountain village.

Lanes are paved in blue stone; dwellings have local blue-stone walls with slab or tile roofs and compact courtyards, consistent with other traditional villages of Zichuan.

Outside the village lies Pu's tomb garden, with ancient cypresses and numerous stelae.In architectural terms the value of Pujiazhuang is that it preserves the entire material fabric of a middle-ranking Qing village in the central Shandong hills - dwellings, lanes, wells, temples and burial ground all present and unspoiled by over-commercialisation - making it one of the most authentic samples for observing rural living conditions in Qing Shandong.Today Pujiazhuang is both a place of literary pilgrimage and an important representative of the literati-residence type among Shandong traditional villages, forming with the Confucius Mansion at Qufu, the Mencius Mansion at Zoucheng and the Wang Xizhi residence at Linyi a sequence of Shandong celebrity residences.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 清代文学家蒲松龄（1640-1715）的故乡
  • 故居为普通农家院落：正房三间、厢房各两间
  • 青砖土坯墙体、麦草或青瓦覆顶
  • 院内柳泉：设茶待客、搜集民间故事之处
  • 村内街巷青石铺砌，民居石墙石板顶
  • 保存清代鲁中中等村落的全部物质形态
  • 村外蒲松龄墓园，古柏参天、碑碣林立
  • 山东名人故居建筑序列的重要一环

32. 东楮岛村

Dongchudao Village
明万历年间至今，四百余年 海草房渔村 中国历史文化名村（2007）/ 中国传统村落（2012首批） 威海市荣成市宁津街道，三面环海

东楮岛村位于山东省最东端的威海荣成市宁津街道，是一个三面环海的小渔村，建村历史可追溯至明万历年间，至今已有四百余年。

2007年6月被建设部、国家文物局命名为中国历史文化名村，2012年入选首批中国传统村落名录，是中国胶东地区海草房保留最完整的村庄。

东楮岛村现存海草房650间，最早的建于300多年前，被誉为中国生态民居活标本。在地图上观看东楮岛村的地形，像一柄探入海中的长勺，村庄三面被海水包围；

沿着狭长的勺柄进入勺头，即是渔村的中心位置，海草房连片分布，宛如童话中的城堡群落。据《荣成县志》记载：邑中罕见瓦屋，砌以石，覆以茅、苫以海带，仅蔽风雨。村里最古老的海草房建于清顺治时期，历经三百余年风雨依然保存完好，是研究胶东地区渔民生活史和建筑史的重要实物资料。

海草房以三合院为主，坐北朝南的正房，东西两侧的厢房，再加上南墙和门楼；

正房和厢房交错，留出了六面带尖顶的山墙，山墙上写着吉星高照、紫气东来等吉祥字样。墙的石块形状不规则，保留了山石的粗糙质地，颜色以黄白灰为主，细看还有横向的沟壑，那是石匠留下的雕凿痕迹。

海草房的建造需经历七十多道工序，全部是手工工艺，可简单划分为备料、垒墙、苫顶三个大的步骤，其中最关键的就是往屋顶上苫海草，当地人称为苫房。房顶由横向的大梁支撑，还有纵向的檩条多根，俗称腰杆子，纵横交叉为方格，构成屋顶的骨架；

然后再安装高粱秆扎成的笆子，一边涂抹黄泥，一边把笆子按压到腰杆子上；等笆子晾干，才开始苫草——从屋檐开始往上苫，用黄泥将海草一束一束顺次苫上去，苫完一排再压上一排，直到屋脊上那一层，要用白灰和黄泥调成浆抹平，俗称压脊。为防风，有的海草房屋脊压了圆瓦，或用旧渔网罩顶，网角拴了石头坠下来。

苫房所用的海草学名大叶海苔，生长在近海浅水中，在海里生长时是碧绿色，上岸后变作紫褐色，晒干后又变成灰色，房顶上的海草经过多年风吹日晒，又会变作银白。海草含有肥厚的胶质和来自海中的盐卤，不容易生虫，用火机点燃只有暗淡的烟却不起明火。2006年荣成海草房建筑技艺被列入省级非物质文化遗产名录。

近年来村里采取两条腿走路策略：鼓励村民自行修缮海草房，或交由村集体统一保护开发，打造海草房特色民宿；2017年建设海上平台、海洋牧场等旅游设施，使古老建筑焕发新生。

— ENGLISH —

Dongchudao village lies in Ningjin subdistrict, Rongcheng, Weihai, at the easternmost tip of Shandong. A small fishing village surrounded by sea on three sides, its founding dates back to the Wanli reign of the Ming, more than four hundred years ago.

In June 2007 it was named a China Historical and Cultural Village by the Ministry of Construction and the State Administration of Cultural Heritage, and in 2012 it was among the first batch of China Traditional Villages.

It is the most completely preserved seaweed-house village in the Jiaodong region.The village retains 650 seaweed-roofed rooms, the earliest built more than three centuries ago, and is honoured as a living specimen of Chinese ecological dwelling.

On the map the village looks like a long spoon reaching into the sea, surrounded by water on three sides; following the narrow handle to the bowl of the spoon one reaches the centre, where seaweed houses stand in continuous clusters like a fairy-tale castle community.The Rongcheng county gazetteer records: tiled houses were rarely seen in the district; walls were laid in stone and roofs covered with straw and thatched with seaweed, barely enough to keep out wind and rain.

The oldest surviving house in the village was built in the Shunzhi period of the Qing and remains sound after more than three hundred years, an important physical source for the study of fishing life and architecture in Jiaodong.The houses are mainly three-side courtyards: a principal room facing south, wings east and west, plus a south wall and gate tower.

Principal and wing rooms interlock to leave six pointed gable walls, on which auspicious phrases such as May the lucky star shine high and Purple vapour from the east are written.

The stone blocks are irregular, retaining the rough texture of the mountain rock in yellow, white and grey, with horizontal chisel marks left by the masons.Building a seaweed house involves more than seventy processes, all by hand, divided broadly into preparing materials, laying walls and thatching the roof - of which the thatching, known locally as shan fang, is the crucial step.

The roof is carried on transverse main beams and a number of longitudinal purlins, locally called waist poles, crossed into a grid that forms the roof skeleton. Then mats woven from sorghum stalks are laid, pressed onto the purlins while clay is smeared on.

Only when the mats have dried does thatching begin: starting at the eaves and working upward, bunches of seaweed are laid in sequence with yellow clay, row upon row, until the ridge course, which is smoothed with a slurry of lime and clay in the step known as pressing the ridge.

Against wind, ridges are sometimes weighted with round tiles, or the roof is covered with an old fishing net whose corners are tied to stones as weights.The seaweed used is Zostera marina, growing in shallow coastal water.

It is bright green in the sea, turns purple-brown after landing and grey when dried; on the roof, after years of sun and wind, it becomes silvery white. The seaweed contains thick gum and sea salt, so insects do not breed in it, and a lighter held to it produces only dull smoke rather than an open flame.

In 2006 the building craft of Rongcheng seaweed houses was inscribed on the provincial intangible cultural heritage list.In recent years the village has followed a two-track strategy: encouraging residents to repair their own houses, or entrusting them to the collective for unified protection and development, creating distinctive seaweed-house homestays; in 2017 offshore platforms and marine ranches were built, giving new life to the old buildings.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 现存海草房650间，最早建于300多年前
  • 2007年中国历史文化名村，2012年首批中国传统村落
  • 地形如探入海中的长勺，三面环海
  • 三合院为主，六面带尖顶山墙
  • 七十多道全手工工序：备料·垒墙·苫顶
  • 大梁+腰杆子构成方格屋顶骨架，笆子抹泥后苫草
  • 压脊：白灰黄泥浆抹平，或压圆瓦、罩渔网
  • 2006年海草房技艺列入省级非物质文化遗产

33. 烟墩角村

Yandunjiao Village
明清 海草房渔村与大天鹅栖息地 中国传统村落 威海市荣成市俚岛镇

烟墩角村位于威海市荣成市俚岛镇，是胶东半岛保存海草房最集中的村落之一，已列入中国传统村落名录。

村名烟墩角，源于明代海防体系中的烟墩即烽火台，与即墨雄崖所、蓬莱水城同属明清胶东海防聚落网络的组成部分，反映出胶东沿海村落军事性与渔村性叠合的独特历史层次。村内尚保存50多幢有200多年历史的海草房。海草房的外墙用的是石块，采自海岸悬崖；

屋顶用的海草，则是来自大海的馈赠。一座海草房，兼有山与海的特性。海草房沿着海岸线生长，起初是三五成簇，后来连成一片，便有了村庄。

从建筑技术看，烟墩角的海草房与东楮岛同源：石墙承重、木梁檩构架、笆子抹泥、海草苫顶、压脊防风，屋顶坡度约50度以快速排水，石墙与草顶共同形成的保温层使室内冬暖夏凉。

烟墩角村最独特的价值，在于自然与人文的双重叠加：每年11月至次年3月，大量大天鹅从西伯利亚飞抵村前的海湾越冬，百年海草房与成群的大天鹅构成中国北方冬季最具辨识度的乡村景观之一，使这个渔村成为人与自然和谐共处的生动范本。从保护与活化的角度看，烟墩角代表了一种与东楮岛不同的路径：东楮岛以集体统一保护开发、打造特色民宿为主；

烟墩角则以观鸟摄影、写生与渔家生活体验为特色，将村落的传统风貌与其所处的海湾生态系统一并纳入保护与利用范围。学者以荣成市烟墩角村为例，对胶东传统滨海村落景观的保护与开发进行过专门研究，指出滨海传统村落的保护不应局限于建筑本体，而应将村落、海湾、湿地、候鸟栖息地与渔业生产方式视为一个完整的文化景观系统，否则单纯修缮建筑而破坏生态基底，最终仍会导致村落活力的丧失。

这一观点对山东乃至全国的沿海传统村落保护具有重要的方法论意义。

— ENGLISH —

Yandunjiao village in Lidao town, Rongcheng, Weihai, is one of the villages with the densest concentration of surviving seaweed houses on the Jiaodong Peninsula and has been inscribed on the China Traditional Village list.

Its name, beacon-mound corner, derives from the beacon mounds of the Ming coastal defence system; together with Xiongyasuo in Jimo and the Penglai Water Castle it belongs to the Ming-Qing Jiaodong coastal defence settlement network, reflecting the distinctive historical layering of military and fishing character in Jiaodong coastal villages.More than fifty seaweed houses over two hundred years old survive in the village.

The outer walls use stone cut from the coastal cliffs; the roof uses seaweed, a gift from the sea. A seaweed house thus partakes of both mountain and sea.

The houses grow along the coastline, at first in clusters of three or five, later joining into a village.Technically the houses of Yandunjiao share their origin with those of Dongchudao: load-bearing stone walls, timber beam-and-purlin frames, clay-smeared mats, seaweed thatching and a weighted ridge, with a roof pitch of about fifty degrees for rapid drainage; walls and thatch together form an insulating layer producing a cool interior in summer and a warm one in winter.The village's most distinctive value lies in the doubling of nature and culture.

From November to March each year large numbers of whooper swans arrive from Siberia to winter in the bay in front of the village; century-old seaweed houses and flocks of swans form one of the most recognisable rural landscapes of winter in northern China, making the village a vivid exemplar of humans living in harmony with nature.In conservation terms Yandunjiao represents a different path from Dongchudao.

Dongchudao relies on unified collective protection and development with distinctive homestays; Yandunjiao is characterised by birdwatching, photography, sketching and fishing-life experiences, bringing both the village's traditional fabric and the bay ecosystem in which it sits within the scope of protection and use.Scholars have studied the protection and development of the traditional coastal village landscape of Jiaodong using Yandunjiao as a case, arguing that conservation of coastal traditional villages should not be limited to the buildings themselves but should treat village, bay, wetland, migratory bird habitat and fishing practices as one complete cultural landscape system; otherwise merely repairing buildings while damaging the ecological substrate will in the end still destroy the vitality of the village.

This view has important methodological significance for the conservation of coastal traditional villages in Shandong and across China.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 保存50多幢200多年历史的海草房
  • 村名源于明代海防烟墩即烽火台
  • 外墙石块采自海岸悬崖，屋顶海草来自大海
  • 石墙承重+木梁檩+笆子抹泥+海草苫顶+压脊
  • 每年11月至次年3月大天鹅抵海湾越冬
  • 百年海草房与成群大天鹅的冬季景观
  • 与东楮岛不同的活化路径：观鸟摄影·写生·渔家体验
  • 村落·海湾·湿地·候鸟·渔业应视为完整文化景观系统

34. 俚岛镇海草房群落

The Seaweed-House Cluster of Lidao Town
明清 海草房聚落群 中国传统村落 威海市荣成市俚岛镇：大庄许家社区、东烟墩社区、烟墩角社区、东崮村、中我岛村

威海荣成市俚岛镇是胶东海草房分布最密集的乡镇。

该镇的大庄许家社区、东烟墩社区、烟墩角社区、东崮村，以及2025年入选第六批山东省传统村落的中我岛村，与宁津街道的东楮岛村、东墩村、留村、马栏耩村、渠隔村，人和镇的院夼村，港湾街道的大鱼岛村，港西镇的小西村、巍巍村，共同构成荣成沿海海草房聚落连绵带。

据不完全统计，荣成市现存海草房有95000多间，分布在317个村，呈零散状分布在沿海区域地带。荣成市民俗研究者于迎雨指出，四十年前海草房曾在我国的海岸线上广泛分布着，但在不到半个世纪的时间里，只有荣成市还存有相对集中的海草房。

威海全市现存海草房4000多栋，分布在环翠区、荣成、文登、乳山的23个乡镇、600多个村落，其中荣成沿海区域的海草房最为古老、集中、最具有代表性。

从聚落形态看，俚岛镇的海草房村落具有高度一致性：村落多选址于海湾内侧的缓坡地带，背山面海，既避冬季北向海风，又便于渔船出入；街巷随地形自由延伸，一般有一条主街平行于海岸，支巷垂直通向海边；

院落以一进三合院或四合院为主，坐北朝南，正房三至五间，厢房两至三间，院墙多用石块垒砌，院门设在东南或正南，门内设影壁。

海草房的屋顶坡度比传统瓦顶更陡，一般在50度以上，有利于雨天排水顺畅，同时可延长海草房的寿命。厚达40厘米以上的石料墙体，在夏日炎炎的白天与草顶一同阻隔了热辐射；冬季，厚实的材料在白天充分吸收日照，并有效阻碍室内热量散失，保证了室内温度的稳定和居住的舒适。

这些村落的居民世代以渔业为生，院落内常见渔网、浮子、船具与海草垛，院外则有晾晒场与小型码头，形成居住、生产、晾晒、停泊一体化的渔村空间结构。这种结构在山东乃至全国的滨海渔村中都极具代表性，是海洋渔耕文化在聚落形态上的直接投射。

— ENGLISH —

Lidao town in Rongcheng, Weihai, has the densest distribution of seaweed houses in Jiaodong.

Its Dazhuang-Xujia, Dongyandun, Yandunjiao and Donggu communities, together with Zhongwodao village which was inscribed in the sixth batch of Shandong provincial traditional villages in 2025, join Dongchudao, Dongdun, Liu and Malanjiang in Ningjin subdistrict, YuanKuang in Renhe town, Dayudao in Gangwan subdistrict and Xiaoxi and Weiwei in Gangxi town to form a continuous belt of seaweed-house settlements along the Rongcheng coast.According to incomplete statistics, more than 95,000 seaweed-house rooms survive in Rongcheng, scattered across 317 villages along the coast.

The Rongcheng folklorist Yu Yingyu has noted that forty years ago seaweed houses were distributed along the whole Chinese coastline, yet in less than half a century only Rongcheng retains them in any concentration.

Weihai as a whole has over 4,000 seaweed houses across 23 townships and more than 600 villages in Huancui, Rongcheng, Wendeng and Rushan, of which the coastal area of Rongcheng is the oldest, most concentrated and most representative.In settlement form the seaweed-house villages of Lidao are highly consistent.

They sit on gentle slopes inside bays, backed by hills and facing the sea, sheltered from winter northerly gales yet convenient for boats. Lanes follow the terrain, generally with one main street parallel to the shore and branch lanes running perpendicular to the water.

Compounds are mostly single-bay three-side or four-side courtyards facing south, with three to five bays in the principal room and two or three in the wings; courtyard walls are of stone, the gate is set to the south-east or due south, and a screen wall stands inside.The roof pitch is steeper than that of a conventional tiled roof, generally above fifty degrees, which both speeds drainage in rain and extends the life of the thatch.

Stone walls more than 40 cm thick block radiant heat in summer together with the thatch; in winter the massive materials absorb daylight and restrain heat loss, stabilising indoor temperature and comfort.Residents have fished for generations.

Nets, floats, boat gear and stacks of seaweed are common in the yards, while drying grounds and small quays lie outside, producing a fishing-village spatial structure that integrates dwelling, production, drying and mooring.

This structure is highly representative among coastal fishing villages in Shandong and in China as a whole, and is the direct projection of maritime fishing culture onto settlement form.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 荣成现存海草房95000多间，分布在317个村
  • 威海全市4000多栋，分布于23个乡镇600多村
  • 俚岛镇：大庄许家·东烟墩·烟墩角·东崮·中我岛
  • 选址海湾内侧缓坡，背山面海避北风
  • 主街平行海岸、支巷垂直通海的街巷结构
  • 一进三合院或四合院，正房三至五间
  • 屋顶坡度50度以上，石墙厚40厘米以上
  • 居住·生产·晾晒·停泊一体化的渔村空间

35. 巍巍村

Weiwei Village
明清 海草房传承村落 中国传统村落 威海市荣成市港西镇

巍巍村位于威海市荣成市港西镇，与港西镇的小西村同属荣成沿海海草房保存较为集中的村落，已列入中国传统村落名录。村名巍巍，形容其地处高岗、房屋层叠而上的形势。巍巍村在中国建筑史上的特殊意义，在于它是海草房苫顶技艺传承人的聚集地。

港西镇巍巍村村民刘玉启是海草房民居建筑技艺传承人。据他介绍，盖海草房可以简单地划分为备料、垒墙、苫顶三个大的步骤，而最关键的步骤就是往屋顶上苫海草，一栋海草房需要七十多道工序，全部都是手工艺。

一栋海草房的好坏、使用时间的长短，主要取决于海草苫得是否严密——因为只要屋子不漏水，墙是很难倒的，可以一直住下去。为此，人们一般都请那些代代相传、经验丰富的苫匠来建造海草房。苫房的原理跟建造瓦房安装瓦片有相通之处，只不过是用海草从下往上依次向脊顶叠压铺设。苫屋顶的材料中除海草外，还掺有一些麦秸；

苫房的时候，每苫一层海草要加一层麦秸。为了抵御大风，海草房的屋顶上还要特地覆盖一层更厚的海草，也有个别的再盖上一层瓦，这在当地俗称为压脊。而在海边或岛上的渔村，渔民们通常把破旧的渔网罩于草顶上，用石块等小件重物作坠子垂脚，起到防风、防鸟、防盗的作用。屋脊的建造左右倾斜为50度角，因为房顶的坡度越大越便于排水。

在东楮岛村的苫房现场可以看到，海草经过抛掷传递，最终铺在房顶；有人从草垛上抱起大团海草向空中扔去，靠近屋檐处有人踩着脚手架接住，再往高处扔，屋脊上早有人等候，探出双手将海草抱在胸前，然后铺在屋顶，不住地拍打。这些匠人是苫匠，掌握着海草房建造的秘密，在渔村很受尊重。

荣成海草房修缮手艺人王书敬说：屋顶上的海草要苫四十公分左右，得捋直，挤得比较结实，所以渗透率比较低，水可以顺着房坡流下去。等苫好了，这一辈子都再不用调房了，住一百年二百年都不会坏的。另一位传承人程洛乾介绍，在屋顶苫好海草后，还需要抹一层泥来固定海草，防止海风把海草吹走。

— ENGLISH —

Weiwei village in Gangxi town, Rongcheng, Weihai, is, together with Xiaoxi village in the same town, one of the villages where coastal seaweed houses survive in relative concentration, and has been inscribed on the China Traditional Village list.

Its name, towering, describes its position on high ground with houses rising in tiers.The special significance of Weiwei in Chinese architectural history lies in its being a gathering place for the transmitters of the seaweed thatching craft.

Liu Yuqi, a villager of Weiwei in Gangxi town, is a transmitter of the building craft of seaweed houses.

According to him, building a seaweed house can be divided simply into preparing materials, laying walls and thatching the roof, of which the crucial step is thatching: more than seventy processes, all by hand.The quality of a house and the length of its life depend chiefly on how tightly the seaweed is laid - for as long as the roof does not leak, the walls will scarcely fall and the house can be lived in indefinitely.

For this reason people generally engage experienced thatchers whose skill has been handed down through generations.The principle of thatching is akin to laying tiles on a tiled roof, except that seaweed is laid from the bottom up, overlapping toward the ridge.

Besides seaweed, wheat straw is mixed in: for each layer of seaweed a layer of straw is added. To resist strong winds an extra-thick layer of seaweed is spread over the roof top, and in some cases a further layer of tiles, a step locally called pressing the ridge.

In fishing villages on the shore or on islands, fishermen usually drape worn nets over the thatch with stones as weights at the corners, guarding against wind, birds and theft.The ridge is built at an angle of fifty degrees, the steeper the pitch the easier the drainage.

At a thatching site in Dongchudao one can see the seaweed passed by throwing until it is finally laid on the roof: someone lifts a great bundle from the stack and tosses it into the air; someone near the eaves standing on scaffolding catches it and throws it higher; and at the ridge someone waits, leans out, gathers the bundle to his chest and spreads it on the roof, patting it down.

These craftsmen are the thatchers, holders of the secret of building seaweed houses, much respected in fishing villages.Wang Shujing, a repair craftsman of Rongcheng seaweed houses, explains that the thatch on the roof should be about forty centimetres deep, combed straight and packed tight so that permeability is low and water runs down the slope.

Once thatched, he says, the house never needs re-roofing in one's lifetime and will not fail in a hundred or two hundred years. Another transmitter, Cheng Luoqian, notes that after thatching a layer of mud is applied to fix the seaweed and prevent the sea wind blowing it away.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 海草房苫顶技艺传承人的聚集地
  • 传承人刘玉启、王书敬、程洛乾
  • 苫房原理：从下往上依次向脊顶叠压铺设
  • 海草掺麦秸，每苫一层海草加一层麦秸
  • 压脊：覆更厚海草或加瓦；渔网罩顶以石坠脚
  • 屋脊左右倾斜50度角，坡度越大越利排水
  • 苫厚约40厘米，捋直压实以降低渗透率
  • 苫好的房子住一百年二百年都不会坏

36. 高家庄子村与招远古村群

Gaojiazhuangzi Village and the Zhaoyuan Ancient Village Group
明清 胶东丘陵商贸型村落 中国传统村落 烟台市招远市辛庄镇：高家庄子村、大涝洼村、孟格庄村、徐家疃村；张星镇等

烟台招远市是山东传统村落资源最富集的县级市之一。

仅辛庄镇即有高家盒子村、大涝洼村、孟格庄村、徐家疃村入选中国传统村落，张星镇则有徐家村、北栾家河村、川里林家村、丛家村、界沟姜家村、口后王家村、奶子场村、上院村、石棚村、段家洼村、仓口陈家村、宅科村等十余村，蚕庄镇有山后冯家村，加上莱州市程郭镇前武官村、栖霞市苏家店镇后寨村、海阳市郭城镇战场泊村，共同构成胶东丘陵地区规模最大的传统村落群。

招远地处胶东半岛西北部，属低山丘陵地带，黄金矿产资源丰富，明清以来采金、冶炼与商贸活动发达，形成了一批由矿冶与商贸支撑的富裕村落。与荣成的海草房渔村不同，招远的传统村落以砖石合院为主，屋顶多采用青瓦而非海草，这与其地处内陆丘陵、不直接临海的区位直接相关。

高家庄子村等村落的建筑，体现出胶东民居的典型特征：院落以四合院为主，正房三至五间，采用抬梁式木构架，硬山顶，墙体下部以规整条石砌筑，上部为青砖，讲究的人家采用磨砖对缝工艺；

屋顶以青瓦覆盖，脊部设吻兽与花脊，出檐较深。门楼是装饰的重点，门枕石、挑檐石、墀头处多施石雕与砖雕，题材以吉祥图案、博古器物与戏曲故事为主。

清末民初，胶东民居在建筑与装饰上出现了明显的中西交融趋势。烟台、青岛开埠后，西洋的建筑样式、玻璃、五金与彩色瓷砖经商路传入内地，胶东民居开始在传统四合院框架上加入拱券门窗、西式柱头、彩色玻璃与水泥抹面等元素，形成所谓中西合璧的门面做法。

研究者将这一时期胶东民居的建筑与装饰特征概括为：结构仍为传统抬梁式，立面与细部则大量吸收西洋装饰语汇，反映出近代开埠对山东内陆乡村建筑审美的深刻影响。

— ENGLISH —

Zhaoyuan, a county-level city in Yantai, is one of the richest in traditional village resources in Shandong.

In Xingzhuang town alone, Gaojiazhuangzi, Dalaowa, Menggezhuang and Xujiatuan have been inscribed as China Traditional Villages, while Zhangxing town contributes more than ten - Xujia, Beiluanjiahe, Chuanlinlinjia, Congjia, Jiegoujiangjia, Kouhouwangjia, Naizichang, Shangyuan, Shipeng, Duanjiawa, Cangkouchenjia and Zhaike - and Canzhuang town adds Shanhoufengjia.

Together with Qianwuguan in Chengguo town, Laizhou, Houzhai in Sujiadian town, Qixia, and Zhanchangpo in Guocheng town, Haiyang, they form the largest group of traditional villages in the hilly Jiaodong interior.Zhaoyuan lies in the north-west of the Jiaodong Peninsula in low hills and is rich in gold; since the Ming and Qing, mining, smelting and commerce flourished there, prosperous villages supported by ore extraction and trade.

Unlike the seaweed fishing villages of Rongcheng, the traditional villages of Zhaoyuan are mainly brick-and-stone courtyard houses roofed with grey tiles rather than seaweed - a direct consequence of their inland hilly position away from the open coast.Villages such as Gaojiazhuangzi show the typical features of Jiaodong dwellings: courtyards are mainly four-side with three to five bays in the principal room, using a post-and-lintel timber frame with a flush gable roof; the lower part of the wall is laid in regular ashlar and the upper part in grey brick, with well-to-do households using the finely ground brick technique with tight joints; roofs are covered in grey tiles with ornamental ridges and beasts and a generous overhang.The gate tower is the focus of decoration.

Carving in stone and brick appears on door-bearing stones, corbels and gable shoulders, with motifs of auspicious emblems, antique vessels and opera scenes.In the late Qing and early Republic, Jiaodong dwellings showed a marked tendency toward Chinese-Western fusion.

After Yantai and Qingdao opened as treaty ports, Western building forms, glass, hardware and coloured tiles travelled inland along trade routes, and Jiaodong dwellings began to add arched openings, Western capitals, coloured glass and cement rendering to the traditional courtyard framework, producing the so-called Chinese-Western fused facade.Researchers summarise the architectural and decorative character of Jiaodong dwellings in this period as retaining a traditional post-and-lintel structure while absorbing a large Western decorative vocabulary on the elevations and in the details, reflecting the profound influence of the treaty ports on the architectural taste of inland Shandong villages.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 招远辛庄镇四村、张星镇十余村集中入选中国传统村落
  • 胶东丘陵地区规模最大的传统村落群
  • 采金、冶炼与商贸支撑的富裕村落
  • 砖石合院为主，青瓦屋顶，与荣成海草房相区别
  • 下部条石、上部青砖，讲究人家磨砖对缝
  • 门枕石、挑檐石、墀头的石雕砖雕
  • 清末民初中西合璧门面：拱券门窗、西式柱头、彩色玻璃
  • 结构传统抬梁式、立面吸收西洋语汇

37. 西河阳村与龙口古村群

Xiheyang Village and the Longkou Ancient Village Group
明清 胶东平原富商村落 中国传统村落 烟台市龙口市诸由观镇西河阳村；徐福街道桑岛村；芦头镇庵夼村、界沟张家村；黄山馆镇馆前后徐村

烟台龙口市地处胶东半岛西北部、渤海湾南岸，是山东传统村落与古建筑遗存富集的县级市。诸由观镇的西河阳村，徐福街道的桑岛村，芦头镇的庵夼村、界沟张家村，黄山馆镇的馆前后徐村等，先后列入中国传统村落名录，构成龙口古村群，与招远古村群、莱州前武官村、栖霞后寨村共同组成胶东丘陵传统村落连绵带。西河阳村是龙口传统村落的代表。

明清时期，龙口人经商足迹遍布东北与京津，形成著名的黄县帮，即龙口旧称黄县商帮。商业资本回流家乡，营建了一批规模宏大、工艺考究的宅院，使西河阳等村落的建筑水平远超一般乡村。这些宅院的共同特征是：多进四合院沿轴线纵深展开，大门、影壁、前院、过厅、正房、后楼依次排列；

墙体下部为规整条石，上部为青砖，磨砖对缝；屋面为青瓦硬山，脊饰精美；门楼、影壁、墀头、檐枋处的砖雕木雕石雕三雕俱全，题材丰富、工艺精湛。部分宅院还设有花园、书房与账房，反映出商人家庭对生活品质与经营管理的双重需求。

村内街巷规整，主街宽阔，两侧高墙连绵，形成胶东富商村落特有的深宅深巷风貌。桑岛村则代表了另一种类型——海岛渔村。桑岛是龙口近海的一座小岛，岛上村落就地取材，以石垒墙，屋顶因海岛风大而采用较缓的坡度并以海草或瓦覆顶，院落紧凑、街巷狭窄以避海风，呈现出与陆地村落显著不同的海岛聚落形态。

黄山馆镇的馆前后徐村，则因位于古代驿道沿线而带有驿站型村落的特征，主街两侧保留有车马店与商铺旧址，是山东串珠式点状分布聚落网络中的一个节点。

— ENGLISH —

Longkou, a county-level city in Yantai, lies on the north-west of the Jiaodong Peninsula on the southern shore of Bohai Bay and is rich in traditional villages and old buildings.

Xiheyang in Zhuyouguan town, Sangdao in Xufu subdistrict, Ankuang and Jiegouzhangjia in Lutou town, and Guanqianhouxu in Huangshanguan town have all been inscribed on the China Traditional Village list, forming the Longkou ancient village group which, together with the Zhaoyuan group, Qianwuguan in Laizhou and Houzhai in Qixia, makes up a continuous belt of traditional villages in the Jiaodong hills.Xiheyang is representative.

In Ming-Qing times merchants from Longkou traded throughout Manchuria, Beijing and Tianjin, forming the celebrated Huangxian bang, the merchant guild of Longkou's former name Huangxian.

Commercial capital flowed home and paid for mansions of great scale and refined craft, raising the building standard of villages such as Xiheyang far above the ordinary countryside.Shared features of these houses are: multi-courtyard compounds unfolding along a deep axis, with gate, screen wall, front court, passage hall, principal room and rear building in sequence; walls with regular ashlar below and grey brick above in finely ground tight-jointed work; grey-tiled flush gable roofs with ornamental ridges; and brick, wood and stone carving on gate towers, screen walls, gable shoulders and eave beams, rich in subject and exquisite in execution.

Some compounds also had gardens, studies and accounting offices, reflecting the double demands of a merchant household for quality of life and business management.Village lanes are regular, the main street broad and flanked by continuous high walls, giving the distinctive deep-house-deep-lane character of wealthy Jiaodong merchant villages.Sangdao village represents another type - the island fishing village.

Sangdao is a small island off Longkou; its settlement is built of local stone, its roofs, because of strong island winds, are of gentler pitch and covered with seaweed or tiles, and its compounds are compact and lanes narrow as protection against the sea wind - an island settlement form markedly different from mainland villages.Guanqianhouxu in Huangshanguan town, situated on an old post road, carries the character of a post settlement, with the remains of caravanserais and shops on either side of its main street - one node in Shandong's bead-like network of point-distributed settlements.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 龙口五村集中入选中国传统村落
  • 明清黄县帮商业资本回流营建豪宅
  • 多进四合院沿轴线纵深展开
  • 下部条石、上部青砖、磨砖对缝
  • 砖雕木雕石雕三雕俱全
  • 部分宅院设花园、书房与账房
  • 桑岛村：海岛渔村，缓坡屋顶、紧凑院落、窄巷避风
  • 馆前后徐村：古驿道沿线的驿站型村落

38. 井塘古村

Jingtang Ancient Village
明代至今，500余年 明代山地城堡式古村 中国传统村落 潍坊市青州市王府街道，市区西南15公里

井塘古村位于潍坊市青州市区西南15公里处的王府街道，是一座历经500余年沧桑风雨、依旧保存较完好的明代建筑风貌的古村落，已列入中国传统村落名录。

村落依山而建，依托衡王府院落，形成了具有明代建筑风格又有西部山区居住特色的古建筑群，是山东省内保存比较完好的一处古村落。井塘古村最突出的特征是它的防御体系。整个村落被古城墙所包围，城墙用青石砌成，每隔30多米修建一处城堡即炮楼，向人们展示着明代古村自卫防御功能。

这种城墙加炮楼的环形防御格局，在山东传统村落中较为罕见，与明代山东社会动荡、山区村落普遍自卫的历史背景直接相关。村内建筑以四合院为主，依山就势层层叠建。墙体采用当地青石干垒或灰浆坐砌，屋顶以石板或青瓦覆盖，院内设石碾、石磨、石桌、石凳，院外街巷以青石板铺砌。

由于地处山区，院落规模较小但布局紧凑，上下院落之间以石阶相连，形成立体的聚落空间。井塘古村最为国内外人士所瞩目的，是位于北峰中的北魏郑道昭《白驹谷题名》与《白云堂题刻》等三处题刻。该石刻作为中国魏碑三奇之一，吸引着众多中外著名书法研究爱好者到此谒拜，使这个山村同时具备了建筑遗产与书法艺术的双重价值。

青州市的传统村落资源在潍坊市中最为富集，除井塘村外，庙子镇的黄鹿井村亦已列入第五批中国传统村落名录。青州作为古九州之一与明清山东东路重镇，其传统村落密集分布的现象，与济南、淄博、烟台、潍坊、济宁、临沂六市共同构成山东村落资源的核心富集带。

今日井塘古村以传统婚俗表演等民俗活动闻名，是山东传统村落活化利用中民俗展示路径的代表案例之一。

— ENGLISH —

Jingtang ancient village lies in Wangfu subdistrict, 15 km south-west of Qingzhou city, Weifang. It has weathered more than five hundred years yet still preserves its Ming architectural character comparatively intact, and has been inscribed on the China Traditional Village list.

Built against the mountain and attached to the estate of the Prince of Heng, it forms an old building group that combines Ming architectural style with the dwelling character of the western hills and is one of the best preserved ancient villages in Shandong.Its most striking feature is the defensive system.

The whole village is enclosed by an old city wall of blue stone with a fortress or gun tower every thirty-odd metres, demonstrating the self-defence function of a Ming mountain village.

This ring defence of wall plus towers is relatively rare among Shandong traditional villages and relates directly to the social unrest of Ming Shandong and the general need for mountain villages to defend themselves.The buildings are mainly courtyard houses rising in tiers against the slope.

Walls are of local blue stone, dry-stacked or mortar-bedded; roofs are of stone slabs or grey tiles; courtyards contain stone rollers, mills, tables and benches; and lanes outside are paved with blue flags.

Because of the mountain terrain the compounds are small but compactly laid out, linked by stone steps to form a three-dimensional settlement space.What attracts visitors from China and abroad above all are three inscriptions on the northern peak, including Zheng Daozhao's Northern Wei Inscription at White Colt Valley and Baiyun Hall Inscription.

Counted among the three wonders of Northern Wei stele calligraphy in China, they draw numerous Chinese and foreign students of calligraphy, giving the mountain village a double value as architectural heritage and as calligraphic art.Qingzhou has the richest concentration of traditional villages in Weifang; besides Jingtang, Huanglujing village in Miaozi town has been inscribed in the fifth batch of China Traditional Villages.

As one of the ancient nine provinces and an important Ming-Qing town of eastern Shandong, Qingzhou's dense distribution of traditional villages, together with Jinan, Zibo, Yantai, Weifang, Jining and Linyi, forms the core resource-rich belt of Shandong's villages.Today Jingtang is known for folk activities such as traditional wedding performances, making it a representative case of the folklore-display approach to the revitalisation of Shandong's traditional villages.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 历经500余年，保存较完好的明代建筑风貌古村
  • 依托明代衡王府院落形成
  • 古城墙包围，每隔30多米设城堡炮楼
  • 四合院依山层层叠建，上下院落以石阶相连
  • 北魏郑道昭《白驹谷题名》《白云堂题刻》
  • 中国魏碑三奇之一，书法艺术价值极高
  • 青州庙子镇黄鹿井村亦为第五批中国传统村落
  • 以传统婚俗表演等民俗活动活化

39. 上九山村

Shangjiushan Village
北宋初年至今，1000余年 千年石头村落 中国传统村落 济宁市邹城市石墙镇，北接朗子山，南邻微山湖

上九山村位于济宁市邹城市石墙镇，始建于北宋初年，距今已一千一百多年的历史，是国内罕见的石头村落。村子因为周边有九座山环绕四周，故名上九山村。古村坐落在两山之间，北接朗子山，向南毗邻微山湖。

村内有明清建筑风格的古民居300余套，石村依山而建，石院、石墙、石阶、石井浑然一体，已列入中国传统村落名录。上九山村所在的邹城，素有东方圣都、邹鲁圣地之称，是孟子故里。

受伏羲文化和儒家文化的影响，上九山村在房屋的建造、格局布置、人员居住上都颇有讲究，反映了当地村民遵守孝道、仁义和礼仪的思想，是儒家思想对村风民俗影响的直接体现。

这种讲究具体地表现为：院落以四合院为基本单元，正房为长辈居住，厢房依长幼分配；大门位置、影壁设置、台阶高度均体现尊卑秩序；村内设有古戏台、老井、钱庄、私塾等公共设施，构成儒家乡村社会中教化、交往、经济与信仰的完整物质载体。

从建筑技术看，上九山村的石砌民居与鲁南山区体系一致：墙体以当地青石与页岩块石垒砌，厚实坚固；屋顶以不规则薄石板错缝沿茬叠压铺设，这种做法既解决了山区缺少烧制瓦的问题，又使屋顶具有极好的耐候性。村落沿山坡层层展开，街巷以石板铺砌，排水利用自然坡度与石砌明沟，形成完整的山地排水系统。

村中石碾有200余年的历史，抗战时期村民们靠它把米碾好支援驻扎在此的115师，使这个千年古村同时成为红色记忆的承载地。近年来上九山古村被辟为景区，是山东传统村落中旅游开发程度较高的案例之一，也因而面临保护与开发如何平衡的普遍课题。

— ENGLISH —

Shangjiushan village in Shiqiang town, Zoucheng, Jining, was founded in the early Northern Song and has a history of over eleven hundred years, making it a rare stone village in China. It takes its name from the nine hills that surround it.

The old village lies between two mountains, joining Mount Langzi to the north and approaching Weishan Lake to the south.

It contains more than 300 old dwellings in Ming-Qing style, built of stone against the slope, with stone yards, walls, steps and wells forming a single whole, and has been inscribed on the China Traditional Village list.Zoucheng, where the village lies, is honoured as the Eastern Holy Capital and the holy land of Zou and Lu, and is the home of Mencius.

Influenced by Fuxi culture and Confucian culture, Shangjiushan is meticulous in house construction, layout and the assignment of rooms, reflecting the villagers' observance of filial piety, benevolence and propriety - a direct expression of Confucian influence on village customs.Concretely, courtyards take the four-side house as the basic unit; the principal room houses the elders and the wings are allocated by seniority; the position of the gate, the setting of the screen wall and the height of the steps all express the order of rank.

Public facilities including an old opera stage, an old well, a money shop and a private school together constitute the complete material carriers of edification, intercourse, economy and belief in a Confucian rural society.In building technique the stone dwellings of Shangjiushan belong to the southern Shandong mountain system: walls are laid in local blue stone and shale blocks, thick and solid; roofs are laid in irregular thin slabs overlapped course by course along the slope.

This both solved the shortage of fired tiles in the hills and gave the roofs excellent weather resistance.The village spreads in tiers up the slope; lanes are paved with stone slabs; drainage uses the natural gradient and open stone channels, forming a complete mountain drainage system.The village stone roller has a history of more than two hundred years; during the war of resistance the villagers used it to hull rice to support the 115th Division stationed there, so that this thousand-year-old village also carries the memory of the revolution.In recent years Shangjiushan has been developed as a scenic area, one of the more heavily touristed cases among Shandong's traditional villages, and therefore faces the universal question of how to balance conservation and development.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 始建于北宋初年，距今1100余年
  • 因周边九座山环绕得名
  • 明清建筑风格古民居300余套
  • 石院·石墙·石阶·石井浑然一体
  • 儒家礼制：正房长辈、厢房长幼、门位影壁台阶体现尊卑
  • 古戏台、老井、钱庄、私塾构成完整乡村公共设施
  • 不规则薄石板错缝沿茬叠压铺顶
  • 200余年石碾，抗战时期支援115师

40. 竹泉村

Zhuquan Village
元明时期至今 泉水竹林型古村 中国传统村落 临沂市沂南县铜井镇

竹泉村位于临沂市沂南县铜井镇，元明时期叫做泉上庄，清朝乾隆年间改名竹泉村，至少有四百年的历史，已列入中国传统村落名录。这里泉依山出、竹因泉生，村民绕泉而居，砌石为房，农耕为业，是中国北方难得一见的桃花源式的生活环境。竹林隐茅舍，家家临清泉，田园瓜果香，居者乐而寿，今世有缘梅做伴，他生无悔竹为邻。

明末兵部右侍郎高名衡、明末青州衡王府仪宾高炯都曾在此修建别墅，使这个山村在明清时期即带有文人园林的色彩。竹泉村最独特之处在于它颠覆了人们对山东乡村的固有印象。山东民居一般认为厚重封闭、缺水少绿，而竹泉村却以泉群喷涌、竹林环绕、石屋错落、流水穿村的景象，呈现出温润如玉的江南水乡韵味，被学者称为北方少见的泉竹水乡。

从建筑与聚落的角度看，竹泉村的核心机制是泉：村内泉群常年喷涌，形成溪流贯穿全村；村民绕泉而居，房屋沿溪流两岸布置，院门多朝向水流；泉水既是饮用水源，也是洗涤、灌溉与景观用水，溪流同时构成村落的排水骨架与空间主轴。这种以泉系组织聚落的方式，与鲁中山区的以沟壑排水、胶东渔村的以海湾停泊、平原村落的以街巷划分，共同构成山东村落形态的四种水系组织模式。

建筑上，竹泉村的石屋采用当地石材干垒，屋顶以草或瓦覆盖，墙体较鲁中石寨略薄，门窗略大，反映出沂蒙山区气候较温和、冬季不及鲁中严寒的特点。今日的竹泉村在保护传统风貌的同时开发了水上休闲娱乐区，是山东传统村落中旅游与社区共生模式的代表案例之一。

同属沂南县的常山庄村、大峪庄村，与沂水县的关顶村、八大庄村、王庄村、云头峪村、崮崖村、石棚村，平邑县的李家石屋村、九间棚村、金三峪村，共同构成沂蒙山区传统村落集群，是临沂市六市富集格局中的重要一极。

— ENGLISH —

Zhuquan village in Tongjing town, Yinan county, Linyi, was called Quanshangzhuang in the Yuan and Ming and renamed Zhuquan in the Qianlong reign of the Qing. With a history of at least four hundred years, it has been inscribed on the China Traditional Village list.

Here springs issue from the mountain and bamboo grows because of the springs; villagers live around the water, build in stone and farm the land - a peach-blossom-spring environment rarely seen in northern China.Bamboo hides the thatched cottages, every household fronts a clear spring, the fields smell of melon and fruit, and the dwellers are happy and long-lived.

In the late Ming, Gao Mingheng, right vice-minister of war, and Gao Jiong, a son-in-law of the Prince of Heng of Qingzhou, both built villas here, giving the mountain village a literati-garden character.The most distinctive thing about Zhuquan is that it overturns the usual image of the Shandong countryside.

Shandong dwellings are generally thought heavy and closed, short of water and greenery, yet Zhuquan presents gushing springs, surrounding bamboo, scattered stone houses and a stream running through the village with the moist, gentle flavour of a Jiangnan water town; scholars call it a rare spring-and-bamboo water town of the north.Architecturally and in settlement terms the core mechanism is the spring.

The springs flow all year, forming a stream that runs through the whole village; houses are arranged along both banks with gates generally facing the water.

The spring water serves for drinking, washing and irrigation as well as for scenery, while the stream forms both the drainage skeleton and the spatial axis.This organisation of a settlement by a spring system, together with gully drainage in the central Shandong hills, bay mooring in Jiaodong fishing villages and lane division in plain villages, makes up the four modes of water-system organisation in Shandong village form.In construction, the stone houses of Zhuquan are dry-stacked from local stone with thatch or tile roofs; walls are somewhat thinner than those of the central Shandong stone fortresses and openings somewhat larger, reflecting the milder climate of the Yimeng hills where winters are less severe than in central Shandong.Today Zhuquan has developed a water-based leisure area while conserving its traditional fabric, making it a representative case of tourism and community coexisting among Shandong's traditional villages.Together with Changshanzhuang and Dayuzhuang in Yinan, Guanding, Badazhuang, Wangzhuang, Yuntouyu, Guya and Shipeng in Yishui, and Lijiashiwu, Jiujianpeng and Jinsanyu in Pingyi, it forms the traditional village cluster of the Yimeng hills - an important pole within the resource-rich pattern of the six cities.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 元明名泉上庄，清乾隆改名竹泉村，四百年历史
  • 泉依山出、竹因泉生，北方少见的泉竹水乡
  • 明末高名衡、高炯曾建别墅
  • 以泉系组织聚落：绕泉而居、屋沿溪布、门朝水流
  • 泉水兼具饮用·洗涤·灌溉·景观·排水五重功能
  • 山东村落水系组织的四种模式之一
  • 石屋墙体较鲁中石寨略薄、门窗略大
  • 与常山庄、九间棚等构成沂蒙山区传统村落集群

41. 常山庄村

Changshanzhuang Village
明清 沂蒙红色古村 中国传统村落 临沂市沂南县马牧池乡

常山庄村位于临沂市沂南县马牧池乡，是沂蒙山区依托石材营造的传统村落，已列入中国传统村落名录。村落地处沂蒙山区腹地，四周群山环抱，村内房屋、院墙、街巷、水渠全部采用当地石材，是鲁中南山区石砌民居体系的东延部分。

常山庄村的建筑延续了沂蒙山区石屋的典型做法：墙体以当地青石与片岩干垒或坐砌，厚实厚重；屋顶以石板或麦草覆盖，两坡硬山，出檐较浅；院落以三合院为主，正房坐北朝南，厢房分列，院门设在东南，院内设石碾、石磨与柴垛，院外街巷以石板铺砌并设石砌排水明沟。

由于地处抗战时期的沂蒙革命根据地核心区，常山庄村同时承载了极为厚重的红色记忆。这一区域留存有大量抗战时期的村落与建筑遗存，村内的石屋、石墙、石巷在战时既是民居，也是掩体与交通壕，石材的坚固性在这一特殊历史时期发挥了实际的防御作用。今日常山庄所在的区域已建成红嫂家乡旅游区，以重温抗战时期古村风貌为定位，通过修复院落、复原场景与展演活动，使传统村落的保护与红色文化的传承形成合力。

从山东传统村落的类型谱系看，常山庄代表了一种重要的复合类型：建筑形态上是沂蒙石砌民居，历史价值上是革命纪念地，活化路径上是红色旅游与乡村社区共建。与之类似的还有沂水县夏蔚镇的王庄村、云头峪村，平邑县地方镇的九间棚村，以及枣庄山亭区凫城镇的王家湾村（峄县抗日民主政府旧址、鲁南第一个红色政权成立地）。这些村落共同构成山东传统村落中极具特色的红色古村系列，是山地石砌民居与近现代历史记忆叠加的独特遗产。

— ENGLISH —

Changshanzhuang village in Mamuchi township, Yinan county, Linyi, is a traditional village in the Yimeng hills built entirely of stone, and has been inscribed on the China Traditional Village list.

Deep in the Yimeng mountains and ringed by hills, its houses, courtyard walls, lanes and water channels are all of local stone, forming the eastern extension of the stone dwelling system of the south-central Shandong hills.Its buildings continue the typical practice of Yimeng stone houses: walls dry-stacked or bedded in local blue stone and schist, thick and massive; roofs of stone slabs or wheat straw, double-pitch with flush gables and shallow overhangs; three-side courtyards with the principal room facing south, wings to either side, the gate to the south-east, stone rollers and mills and woodpiles inside, and lanes outside paved with slabs and drained by open stone channels.Because it lay in the core of the Yimeng revolutionary base area during the war of resistance, Changshanzhuang also carries a profound revolutionary memory.

The region preserves numerous wartime village and building remains; the stone houses, walls and lanes served simultaneously as dwellings, cover and communication trenches, and the solidity of stone performed a real defensive function in that exceptional period.Today the area containing Changshanzhuang has been developed as the Red Sister-in-law Hometown tourist area, positioned around reliving the appearance of the old village during the war.

Through courtyard restoration, scene reconstruction and performance activities, conservation of the traditional village and transmission of revolutionary culture reinforce each other.Within the typology of Shandong's traditional villages, Changshanzhuang represents an important composite type: in architectural form a Yimeng stone dwelling, in historical value a revolutionary memorial site, and in revitalisation path a combination of red tourism and rural community building.Comparable cases include Wangzhuang and Yuntouyu in Xiawei town, Yishui county, Jiujianpeng in Difang town, Pingyi county, and Wangjiawan in Fucheng town, Shanting district, Zaozhuang (site of the Yi county anti-Japanese democratic government, the first red political power in southern Shandong).

Together these villages form a highly distinctive series of red ancient villages within Shandong's traditional villages - a unique heritage in which mountain stone dwellings and modern historical memory are superimposed.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 沂蒙山区腹地的石砌传统村落
  • 房屋、院墙、街巷、水渠全用当地石材
  • 墙体干垒或坐砌，屋顶石板或麦草，两坡硬山
  • 三合院为主，院门东南，石砌排水明沟
  • 抗战时期沂蒙革命根据地核心区
  • 战时石屋石墙石巷兼具民居·掩体·交通壕功能
  • 今日为红嫂家乡旅游区，保护与传承形成合力
  • 与王家湾、九间棚等构成红色古村系列

42. 五埠村与二奇楼村

Wubu Village and Erqilou Village
明清 泰山南麓石砌村落 中国传统村落 泰安市肥城市孙伯镇五埠村、岈山村；岱岳区道朗镇二奇楼村、里峪村

泰安市是山东传统村落的重要分布区之一。肥城市孙伯镇的五埠村、岈山村，岱岳区道朗镇的二奇楼村，与大汶口镇的山西街村共同构成泰山南麓的传统村落群，其中五埠村、二奇楼村已列入中国传统村落名录。

这一区域地处泰山南麓的丘陵地带，海拔介于150至400米之间，既不同于鲁中山区的高山深谷，也不同于鲁西平原的一马平川，形成了独具特色的过渡地带村落形态。

五埠村、二奇楼村的建筑兼具山地石砌与平原合院两方面的特征：墙体下部以当地青石垒砌，上部则较多使用土坯或青砖，呈现出由石向砖土过渡的材料梯度；屋顶以青瓦或石板覆盖，坡度介于鲁中山区的陡坡与鲁北平顶之间；

院落以三合院、四合院为主，布局较为规整，反映出平原地区礼制秩序对村落形态的影响。

这一过渡性，使泰山南麓村落成为理解山东民居类型分区的极佳样本：从胶东海草房到鲁中干垒石屋，从鲁南石板房到鲁西平原囤顶房，各类特征在这一区域交汇融合，形成连续渐变的谱系，而非彼此割裂的类型。区域内还保存有大量历史文化遗存。

道朗镇里峪村有仰天神龟、国画崖、泰山老奶奶石等景点十余处，以及黄巢寨遗址与绵延数十里的齐长城遗址；村庄完好保存了依山势而建的石屋，古朴而天然。大汶口镇的山西街村则紧邻大汶口遗址，使史前聚落与明清村落共处一地，形成跨越数千年的聚落史现场。

从保护实践看，泰安片区近年通过成立党建联盟、发展特色旅游，吸引了大批游客与写生学生，使里峪村等昔日闭塞的山村重新获得活力，是山东传统村落活化中社区组织驱动型的典型案例。

— ENGLISH —

Tai'an is one of the important distribution areas for traditional villages in Shandong.

Wubu and Yashan in Sunbo town, Feicheng, Erqilou in Daolang town, Daiyue district, and Shanxijie in Dawenkou town together form a traditional village group at the southern foot of Mount Tai, of which Wubu and Erqilou have been inscribed on the China Traditional Village list.The area lies in the hills south of Mount Tai at elevations between 150 and 400 metres - neither the high mountains and deep valleys of central Shandong nor the open flatness of the western plain - and has produced a distinctive transitional village form.The buildings of Wubu and Erqilou combine features of both mountain stone and plain courtyard construction: the lower part of the wall is laid in local blue stone while the upper part makes greater use of adobe or grey brick, showing a material gradient from stone to brick and earth; roofs are of grey tiles or stone slabs with pitches between the steep slopes of central Shandong and the flat roofs of the north; compounds are mainly three-side or four-side courtyards with relatively regular layouts, reflecting the influence of plain ritual order on village form.This transitional character makes the villages south of Mount Tai an excellent sample for understanding the regional typology of Shandong dwellings.

From the seaweed houses of Jiaodong to the dry-stacked stone houses of central Shandong, from the slab-stone houses of the south to the flat trap roofs of the western plain, all these characteristics meet and blend here, forming a continuously graded spectrum rather than mutually isolated types.The area also preserves abundant historical remains.

Liyu village in Daolang town has more than ten scenic spots including the Sky-looking Tortoise, the Painting Cliff and the Old Granny of Mount Tai rock, together with the Huangchao Fortress site and several tens of li of the Great Wall of Qi; the village preserves intact its stone houses built along the slope, simple and natural.

Shanxijie village in Dawenkou town adjoins the Dawenkou site, so that a prehistoric settlement and a Ming-Qing village occupy the same ground - a settlement-history site spanning several millennia.In conservation practice, the Tai'an area has in recent years formed party-building alliances and developed distinctive tourism, attracting large numbers of visitors and sketching students and restoring vitality to formerly isolated mountain villages such as Liyu - a typical case of the community-organisation-driven approach to revitalising traditional villages in Shandong.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 泰山南麓丘陵地带，海拔150至400米
  • 墙体下部青石、上部土坯或青砖的材料梯度
  • 屋顶坡度介于鲁中陡坡与鲁北平顶之间
  • 各类民居特征交汇融合的连续渐变谱系
  • 里峪村：仰天神龟、国画崖、黄巢寨遗址、齐长城遗址
  • 大汶口山西街村：史前聚落与明清村落共处一地
  • 肥城五埠村、岈山村与岱岳二奇楼村同批入选
  • 社区组织驱动型活化：党建联盟+特色旅游+写生

43. 独山村与贾堌堆村

Dushan Village and Jiagudui Village
明清 鲁西湖区与平原村落 中国传统村落（第六批） 济宁市微山县两城镇独山村；梁山县大路口乡贾堌堆村

济宁市是山东传统村落资源最富集的六市之一。

2023年第六批中国传统村落名录中，济宁市邹城市田黄镇杨峪村、微山县两城镇独山村、金乡县羊山镇葛山村、嘉祥县纸坊镇马市村、商村、隋庄村、青山村、武翟山村、泗水县泗张镇桃源村、梁山县大路口乡贾堌堆村一次性入选，使济宁成为第六批入选数量最多的市之一。

独山村位于微山县两城镇，地处南四湖东岸，是典型的湖区村落。村落因独山而得名，历史上居民半农半渔，既耕种湖滨洼地，也在南四湖中捕捞。建筑上，湖区村落需应对湖水涨落与潮湿环境，房屋多建于高地或人工垫高的台基之上，墙体下部以砖石砌筑以防潮，院落较为开阔以利晾晒渔获。

贾堌堆村位于梁山县大路口乡，拥有一处省级文物保护单位贾堌堆遗址，以及一项省级非物质文化遗产枣梆剧。近年来该村以龙山风韵、特色旅游、农耕小寨、魅力古村为定位，打造农家寨景区，走出一条美丽乡村建设与特色产业同步发展、三产融合与农民增收共进的发展之路。

从区域视角看，济宁的村落类型最为丰富：既有微山湖的湖区村落（独山村、南阳镇），也有邹鲁圣地的山地石村（上九山、杨峪），还有嘉祥、金乡一带的平原农耕村落，以及泗水的丘陵村落（桃源村）。这种类型多样性，源于济宁横跨泰沂山脉西麓、南四湖与鲁西平原三大地貌单元的特殊区位，使济宁成为山东村落类型的天然博物馆。

值得注意的是，近30年冀鲁豫农业村落民宅景观演化过程与机理的研究表明，包括济宁在内的冀鲁豫交界地区，农村民宅在近三十年中经历了从土坯草房到砖瓦平房再到二层楼房的快速更替，传统风貌的消失速度远快于山区村落，这也是鲁西平原传统村落保护面临的共同难题。

— ENGLISH —

Jining is one of the six cities richest in traditional village resources in Shandong.

In the sixth batch of China Traditional Villages announced in 2023, Yangyu in Tianhuang town, Zoucheng, Dushan in Liangcheng town, Weishan, Geshan in Yangshan town, Jinxiang, Mashi, Shang, Sui, Qingshan and Wuzhaishan in Zhifang town, Jiaxiang, Taoyuan in Sizhang town, Sishui, and Jiagudui in Dalukou township, Liangshan, were all inscribed at once, making Jining one of the cities with the largest number of selections in that batch.Dushan village in Liangcheng town, Weishan, lies on the eastern shore of the Nansi lakes and is a typical lake-district village.

Named after Mount Du, its inhabitants historically combined farming and fishing, cultivating the lakeshore lowlands and fishing in the lakes.

Buildings must cope with the rise and fall of lake water and with humidity: houses stand on high ground or on artificially raised platforms, the lower walls are of brick or stone against damp, and courtyards are relatively open for drying the catch.Jiagudui village in Dalukou township, Liangshan, possesses the Jiagudui site, a provincial-level protected heritage site, and the Zao-bang opera, a provincial intangible cultural heritage.

In recent years, positioning itself around the charm of Longshan, distinctive tourism, a small farming hamlet and a charming old village, it has built a farmhouse-fortress scenic area and found a path in which the building of beautiful villages advances together with distinctive industries and the integration of three industries with rising peasant incomes.Regionally, Jining has the richest variety of village types: lake-district villages on Weishan Lake (Dushan, Nanyang), mountain stone villages in the holy land of Zou and Lu (Shangjiushan, Yangyu), plain farming villages in Jiaxiang and Jinxiang, and hill villages in Sishui (Taoyuan).

This diversity derives from Jining's special position spanning the western foot of the Tai-Yi range, the Nansi lakes and the western Shandong plain, making it a natural museum of Shandong village types.It is worth noting that research on the process and mechanism of the landscape evolution of rural dwellings in the Hebei-Shandong-Henan border region over the past thirty years shows that in this area, including Jining, rural houses have passed rapidly from adobe and thatch to brick-and-tile bungalows and then to two-storey buildings, with traditional fabric disappearing far faster than in mountain villages - a common difficulty for the conservation of traditional villages on the western Shandong plain.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 2023年第六批名录中济宁十村一次性入选
  • 独山村：南四湖东岸的半农半渔湖区村落
  • 湖区村落建于高地或垫高台基，下部砖石防潮
  • 贾堌堆村：贾堌堆遗址+枣梆剧两项省级遗产
  • 济宁横跨泰山西麓、南四湖、鲁西平原三大地貌单元
  • 村落类型最丰富的山东天然博物馆
  • 泗水桃源村、邹城杨峪村、金乡葛山村等类型各异
  • 平原村落传统风貌消失速度快于山区

第四章 民居类型与营造技艺

Chapter 4 Dwelling Types and Building Crafts

44. 山东民居的院落格局与基本型制

Courtyard Layout and Basic Typology of Shandong Dwellings
明清定型，延续至20世纪中叶 民居型制总论 总论 山东省全域，各文化区差异显著

山东传统民居在漫长的发展中形成了一套以院落为核心的型制体系，其基本单元是「一正两厢」的三合院与「一正两厢一倒座」的四合院。

院落通常坐北朝南，沿南北纵轴布置，正房居于北端最高处，体量最大、举架最高、装修最精，由长辈居住；东西厢房对称列于两侧，由晚辈居住或作厨房、储藏；南侧的倒座房用作客房、书房或杂物间；

大门多设于东南角的巽位，取「紫气东来」之意，进门迎面设影壁，形成「先抑后扬」的空间序列。这种格局在鲁中、鲁西、鲁北的平原与丘陵地带高度一致，是儒家长幼有序、尊卑有别的伦理秩序在建筑上的直接投射。

胶东半岛因受海洋气候影响，院落更为紧凑，正房与厢房常常紧贴，天井狭小如斗，俗称「四合房」或「四合院」；

鲁中山区受地形限制，院落多依山就势，呈不规则形态，正房与厢房常不在同一标高，形成「错层院」；鲁西南与鲁西平原地区则因地势平坦、宅基宽裕，院落开敞方正，多见两进、三进的深宅大院。

在山东民居中，院落不仅是采光通风与排水的物理容器，更是家庭生产（晾晒粮食、饲养家禽、堆放农具）与礼仪活动（婚丧嫁娶、年节祭祀）的场所，院落的尺度、铺装与植树（石榴、枣树、槐树）都有约定俗成的讲究。值得指出的是，山东民居的正房开间以三间最为普遍，即「一明两暗」：明间为堂屋，设祖宗牌位或神龛，是会客与祭祀的核心；

两次间为卧室，以火炕取暖。经济条件较好的人家修建五间正房，或在正房两端加设耳房，形成「明三暗五」「四破五」等灵活做法。

屋顶形式以硬山顶为主，胶东、鲁中多用草顶或石板顶，鲁西、鲁北多用仰瓦灰梗的小青瓦顶，山墙做法则有「老檐出头」「挑檐石」「墀头」等多种地域变体，构成了辨识度极高的齐鲁乡土风貌。

— ENGLISH —

Over a long evolution the traditional dwellings of Shandong developed a courtyard-centred typological system whose basic units are the three-building courtyard of one main room flanked by two wings, and the four-building siheyuan that adds a south-facing reversal hall.The compound normally sits north and faces south along a north-south axis: the principal room stands at the highest northern end, largest in volume, tallest in framework and richest in ornament, and is occupied by the senior generation; the east and west wing rooms are arranged symmetrically for junior family members, kitchens or storage; the southern reversal house serves guests, study or storage.The main gate is usually placed at the south-east xun position to invoke the auspicious image of purple qi arriving from the east, and a screen wall stands directly inside it, producing the classic spatial sequence of compression followed by release.This layout is remarkably consistent across the plains and hills of central, western and northern Shandong, and is a direct architectural projection of the Confucian ethical order of seniority and hierarchy.On the Jiaodong Peninsula, under the influence of the maritime climate, courtyards are tighter: principal and wing rooms often abut one another and the skywell is as small as a measure, locally called a four-combined house.In the central Shandong mountains, terrain forces irregular plans: principal and wing rooms frequently sit at different levels, creating split-level courtyards.

In the flat, generous building plots of south-western and western Shandong, by contrast, compounds are open and square and two- or three-courtyard deep mansions are common.In the Shandong dwelling the courtyard is not merely a physical container for light, ventilation and drainage, but also the venue of domestic production - drying grain, keeping poultry, stacking farm tools - and of ritual life: weddings, funerals and seasonal sacrifices.Its dimensions, paving and planted trees (pomegranate, jujube, locust) all follow well-established conventions.It is worth noting that three bays is by far the most common width for the principal room, in the pattern known as one bright and two dark: the central bay is the hall housing the ancestral tablets or a niche, and is the core of reception and sacrifice, while the two flanking bays are bedrooms warmed by heated kang beds.

Wealthier households built five-bay principal rooms or added side ear rooms at the two ends, producing flexible variants known as three bright with five implied, or four broken into five.The gable roof predominates; thatch or stone-slab coverings are typical of Jiaodong and central Shandong, while small grey tiles laid in alternating concave and convex courses with lime ridges are typical of western and northern Shandong.Gable-wall treatments vary regionally as projecting old eaves, projecting eave stones and decorated chitou piers, together forming the highly recognisable local character of Qilu vernacular architecture.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 基本单元：三合院与四合院，坐北朝南沿纵轴布置
  • 正房居北最高，长辈居住；厢房居两侧；倒座在南
  • 大门设在东南巽位，进门设影壁，先抑后扬
  • 胶东院落紧凑、天井如斗；鲁中依山就势成错层院
  • 鲁西鲁北院落开敞方正，多见两三进深宅
  • 正房以三间最普遍，明间堂屋设祖宗牌位
  • 五间正房或加耳房，称明三暗五、四破五
  • 硬山顶为主，草顶／石板顶／小青瓦顶分区明显

45. 胶东海草房营造技艺

The Building Craft of Jiaodong Seaweed-Roof Houses
秦汉起源，明清成熟，传承至今 国家级非物质文化遗产（省级名录·荣成市） 山东省省级非物质文化遗产代表性项目 威海荣成、文登，青岛崂山、即墨，烟台海阳、莱州等沿海地带

海草房是胶东半岛沿海最具标志性的民居形式，以海带草（当地称海苔草、海草）苫盖屋顶而得名，其营造技艺已列入山东省省级非物质文化遗产代表性项目名录，申报地区为荣成市。胶东民间流传着这样的歌谣：日照的墙，荣成的草，蓬莱的楼，黄县的房——其中「黄县的房」与「荣成的草」正是山东民居最常见的两句评语。

旧时地方志记载：邑中罕见瓦屋，砌以石，覆以茅、苫以海带，仅蔽风雨。可见海草房在历史上是沿海居民最普遍的住居形式。海草房的建造需经历七十多道工序，全部为手工操作，可划分为备料、垒墙、苫顶三大步骤，其中最关键的是往屋顶上苫海草，当地人称为苫房。

备料阶段，匠人需在冬季风浪过后到海边收集被海浪推上岸的大叶海苔草，晒干后捆成小把；石料则就地开采花岗岩，加工成不规则的块石，保留山石的粗糙质地。垒墙阶段，墙体多用不规则石块干砌或以黄泥、石灰浆坐浆砌筑，厚度常在五十厘米以上，下宽上窄，收分明显，转角处用较大的「把角石」咬合，门窗洞口以长条石做过梁，俗称「挑檐石」。

苫顶是整个营造过程中技术含量最高的环节。屋顶由横向大梁与纵向檩条（俗称腰杆子）纵横交叉成方格骨架，再铺设高粱秆扎成的笆子，一边抹黄泥一边按压到檩条上；

笆子晾干后开始苫草，从屋檐起自下而上一排压一排，每束海草都用黄泥压住根部，层层叠压，最后在屋脊处用白灰与黄泥调成的浆抹平，俗称压脊。苫好的屋顶坡度极陡，一般在四十五度至六十度之间，这是为了迅速排泄雨水，而厚达半米以上的海草层则提供了优异的保温隔热性能，使室内冬暖夏凉。

海草中含有大量的盐分与胶质，具有防腐、防虫、阻燃的特性，一座精心苫作的屋顶可维持四五十年甚至上百年不腐不漏，远胜于一般的麦秸草顶，这是海草房能够成为生态民居典范的物质基础。苫顶匠人被尊称为苫匠，是胶东乡村中地位很高的手艺人，其技艺以师徒相授、口传心授的方式传承。

由于近年来近海海草资源减少、愿意从事苫作的年轻人稀少，海草房营造技艺一度面临传承危机；荣成市通过建立传承人工作室、举办苫房技艺大赛、对现存海草房实施抢救性修缮等措施，使这门技艺重新焕发生机。从生态角度看，海草房就地取材、可循环利用、建造过程零碳排放，被誉为中国生态民居的活标本。

— ENGLISH —
