院中银杏为宋仁宗景祐三年（1036年）祭祀东镇庙时所植，树冠覆盖面近一亩；庙内古槐相传为唐朝所植，已历经一千二百余年。东镇庙背倚凤凰岭、面临汶水，山青水秀、风景幽雅，是古代帝王将相、文人墨客朝拜祭祀沂山的第一站。

自民国初年失于管理，大部分建筑倒塌、碑碣损坏，至20世纪80年代仅存鼓楼、前后殿及少数房舍，且残破不堪；1985年维修碑碣，1993年维修鼓楼与后殿，在原建筑基址上重建了山门钟楼与前殿，并修建院墙，现庙已初现原貌。

— ENGLISH —

The Eastern Peak Temple stands at Jiulongkou on the eastern flank of Mount Yi in Linqu and is where successive emperors sacrificed to the god of Mount Yi.Ancient China recognised the Five Great Mountains and the Five Great Peaks: Mount Tai is honoured as first of the Five Great Mountains, and Mount Yi as first of the Five Great Peaks - the Eastern Peak Mount Yi, the Western Peak Mount Wu, the Southern Peak Mount Kuaiji, the Northern Peak Mount Yiwulü and the Central Peak Mount Huo - making it one of the most important components of the ancient system of sacrifices to mountains, peaks, seas and rivers.The sacrificial culture of Mount Yi reaches back to the age of Yan and Huang: the Yellow Emperor is said to have enfeoffed Mount Yi, Shun first established it as a great peak, and the Han emperor Wudi came in person and ordered ritual officers to sacrifice; the Tang, Song, Jin, Yuan, Ming and Qing repeatedly added titles and never let the sacrifices lapse, with sixteen emperors of ten dynasties ascending to sacrifice by imperial decree.The temple's chronology is clear: said to have been founded in the Western Han on the summit at the Jade Emperor Peak under the name Mount Tai Shrine; in 594 Emperor Wen of Sui decreed that the Eastern Peak Mount Yi be sacrificed to and a shrine established on the mountain, and the temple was moved to the mountainside beside the Fayun Temple as the Shrine of the God of the Eastern Peak Mount Yi, with sacrifices in spring and autumn; and in 962, the third year of the Jianlong era of the Song, it was moved again to the foot of the mountain, its present site.Founded in the Song, extended and rebuilt repeatedly under the Yuan, Ming and Qing, the temple grew grand and imposing; by the late Qing the precinct measured one hundred and eighty metres north to south and two hundred and ten metres east to west, a total area of 37,800 square metres of which 3,900 were built over.It was originally in three courts, east, central and west, with the principal buildings in the central one: in front an arched archway gatehouse, the central opening taller and carrying a stone tablet inscribed Eastern Peak Temple, the flanking openings slightly lower, and two general's halls east and west of the gate; behind them a bell tower to the east and a drum tower to the west, both in pavilion form; further in, the Imperial Incense Pavilion, built in the Chenghua era of the Ming; and behind it the main hall, seven bays wide and three deep with a nine-ridge hip-gable roof in green glazed tiles, its eaves flying outward over carved and painted beams.Before it is a sacrificial terrace of locked railing panels with more than ten steps on the sides and front; north of the hall are the Robing Hall and, last, the Resting Hall, built in the Chenghua era, each three bays with a gable roof in grey tiles; east of the Robing Hall is the Scripture Tower.The greatest value of the temple lies in its forest of steles.An incomplete count of 1904 recorded more than three hundred and sixty steles; one hundred and forty-five survive, including four imperial steles - the Yuan Dade stele, the Ming Hongwu stele, the Kangxi stele Where Numinous Breath Gathers and the Qianlong stele Companion Peak of the Great East - together with sixty-seven imperial prayer tablets.It is the temple with the largest surviving collection of steles from the sacrifices to mountains, peaks, seas and rivers, more than thirty more than the Dai Temple at Mount Tai, and is an important body of documents for the study of imperial sacrificial ritual and of the political, economic and cultural life of successive periods.The temple also has notable ancient trees: two cypresses in front, said to have been planted in the Han and now over a thousand years old; a ginkgo in the court planted in 1036 when sacrifice was offered, with a crown covering nearly one mu; and a locust said to date from the Tang, now more than twelve hundred years old.Backed by Phoenix Ridge and facing the Wen River, in beautiful and secluded scenery, the temple was the first stop for emperors, ministers and literati who came to worship at Mount Yi.Neglected from the early Republic, most of its buildings collapsed and its steles were damaged, so that by the 1980s only the drum tower, the front and rear halls and a few rooms remained, all dilapidated; the steles were repaired in 1985, the drum tower and rear hall in 1993, and the gate, bell tower and front hall were rebuilt on their original foundations together with the precinct wall, so that the temple now begins to show its former appearance.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 沂山为五镇之首，十朝十六位帝王登封诏祭
  • 西汉太初创泰山祠，隋开皇十四年立祠，宋建隆三年迁今址
  • 清末占地三万七千八百平方米，分东中西三院
  • 正殿七间三进，九脊歇山绿琉璃瓦顶
  • 现存碑碣一百四十五幢，含御碑四幢、祝文碑六十七幢
  • 岳镇海渎祭祀存碑最多，比泰山岱庙多三十余幢
  • 汉柏两株、宋银杏一株、唐槐一株，古树名木众多
  • 1985年起陆续维修，1993年重建山门钟楼前殿

78. 山东家族祠堂建筑

Ancestral Hall Architecture in Shandong
明清鼎盛 宗祠建筑 乡土礼制建筑 山东省全域，以鲁中、胶东、鲁西南宗族聚居村落最为集中

家族祠堂是宗法制度在乡土社会中的物质载体，是村落中等级最高、体量最大、位置最显要的公共建筑。山东是宗族观念浓厚的地区，凡是聚族而居的大姓村落必设祠堂，形成「村必有祠、族必有谱、祭必有田」的格局，新城王氏忠勤祠、邹城孟府家庙、丁氏故宅的堂号都是其中的代表。

祠堂的选址极为讲究，通常位于村落的核心位置——或居于村中高地，或位于主要街巷的中段，或正对村口，其朝向多与村落的风水格局相配合，务求「藏风聚气、坐镇一村」。鲁中与胶东的山区村落，祠堂往往建在村落的最高处，从村外远远就能望见，既是宗族凝聚力的象征，也是村落空间秩序的统率。平面布局上，山东祠堂多为三进或两进的四合院。

第一进为门厅（或称「门楼」「头门」），两侧或设塾房，是族人聚会、议事之处；第二进为享堂（或称「拜殿」「正厅」），是祠堂的主体，面阔三间或五间，前有廊或月台，是举行祭祀仪式、宣读族规、处理族务的场所；第三进为寝殿（或称「后寝」「寝室」），供奉历代祖先牌位，是祠堂中最神圣的空间，牌位按昭穆制度排列，始祖居中，二世、四世、六世居左（昭），三世、五世、七世居右（穆）。

两厢则用作存放祭器、族谱、义仓或作为族学。在建筑形制上，祠堂是村落中唯一可以使用较高等级做法的建筑：屋顶可用硬山顶或悬山顶，有的甚至用歇山顶；可用斗栱、彩画、脊兽（数量仍受限制）；

门楼可做三开间的屋宇式大门，设门当户对，门前设石狮、旗杆石、上马石，以示族中有人功名在身；墙体磨砖对缝，墀头、影壁砖雕精细。这些做法在普通民居中是不允许的，唯有祠堂作为「礼」的空间，获得了有限度的等级豁免。祠堂的功能远不止祭祀。

它是族权的象征与行使场所——族长在此主持祭祀、裁决纠纷、执行族规、分配义田收益、兴办族学、赈济族中贫弱；它也是村落的文化中心——年节时在此演戏、聚宴、放灯，平日里则作为族人婚丧嫁娶的公共空间；在战乱时期，祠堂还常常充当村落的议事与防御指挥中心。山东祠堂建筑的地域特色鲜明：鲁中山区的祠堂用当地石材砌筑，墙体厚重、装饰简朴，与石砌民居浑然一体；

胶东的祠堂则多用青砖小瓦，做工精细，屋顶举折平缓、出檐深远；鲁西南的祠堂规模较小，多用土坯或夯土，但祭祀仪式之隆重不减。近代以来，随着宗族制度的瓦解，大量祠堂被改作学校、仓库、村公所或废弃，保存至今且格局完整者已不多见，因此对现存祠堂的调查、记录与保护显得尤为紧迫。

— ENGLISH —

The family ancestral hall is the material vehicle of the lineage system in rural society and the highest-ranking, largest and most prominent public building in a village.Shandong is a region of strong lineage consciousness, and wherever a great clan settled together there had to be an ancestral hall, producing the pattern that every village has its hall, every lineage its genealogy and every sacrifice its endowment land; the Zhongqin Hall of the Wang family of Xincheng, the family temple of the Mencius Mansion and the hall names of the Ding Family Residence are representative examples.Siting is a matter of great care: the hall normally occupies the core of the village - on high ground at the centre, midway along the main lane, or facing the village entrance - and its orientation is usually coordinated with the geomantic scheme so as to store wind and gather qi and to command the settlement.In the mountain villages of central Shandong and Jiaodong the hall is often built at the highest point, visible from far off, at once a symbol of lineage cohesion and the commander of village spatial order.In plan, Shandong ancestral halls are usually two- or three-court siheyuan.The first court is the gate hall, sometimes with side rooms, where clansmen gather and discuss affairs; the second is the offering hall, the main body of the complex, three or five bays wide with a veranda or moon terrace in front, where sacrifices are performed, lineage rules read out and clan business transacted; the third is the rear chamber, the most sacred space, housing the tablets of ancestors through the generations.Tablets are arranged by the system of zhao and mu: the founding ancestor in the centre, the second, fourth and sixth generations to the left as zhao, and the third, fifth and seventh to the right as mu.The side ranges hold ritual vessels, genealogies and charitable granaries, or serve as a lineage school.In building form, the ancestral hall is the only building in a village permitted to use the higher-grade treatments: gable or overhanging gable roofs, sometimes even hip-gable; dougong, polychrome painting and ridge beasts, though the number remains limited; a three-bay free-standing gate with gate blocks and gate pegs, and stone lions, flagpole stones and mounting blocks before it to show that members of the lineage hold degrees; walls of rubbed brick with fine brick carving on chitou piers and screen walls.All these are forbidden in ordinary dwellings, and only the ancestral hall, as a space of ritual, obtains a limited exemption from the regulations.Its functions go far beyond sacrifice.It is the symbol of lineage authority and the place where that authority is exercised: the lineage head presides over sacrifices, adjudicates disputes, enforces the rules, distributes the income of charitable land, runs the lineage school and relieves the poor; it is also the cultural centre of the village, where opera is performed, feasts held and lanterns lit at the new year, and which serves as public space for weddings and funerals; in time of war it often becomes the centre for village deliberation and the command of defence.Regional character is marked: in the central Shandong mountains halls are built of local stone with thick walls and simple ornament, at one with the stone dwellings around them; in Jiaodong they are of grey brick and small tiles, finely made with gentle roof pitches and deep eaves; in south-western Shandong they are smaller and of adobe or rammed earth, though the ceremonies performed in them are no less solemn.Since modern times, with the dissolution of the lineage system, great numbers of halls have been converted into schools, storehouses or village offices, or abandoned; few survive with their layout intact, and the survey, recording and protection of those that remain is therefore urgently needed.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 村必有祠、族必有谱、祭必有田的宗族格局
  • 选址居村落核心或高地，藏风聚气、坐镇一村
  • 三进格局：门厅—享堂—寝殿，两厢存祭器族谱义仓
  • 牌位按昭穆制：始祖居中，二四六世左昭，三五七世右穆
  • 祠堂是村落中唯一可用较高等级做法的建筑
  • 门前石狮、旗杆石、上马石以示族中有人功名在身
  • 功能远超祭祀：族权行使、文化中心、战时指挥
  • 地域差异：鲁中石构、胶东青砖小瓦、鲁西南土坯夯土

79. 山东民间信仰建筑与泰山石敢当

Folk Belief Architecture and the Mount Tai Stone Dare-to-Resist of Shandong
汉代起源，明清鼎盛，延续至今 民间信仰建筑 山东省省级非物质文化遗产（泰山石敢当习俗） 山东省全域，以泰山周边、胶东沿海、运河沿线最为盛行

在国家祀典与宗族祠堂之外，山东还存在着一个庞大而活跃的民间信仰建筑体系，它与民众的日常生活息息相关，构成了乡土社会精神世界最贴近地面的那一层。山东的民间信仰建筑主要有以下几类。一是海洋信仰建筑，以天后宫（妈祖庙）与龙王庙为主，分布于胶东沿海与运河沿线。

青岛天后宫始建于明成化三年（1467年），是青岛市区现存最古老的建筑，历经明清多次重修，供奉妈祖、龙王与财神；每年农历新年举办的「天后宫新正民俗文化庙会」已被列入山东省省级非物质文化遗产名录。烟台福建会馆（天后行宫）则是北方唯一独具闽南风格的天后宫建筑，是全国仅存的两座最高等级「天后行宫」之一。二是山神与土地信仰建筑，以泰山石敢当为核心。

泰山石敢当是泰山信仰最具代表性的民俗符号，其习俗源于古代的灵石崇拜，西汉史游的《急就章》中已有「师猛虎，石敢当，所不侵，龙未央」的记载，唐代以后与泰山信仰结合，形成了「泰山石敢当」的完整体系。其做法是在宅基、巷口、桥头、河岸等被认为有「冲煞」之处，立一块刻有「泰山石敢当」或「石敢当」五字的石碑或石条，用以镇宅辟邪、保境安民。

石敢当的尺寸、形制、刻法、立石时间与仪式都有讲究，有的还刻有狮头、虎头、太极八卦等图案，是山东最具辨识度的乡土文化符号之一，其习俗已列入山东省省级非物质文化遗产名录。

三是行业神与先贤信仰建筑，如鲁班庙（曲阜、滕州，鲁班传说已列入山东省省级非物质文化遗产名录）、窑神庙（淄博博山，供奉陶瓷与琉璃行业的保护神）、盐神庙（潍坊寒亭，寒亭盐神节为市级非物质文化遗产）、关帝庙（遍布全省，以聊城山陕会馆的关帝大殿最为宏丽）、药王庙、财神庙、文昌阁、魁星楼等，这些建筑反映了各行业与阶层从业者的精神诉求。

四是道教宫观建筑，以崂山太清宫、蓬莱阁、泰山岱庙与碧霞祠为代表，崂山是中国道教发源地之一，太清宫始建于西汉建元元年（公元前140年），是崂山规模最大、历史最悠久的道观，崂山道教音乐已列入山东省省级非物质文化遗产名录。

民间信仰建筑在形制上有共同特点：规模不大（通常一至三间），但位置显要（多在村口、街巷交汇处、桥头等高可达性之处）；形制自由，不受官式制度约束，屋顶、开间、装饰因地制宜，富有地方色彩；使用频率高，是村民日常祭祀、聚会、纳凉、议事的场所，建筑与日常生活的关系远比高等级的寺观庙宇密切。这些建筑虽然单体价值不高，但作为乡土文化景观的有机组成部分，与村落格局、街巷肌理、生活方式融为一体，是理解山东乡村社会不可缺失的一环。

— ENGLISH —

Beyond the state sacrificial canon and the lineage hall, Shandong possesses a vast and active system of folk belief architecture, closely bound to everyday life and forming the layer of the rural spiritual world nearest the ground.It falls into several classes.

The first is marine belief, chiefly the Tianhou or Mazu palaces and the Dragon King temples, distributed along the Jiaodong coast and the canal.The Qingdao Tianhou Palace, first built in 1467, is the oldest surviving building in the urban area of Qingdao; repeatedly rebuilt under the Ming and Qing, it enshrines Mazu, the Dragon King and the god of wealth, and its New Year folk temple fair has been inscribed on the Shandong provincial intangible cultural heritage list.The Yantai Fujian Guild Hall, or Tianhou travelling palace, is the only Tianhou palace in the north built in the Fujian style and one of only two surviving travelling palaces of the highest rank.The second class is belief in mountain gods and the earth, centred on the Mount Tai stone dare-to-resist.This is the most representative folk symbol of Mount Tai belief; the custom derives from ancient stone worship, and the Han work Ji Jiu Zhang already records the line the master awes the fierce tiger, the stone dares to resist, nothing invades, the dragon is unending; from the Tang it combined with Mount Tai belief to form the complete system of the Mount Tai stone dare-to-resist.The practice is to erect at the base of a house, at a lane mouth, at a bridgehead or on a river bank - wherever a harmful alignment is thought to strike - a stone tablet or bar carved with the characters Mount Tai Stone Dares to Resist, or simply Stone Dares to Resist, in order to guard the dwelling, avert evil and keep the locality at peace.The dimensions, form, engraving, timing and ceremony of erection all follow convention, and some stones are additionally carved with lion or tiger heads or the eight trigrams; it is one of the most recognisable symbols of Shandong local culture and the custom has been inscribed on the provincial intangible cultural heritage list.The third class comprises temples of trade gods and worthies: the Lu Ban temple at Qufu and Tengzhou, the legend of Lu Ban being on the provincial list; the Kiln God temple at Boshan in Zibo, protecting the ceramic and glass trades; the Salt God temple at Hanting in Weifang, whose salt god festival is a municipal heritage item; the Lord Guan temples found throughout the province, of which the finest is the great hall of the Shanshan Guild Hall in Liaocheng; and the medicine king, wealth god, Wenchang and Kuixing temples, all expressing the spiritual concerns of particular trades and classes.The fourth class is Daoist temple architecture, represented by the Taiqing Palace on Mount Lao, the Penglai Pavilion, the Dai Temple at Mount Tai and the Bixia Temple; Mount Lao is one of the birthplaces of Chinese Daoism, and the Taiqing Palace, founded in 140 BC, is its largest and oldest monastery, while the Daoist music of Mount Lao is on the provincial heritage list.Folk belief buildings share certain characteristics: small in scale, usually one to three rooms, but prominent in position, at the village entrance, at lane junctions or at bridgeheads where they are easily reached; free in form, unconstrained by official regulation, with roofs, bays and ornament adapted to local conditions and rich in regional colour; and heavily used, as places of daily sacrifice, gathering, cooling off in summer and deliberation, so that the relation of building to daily life is far closer than in the great temples and monasteries.Individually their value may not be high, but as an organic part of the vernacular cultural landscape - woven into the village plan, the lane texture and the way of life - they are an indispensable element in understanding rural society in Shandong.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 海洋信仰：青岛天后宫（1467）为市区最古老建筑
  • 天后宫新正民俗文化庙会列入山东省省级非遗
  • 泰山石敢当源出汉代《急就章》，唐代与泰山信仰结合
  • 立于宅基巷口桥头，刻泰山石敢当五字镇宅辟邪
  • 石敢当习俗列入山东省省级非物质文化遗产
  • 行业神：鲁班庙、窑神庙、盐神庙、关帝庙遍布全省
  • 崂山太清宫始建于西汉建元元年，崂山道教音乐为省级非遗
  • 民间庙宇规模小、位置显要、形制自由、使用频率高

第七章 寺观塔幢与楼阁

Chapter 7 Monasteries, Pagodas and Towers

80. 山东寺观塔幢与楼阁概述

An Overview of Monasteries, Pagodas and Towers in Shandong
东汉传入，北朝兴起，隋唐鼎盛，宋元明清延续 宗教建筑总论 总论 山东省全域，以济南、青州、泰安、烟台、聊城最为集中

山东的宗教建筑传统悠长：佛教自东汉传入，东晋时期高僧朗公在泰山北麓琨瑞山建立佛教道场，是山东佛教建筑史的开端；道教则因崂山、泰山、沂山的修仙传统而兴盛，崂山更是中国道教的发源地之一。历经北朝的石窟开凿、隋唐的寺院兴建、宋元的塔幢营造、明清的楼阁营建，山东留下了数量可观、类型完整的宗教建筑遗产。山东寺观塔幢与楼阁的价值首先在于其时代序列的完整性。

从北朝的驼山石窟（北周至盛唐）、隋代的四门塔（中国现存最早的石塔）、唐代的龙虎塔与千佛崖造像、宋代的辟支塔与罗汉彩塑、金元的尼山孔庙与颜庙、明代的光岳楼与蓬莱阁、清代的碧霞祠与灵岩寺重修，几乎每个朝代都有代表性实物留存，构成了一部可以触摸的建筑编年史。

其次是类型的多样性。

石窟寺（驼山、云门山、千佛崖）、地面寺院（灵岩寺、神通寺、兴国寺）、道观（崂山太清宫、蓬莱阁）、各类佛塔（楼阁式砖塔、亭阁式石塔、密檐塔、墓塔林）、经幢、石祠（孝堂山郭氏墓石祠）、楼阁（光岳楼、蓬莱阁、太白楼）、清真寺与异邦王陵（苏禄王墓），几乎涵盖了中国宗教建筑的所有类型。

第三是地域风格的鲜明性。山东的寺观塔幢在建筑技术上表现出与中原、江南既相通又相异的特征：相通之处在于遵循官式制度的基本框架——抬梁式木构架、斗栱铺作、举折制度、屋顶等级；

相异之处则在于地方做法的灵活运用——就地取材（青石、花岗岩、汉白玉）、结构简化（减少斗栱层数、简化梁架）、装饰地方化（鲁中的粗犷、胶东的精细、鲁西的朴拙），形成了「官式为骨、地方为肉」的独特品格。在选址上，山东的寺观塔幢体现出强烈的山水意识。

灵岩寺「深山藏古寺，流水伴钟鸣」，神通寺依山临涧、寺塔相依，崂山太清宫面海背山、藏而不露，蓬莱阁雄踞丹崖、俯瞰沧海，均是中国宗教建筑选址艺术的典范，体现了「天人合一」的宇宙观在建筑上的投射。

这些建筑与其所处的自然环境共同构成了完整的文化景观，是山东作为文化大省最直观的物质见证。

— ENGLISH —

Shandong has a long tradition of religious building: Buddhism entered in the Eastern Han, and in the Eastern Jin the monk Langgong founded a Buddhist community at Mount Kunrui on the northern flank of Mount Tai, beginning the history of Buddhist architecture in the province; Daoism flourished through the cultivation traditions of Mount Lao, Mount Tai and Mount Yi, Mount Lao being one of the birthplaces of Chinese Daoism.Through the cave-cutting of the Northern Dynasties, the temple building of the Sui and Tang, the pagoda construction of the Song and Yuan and the tower building of the Ming and Qing, Shandong has preserved a considerable and typologically complete heritage of religious architecture.Its first value lies in the completeness of its chronological sequence: from the Northern Zhou to High Tang caves of Mount Tuo, the Sui Four-Door Pagoda - the earliest surviving stone pagoda in China - the Tang Dragon-Tiger Pagoda and Thousand-Buddha Cliff images, the Song Pizhi Pagoda and painted luohan, the Jin and Yuan Nishan and Yan temples, the Ming Guangyue Tower and Penglai Pavilion, and the Qing Bixia Temple and the rebuilding of Lingyan Temple, representative works survive from almost every dynasty, forming a chronicle of architecture that can be touched.The second value is typological variety: cave temples at Mount Tuo, Mount Yunmen and the Thousand-Buddha Cliff; surface monasteries such as Lingyan, Shentong and Xingguo; Daoist temples such as the Taiqing Palace on Mount Lao and the Penglai Pavilion; pagodas of every kind - storeyed brick pagodas, pavilion-type stone pagodas, closely-eaved pagodas and forests of memorial pagodas; sutra pillars; stone offering shrines such as the Guo family tomb shrine at Xiaotangshan; towers such as the Guangyue Tower, the Penglai Pavilion and the Taibai Tower; and mosques and foreign royal tombs such as that of the King of Sulu - covering virtually every type of Chinese religious architecture.The third value is the distinctness of regional style.In technique, the temples and pagodas of Shandong show features both shared with and distinct from those of the Central Plains and the south.What is shared is adherence to the basic framework of the official system - the post-and-lintel timber frame, dougong sets, the rules of roof pitch, and roof grades.What differs is the flexible use of local practice: the use of local materials such as bluestone, granite and white marble; the simplification of structure by reducing the number of dougong tiers and simplifying the frame; and the localisation of ornament - rugged in central Shandong, refined in Jiaodong, plain in western Shandong - producing the distinctive quality of official system as the bones and local practice as the flesh.In siting, these buildings show a strong feeling for landscape: Lingyan Temple is a case of an ancient temple hidden deep in the mountains with a stream accompanying the bell; Shentong Temple leans against the hill above a ravine with pagoda and temple depending on each other; the Taiqing Palace on Mount Lao faces the sea with the mountain behind it, concealed rather than exposed; and the Penglai Pavilion stands on red cliffs looking down on the ocean.All are models of the Chinese art of siting religious buildings and express the cosmology of the unity of heaven and man projected into architecture; together with their natural surroundings they form complete cultural landscapes, the most direct material witness of Shandong as a province of culture.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 东晋朗公开山东佛教道场，崂山为道教发源地之一
  • 时代序列完整：北朝石窟至清代楼阁各代实物俱存
  • 类型多样：石窟、寺院、道观、佛塔、经幢、石祠、楼阁、王陵
  • 官式为骨、地方为肉：遵循制度框架而灵活地方化
  • 就地取材：青石、花岗岩、汉白玉
  • 灵岩寺深山藏古寺，流水伴钟鸣
  • 崂山太清宫面海背山，藏而不露
  • 宗教建筑与自然环境共同构成完整文化景观

81. 济南神通寺、四门塔与龙虎塔

Shentong Temple, the Four-Door Pagoda and the Dragon-Tiger Pagoda in Jinan
四门塔隋大业七年（611）建 寺院与石塔 全国重点文物保护单位（1961年第一批） 济南市历城区柳埠镇，泰山北麓琨瑞山之阳

神通寺位于济南历城区柳埠镇泰山北麓的琨瑞山之阳，是山东现存最早的佛教寺院遗址之一。东晋时期，高僧朗公（佛图澄的弟子）来到琨瑞山建立佛教道场，与在此隐居的隐士张忠过从甚密，传说朗公讲到绝妙之时，群山为之感动、岩石点头称道，灵岩由此而得名；

此后朗公在灵岩山「别立精舍数十余区」，又在此创建了神通寺（初名朗公寺，隋开皇三年改名神通寺），是山东佛教建筑的源头。

四门塔位于神通寺遗址东侧，建于隋大业七年（611年），是中国现存最早的单层石塔，也是中国现存最早的亭阁式石塔，1961年被国务院公布为第一批全国重点文物保护单位。

塔为单层方形，全部用当地出产的大块青石砌筑，高十五点零四米，每边宽七点三八米，四面各辟一个半圆拱券门，故称四门塔。塔内中心有一根方形石柱，柱上四面各置一尊石雕佛像，佛像神情端庄、衣纹流畅，是隋代雕刻艺术的精品。

塔身上部用石块层层叠涩出挑，形成挑檐，顶部为四角攒尖的塔顶，上置方形须弥座式的塔刹，整个塔形体简洁、比例匀称、轮廓挺拔，是中国早期石塔建筑的杰出代表，也是研究中国佛塔由印度窣堵坡向中国楼阁式塔演变的关键实例。

龙虎塔位于神通寺遗址内、四门塔西北，因塔身雕刻有龙、虎而得名，建于唐代，是唐代石塔的代表作。

龙虎塔为方形单层亭阁式石塔，高约十点八米，下部为高大的石砌塔基与塔身，塔身四面辟门，门侧浮雕龙、虎、力士、飞天、伎乐等形象，刀法圆润、造型生动、层次丰富，上部为砖砌的塔檐与塔顶，形成了「石身砖顶、刚柔并济」的独特效果，是研究唐代石雕艺术与砖石混合结构建筑的重要实例。

此外，神通寺遗址内还有千佛崖造像与墓塔林。千佛崖位于寺址西侧的白虎山崖壁上，有唐代大小窟龛一百余个、造像二百余尊，造像题记中有唐武德、贞观、显庆、永淳、文明等年号，是研究唐代造像艺术与纪年的重要资料。

神通寺墓塔林（又称「祖师林」）位于寺址西北，现存宋元明清各代墓塔四十余座，形制有亭阁式、密檐式、经幢式、喇嘛塔式等多种，是山东现存规模较大的墓塔林之一，与灵岩寺墓塔林并称为山东墓塔艺术的两大宝库。

— ENGLISH —

Shentong Temple stands on the southern slope of Mount Kunrui on the northern flank of Mount Tai at Liubu in Licheng District, Jinan, and is one of the earliest surviving Buddhist monastic sites in Shandong.In the Eastern Jin the monk Langgong, a disciple of Fotucheng, came to Mount Kunrui and founded a Buddhist community, becoming close to the hermit Zhang Zhong who lived there; it is said that when Langgong reached the subtlest point of his exposition the hills were moved and the rocks nodded in assent, from which the name Lingyan, Numinous Rock, derives.Langgong later established several dozen retreats on Mount Lingyan and founded Shentong Temple here, originally called Langgong Temple and renamed Shentong in 583 - the fountainhead of Buddhist architecture in Shandong.The Four-Door Pagoda stands east of the temple site; built in 611, the seventh year of the Daye era of the Sui, it is the earliest surviving single-storey stone pagoda in China and the earliest surviving pavilion-type stone pagoda, and was listed by the State Council in 1961 in the first batch of National Key Cultural Heritage Sites.It is a single-storey square structure built entirely of large local bluestone blocks, 15.04 metres high and 7.38 metres on each side, with a semicircular arched opening on each of the four faces, whence its name.At the centre inside stands a square stone pillar carrying a carved stone image of the Buddha on each side, the figures dignified in expression and fluid in drapery, fine examples of Sui carving.The upper part of the body corbels out in successive courses of stone to form the eaves, and the top is a four-sided pyramidal roof carrying a square pedestal-shaped finial; simple in mass, well proportioned and upright in silhouette, the pagoda is an outstanding representative of early Chinese stone pagoda architecture and a key example for studying the evolution of the Chinese pagoda from the Indian stupa to the storeyed tower.The Dragon-Tiger Pagoda stands north-west of the Four-Door Pagoda within the temple site and takes its name from the carved dragons and tigers on its body; built in the Tang, it is a representative work of Tang stone pagodas.A square single-storey pavilion-type stone pagoda about 10.8 metres high, it has a tall stone base and body with an opening on each face, beside which dragons, tigers, guardian figures, flying apsaras and musicians are carved in relief with rounded technique, lively forms and rich layering; the eaves and roof above are of brick, producing the distinctive effect of a stone body with a brick crown, hardness and softness combined.It is an important example for the study of Tang stone carving and of mixed brick-and-stone construction.The site also contains the Thousand-Buddha Cliff and a forest of memorial pagodas.The cliff, on the face of White Tiger Hill west of the temple, holds more than a hundred Tang niches and over two hundred images; inscriptions bear the Tang era names Wude, Zhenguan, Xianqing, Yongchun and Wenming, making it important material for the study of Tang sculpture and chronology.The pagoda forest, also called the Forest of Patriarchs, lies north-west of the site and preserves more than forty memorial pagodas of the Song, Yuan, Ming and Qing in pavilion, closely-eaved, sutra-pillar and lamaist forms; it is one of the larger pagoda forests in Shandong and, with that of Lingyan Temple, one of the two great treasuries of memorial pagoda art in the province.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 东晋朗公创建，初名朗公寺，隋开皇三年改名神通寺
  • 四门塔建于隋大业七年，中国现存最早的单层石塔
  • 塔高十五点零四米，边长七点三八米，四面各辟券门
  • 塔内方柱四面各置隋代石雕佛像
  • 上部石块叠涩出挑成檐，四角攒尖顶置须弥座塔刹
  • 龙虎塔唐代建，石身砖顶，雕龙虎力士飞天伎乐
  • 千佛崖唐代窟龛百余、造像二百余尊，有明确纪年
  • 神通寺墓塔林存宋元明清墓塔四十余座

82. 济南灵岩寺

Lingyan Temple in Jinan
东晋始建，现存建筑为宋明清遗构 佛教寺院 全国重点文物保护单位（1982年第二批）/ 世界文化与自然双重遗产（泰山组成部分） 济南市长清区万德街道，泰山北麓灵岩峪方山之阳

灵岩寺位于济南长清区万德街道、泰山北麓灵岩峪方山之阳，始建于东晋，由高僧佛图澄的徒弟僧朗主持建造，距今已有一千六百多年的历史。自唐代起与浙江国清寺、南京栖霞寺、湖北玉泉寺并称「海内四大名刹」，并名列其首；现为世界自然与文化双遗产泰山的重要组成部分、全国重点文物保护单位、国家级风景名胜区。

古人云：游泰山而不至灵岩不成游也；明代文学家王世贞更有「灵岩是泰山背最幽绝处」之叹。灵岩寺旅游区群山环抱、岩幽壁峭、柏檀叠秀、泉甘茶香，寺院依山取势，共有殿宇三十六处、亭阁十八座。

千佛殿是寺内主体建筑，位于摩顶松之北，始建于唐贞观年间（627—649年），宋嘉祐与明嘉靖、万历年间重修，现存木结构为明代建筑。

千佛殿依山建于高大的台基之上，面阔七间、进深四间，单檐庑殿顶，举折平缓、出檐深远，檐下置疏朗宏大的斗栱，木棱彩绘华丽，檐角长伸高耸，有展翅欲飞之势。前檐下立有八根石柱，柱础皆雕刻有龙、凤、花、叶、水波及莲瓣、宝装荷花等纹样，雕工精美，凸显唐宋之风。

殿内正中塑有通体贴金的三身佛：中为法身毗卢遮那佛，由藤胎髹漆塑造，宋治平二年（1065年）从钱塘运至灵岩；东侧为报身卢舍那佛，明成化十三年（1477年）用两千五百公斤铜铸成；

西为应身释迦牟尼佛，亦为铜质，明嘉靖二十三年（1544年）铸造。千佛殿最受称道的是四十尊彩色泥塑罗汉像，其中三十二尊塑于宋治平三年（1066年），八尊补塑于明万历年间。这些塑像皆坐于八十厘米高的砖砌束腰座上，顶距座面高度在一百零五至一百一十厘米之间。

古代艺术家打破传统的佛教造像模式，侧重于写实，以形写神、以神表情、以情现心，重在体现每尊罗汉的个性与特点，刻画其内心世界。观其形态，或端恭、或柱杖、或合掌、或趺坐，或口讲手指、或侧耳细听，无不准确生动；察其神情，有的勇猛愠怒，有的和善老成，有的据理力争，有的闭眸沉思，有的笑容可掬，有的俯首低吟，有的纵目远眺，无不细致入微。

1982年维修时还发现，这些彩塑罗汉像人体一样有腹腔，腹腔内有用丝绸做的五脏六腑，此外还有五铢钱、开元通宝和宋代前三朝的铜币及宋代铜镜、墨书题记等文物。

1912年梁启超来此游览，赞誉千佛殿泥塑罗汉像为「海内第一名塑」；刘海粟题词：灵岩名塑，天下第一，有血有肉，活灵活现。

辟支塔是灵岩寺的主要标志，始建于宋淳化五年（994年），竣工于嘉祐二年（1057年），历时六十三年完工。

塔为一座八角九层十二檐的楼阁式砖塔，下三层为重檐、上六层为单檐，塔高五十五点七米，塔基为石筑八角，上有浮雕，镌刻古印度孔雀王朝阿育王皈依佛门等故事。塔身为青砖砌就，各层皆施腰檐，二至四层檐下置平座；塔檐与塔径自下而上逐层递减，收分得体。塔内一至四层设塔心柱，内辟券洞、砌有台阶，可拾级而上；自第五层以上砌为实体，登塔须沿塔壁外腰檐左转九十度进入上层门洞。

塔刹由覆钵露盘、相轮、宝盖、圆光、仰月、宝珠组成，自宝盖下垂八根铁链，由第九层塔檐角上的八尊铁质金刚承接，在塔内延续到地下，起避雷作用。宋代文学家曾巩有诗赞曰：法定禅房临峭谷，辟支灵塔冠层峦。

墓塔林是灵岩寺历代高僧的墓地，现有北魏、唐、宋、金、元、明、清历代石质墓塔一百六十七座，墓志铭、石碑八十一通，是中国最大的石砌塔林，1982年即被冠以「露天石刻艺术博物馆」的殊荣。墓塔一般由塔座、塔身、塔刹组成，塔座呈方形、圆形、八角形，有浮雕装饰；塔身较高大，上刻僧人法名年号；

塔刹则有相轮、覆盆、仰月、宝珠、花卉、龙图等图案造型。塔林中部为一南北甬道，北端建有砖石结构、单层重檐式北魏祖师塔（法定墓塔），甬道两侧列峙全石结构群塔，依塔身形制分为方碑形塔、钟形塔、鼓形塔、窣堵婆塔（喇嘛塔）、经幢式塔、亭阁式塔六种。墓塔林不仅是数量上的第一，更是一部刻在石头上的中国建筑简史。

灵岩寺内名泉众多：卓锡泉、白鹤泉、双鹤泉三泉相临，俗称「五步三泉」，泉水从石涧中冒出，甘冽终年不竭，注入石池宛如明镜，即著名的「镜池春晓」；袈裟泉因泉旁立一形似袈裟的铸铁块而得名，为济南七十二名泉之一；此外还有甘露泉、檀抱泉、飞泉等，卓锡泉、袈裟泉、檀抱泉均列入济南七十二名泉。

— ENGLISH —

Lingyan Temple stands on the southern slope of Fang Mountain in the Lingyan valley on the northern flank of Mount Tai at Wande in Changqing District, Jinan; founded in the Eastern Jin under the monk Senglang, a disciple of Fotucheng, it is more than sixteen hundred years old.From the Tang it was counted with the Guoqing Temple in Zhejiang, the Qixia Temple in Nanjing and the Yuquan Temple in Hubei as the four great monasteries within the seas, and placed first among them; it is now an important part of the Mount Tai World Cultural and Natural Heritage, a National Key Cultural Heritage Site and a national scenic area.The ancients said that a visit to Mount Tai without Lingyan is no visit at all, and the Ming writer Wang Shizhen sighed that Lingyan is the most secluded and exquisite place behind Mount Tai.Ringed by hills with steep cliffs, tiered cypresses and sandalwoods, sweet springs and fragrant tea, the monastery follows the mountain and comprises thirty-six halls and eighteen pavilions.The Thousand-Buddha Hall is the principal building, north of the Moding pine; founded in the Zhenguan era of the Tang (627-649), rebuilt in the Jiayou era of the Song and the Jiajing and Wanli eras of the Ming, its surviving timber structure is Ming.Built on a tall terrace against the hill, it is seven bays wide and four deep with a single-eave hip roof, gentle pitch and deep eaves; below the eaves are large, widely spaced dougong, the timber members richly painted, and the eave corners stretch long and high as if about to take flight.Eight stone columns stand below the front eaves, their bases carved with dragons, phoenixes, flowers, leaves, water ripples, lotus petals and jewelled lotus - exquisite work that reveals the Tang and Song manner.Inside are three gilded images of the three bodies of the Buddha: in the centre the dharma body, Vairocana, made of lacquer over a rattan core and brought from Qiantang in 1065; to the east the reward body, Rocana, cast in 1477 from two thousand five hundred kilograms of bronze; and to the west the response body, Sakyamuni, also in bronze, cast in 1544.What the hall is most praised for is the forty painted clay luohan, thirty-two of them modelled in 1066 and eight added in the Wanli era.They sit on brick waisted plinths eighty centimetres high, the figures between 105 and 110 centimetres from seat to crown.The ancient artists broke with the traditional mode of Buddhist image-making and emphasised realism, using form to convey spirit, spirit to convey expression and expression to reveal the mind, intent above all on the individuality of each luohan and on his inner world.In posture some are reverent, some lean on staves, some join their palms, some sit cross-legged, some speak and point, some listen with head inclined - all exact and lively; in expression some are fierce and angry, some kind and seasoned, some arguing their case, some sunk in thought with closed eyes, some wreathed in smiles, some chanting with lowered head, some gazing into the distance - all minutely observed.During repairs in 1982 it was discovered that these painted luohan have body cavities like human beings, containing silk-made internal organs, together with wushu coins, Kaiyuan tongbao and coins of the first three Song reigns, Song bronze mirrors and ink inscriptions.In 1912 Liang Qichao visited and praised them as the finest sculpture within the seas; Liu Haisu wrote that the famous sculpture of Lingyan is first under heaven, flesh and blood, vividly alive.The Pizhi Pagoda, the emblem of the temple, was begun in 994 and completed in 1057, taking sixty-three years.An octagonal nine-storey brick pagoda with twelve eaves - double eaves on the lowest three storeys and single above - it is 55.7 metres high on an octagonal stone base carved in relief with scenes such as the conversion of King Ashoka of the Maurya dynasty.The body is of grey brick with a cornice at every storey and balconies beneath the eaves of the second to fourth; cornices and diameters diminish storey by storey with graceful batter.Inside, the first four storeys have a central pillar with arched openings and steps allowing ascent; from the fifth storey upward the body is solid, so that climbing requires turning left ninety degrees along the external cornice to reach the doorway of the next storey.The finial consists of an inverted bowl, a dew plate, ring wheels, a canopy, a halo, an upturned moon and a precious pearl, with eight iron chains hanging from the canopy to eight iron guardians at the corners of the ninth storey and continuing down inside the pagoda to the ground, serving as lightning protection.The Song writer Zeng Gong wrote: the Dharma-filled meditation chamber faces the sheer valley, the Pizhi spirit pagoda crowns the layered peaks.The pagoda forest, the cemetery of the monastery's eminent monks, contains one hundred and sixty-seven stone memorial pagodas of the Northern Wei, Tang, Song, Jin, Yuan, Ming and Qing together with eighty-one epitaphs and steles; it is the largest stone pagoda forest in China and was honoured as early as 1982 as an open-air museum of stone carving.A pagoda generally consists of base, body and finial: the base square, round or octagonal with relief decoration; the body tall and carrying the monk's name and date; and the finial with ring wheels, an inverted basin, an upturned moon, a precious pearl, flowers and dragon motifs.A north-south passage runs through the middle of the forest, at its north end the single-storey double-eave brick and stone pagoda of the Northern Wei patriarch Fading, and along both sides rows of all-stone pagodas classified by body form into six types: stele-shaped, bell-shaped, drum-shaped, stupa or lamaist, sutra-pillar and pavilion-shaped.First in number, the forest is also a short history of Chinese architecture carved in stone.The monastery is rich in famous springs: the Zhuoxi, White Crane and Twin Crane springs lie close together and are known as three springs in five paces, their water rising from rock crevices, sweet and never failing, collecting in a stone pool like a mirror in the celebrated scene Spring Mirror at Dawn; the Jiasha, or Kasaya, Spring, named for a cast-iron block shaped like a monk's robe beside it, is one of the seventy-two famous springs of Jinan; and there are also the Sweet Dew, the Embracing Sandalwood and the Flying springs, the Zhuoxi, Jiasha and Embracing Sandalwood all being counted among the seventy-two.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 东晋始建，海内四大名刹之首，泰山双遗产组成
  • 千佛殿面阔七间进深四间，单檐庑殿，明代木构
  • 四十尊彩塑罗汉：三十二尊宋塑，八尊明补塑
  • 彩塑有腹腔，内有丝绸做的五脏六腑
  • 梁启超誉海内第一名塑，刘海粟题天下第一
  • 辟支塔宋淳化五年始建，历时六十三年，高五十五点七米
  • 八角九层十二檐楼阁式砖塔，铁链延续地下起避雷作用
  • 墓塔林一百六十七座为中国最大，分六种形制

83. 蓬莱阁与崂山太清宫

The Penglai Pavilion and the Taiqing Palace on Mount Lao
蓬莱阁北宋嘉祐六年（1061）建，太清宫西汉建元元年（前140）始建 道教楼阁与宫观 全国重点文物保护单位（1982年第二批、2013年第七批） 烟台市蓬莱区丹崖山巅（蓬莱阁）、青岛市崂山区太清湾畔（太清宫）

蓬莱阁位于烟台蓬莱区城北丹崖山巅，始建于北宋嘉祐六年（1061年），与黄鹤楼、岳阳楼、滕王阁并称为中国四大名楼，是山东现存规模最大的古代楼阁建筑群，1982年被国务院公布为第二批全国重点文物保护单位。

蓬莱阁并非单座楼阁，而是一组由六个单体建筑组成的建筑群，包括蓬莱阁（主阁）、天后宫、龙王宫、吕祖殿、三清殿、弥陀寺，总建筑面积一万八千九百余平方米，占地三点二八万平方米。

主阁为双层木结构楼阁，坐北朝南，建于丹崖山绝壁之上，高十五米，重檐歇山顶，底层长十四点八米、进深九点六五米，四面回廊，环以明廊，可供人凭栏远眺。阁上悬清代书法家铁保手书的「蓬莱阁」巨匾，阁内楹联、碑刻、诗文、石刻琳琅满目，其中「海市蜃楼皆幻影，忠臣孝子即神仙」的名联最为人称道。

蓬莱阁的价值首先在于其选址：丹崖山拔海而起，山势陡峭，阁踞山巅，北临大海，与长山列岛隔海相望，是观赏「海市蜃楼」的最佳位置，古人视此地为神仙居所，「八仙过海」的传说即源于此，「八仙过海传说」已列入山东省省级非物质文化遗产名录。

其次在于其军事价值：阁下即为蓬莱水城（备倭城），是中国现存最完整的古代军港之一，阁与水城构成了一个完整的海防体系，是研究明代海防建筑的重要实例。

崂山太清宫位于青岛崂山区太清湾畔，俗称下宫，始建于西汉建元元年（公元前140年），是崂山规模最大、历史最悠久的道观，2013年被国务院公布为第七批全国重点文物保护单位。

太清宫背依老君峰、面向太清湾，三面环山、一面朝海，是中国道教建筑「藏风聚气」选址理念的典范。太清宫由三官殿、三清殿、三皇殿三座主要院落组成，共有殿宇房舍一百五十余间，建筑面积两千五百余平方米，占地三万平方米。三官殿供奉天官、地官、水官（即尧、舜、禹）；三清殿供奉玉清元始天尊、上清灵宝天尊、太清道德天尊；

三皇殿供奉天皇、地皇、人皇（即伏羲、神农、黄帝）。建筑为硬山顶，青瓦灰墙，梁架简朴，不施斗栱与彩画，体现了道家「见素抱朴」的审美理想，与宏丽的皇家道观形成鲜明对比。

宫内有大量古树名木，其中汉柏、唐榆、宋银杏树龄千年以上，最为著名的是三官殿院内的「汉柏盘龙」，相传为张廉夫手植，已有两千余年树龄，古柏之上缠绕着一棵凌霄，树上又生出一株盐肤木，形成「三树合一」的奇观。太清宫外还有神水泉、龙头榆等名胜，宫前海边有「太清水月」的著名景观。

崂山是中国道教发源地之一，崂山道教音乐已列入山东省省级非物质文化遗产名录。蓬莱阁与崂山太清宫代表了山东道教建筑的两种范式：前者是「显」——踞山临海、雄视四方，以楼阁的形体与气势取胜；

后者是「隐」——背山面海、藏而不露，以院落的空间与环境的融合见长。一显一隐，共同诠释了中国道教建筑的选址智慧与空间理想。

— ENGLISH —

The Penglai Pavilion stands on the summit of Red Cliff Hill north of Penglai in Yantai; first built in 1061, it is counted with the Yellow Crane Tower, the Yueyang Tower and the Pavilion of Prince Teng as one of the four great towers of China, and is the largest surviving ancient tower complex in Shandong; it was listed by the State Council in 1982 in the second batch of National Key Cultural Heritage Sites.It is not a single tower but a group of six structures - the main pavilion, the Tianhou Palace, the Dragon King Palace, the Lü Dongbin Hall, the Hall of the Three Pure Ones and the Amitabha Temple - with a total floor area of over eighteen thousand nine hundred square metres on a site of 32,800 square metres.The main pavilion is a two-storey timber structure facing south, built on the cliff edge of Red Cliff Hill, fifteen metres high with a double-eave hip-gable roof; the ground floor is 14.8 metres long and 9.65 metres deep, surrounded by an open veranda on all four sides from which visitors can lean on the rails and gaze into the distance.A great plaque in the hand of the Qing calligrapher Tie Bao hangs above, and the pavilion is filled with couplets, steles, poems and stone inscriptions, the most admired being the couplet that mirages over the sea are all illusions, while loyal ministers and filial sons are the true immortals.Its value lies first in its siting: Red Cliff Hill rises sheer from the sea, the pavilion occupies the summit and looks north over the water toward the Changshan islands, the best position from which to observe the mirage; the ancients took this to be the dwelling of immortals, and the legend of the Eight Immortals crossing the sea originated here, that legend now being on the Shandong provincial intangible cultural heritage list.Its second value is military: below the pavilion lies the Penglai Water Castle, the anti-pirate fortress, one of the most complete surviving ancient naval bases in China; pavilion and water castle together form a complete coastal defence system and are important material for the study of Ming military architecture.The Taiqing Palace, or Lower Palace, stands beside Taiqing Bay in the Laoshan District of Qingdao; founded in 140 BC, it is the largest and oldest Daoist monastery on Mount Lao and was listed by the State Council in 2013 in the seventh batch of National Key Cultural Heritage Sites.Backed by the Old Lord Peak and facing Taiqing Bay, enclosed by hills on three sides and open to the sea on the fourth, it is a model of the Daoist ideal of storing wind and gathering qi in siting.It consists of three principal courts - the Hall of the Three Officers, the Hall of the Three Pure Ones and the Hall of the Three Sovereigns - with more than one hundred and fifty halls and rooms, a floor area of over twenty-five hundred square metres and a site of thirty thousand square metres.The Hall of the Three Officers enshrines the officers of heaven, earth and water, identified with Yao, Shun and Yu; the Hall of the Three Pure Ones enshrines the Jade Pure Primordial Lord, the Supreme Pure Numinous Treasure Lord and the Grand Pure Lord of the Way; and the Hall of the Three Sovereigns enshrines the sovereigns of heaven, earth and man, identified with Fuxi, Shennong and the Yellow Emperor.The buildings have gable roofs, grey tiles and grey walls, with simple frames and no dougong or polychrome painting, expressing the Daoist aesthetic ideal of embracing the uncarved block, in sharp contrast to the splendour of imperial Daoist temples.The monastery holds many ancient trees, including cypresses of the Han, elms of the Tang and a ginkgo of the Song, all over a thousand years old; the most famous is the coiling dragon cypress in the court of the Three Officers, said to have been planted by Zhang Lianfu more than two thousand years ago, around which a Chinese trumpet vine twines and from which a sumac grows, producing the marvel of three trees in one.Outside the monastery are the Sacred Water Spring and the Dragon Head Elm, and before it the sea view known as Taiqing Moon in Water.Mount Lao is one of the birthplaces of Chinese Daoism, and its Daoist music is on the Shandong provincial intangible cultural heritage list.The Penglai Pavilion and the Taiqing Palace represent two paradigms of Daoist architecture in Shandong: the first is manifest, crowning a cliff above the sea and commanding the four quarters, winning by the mass and momentum of a tower; the second is hidden, backed by mountain and facing sea, concealed rather than exposed, excelling through the fusion of courtyard space with its environment.One manifest and one hidden, together they interpret the wisdom of siting and the spatial ideal of Chinese Daoist architecture.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 蓬莱阁北宋嘉祐六年建，中国四大名楼之一
  • 六单体组成：主阁、天后宫、龙王宫、吕祖殿、三清殿、弥陀寺
  • 主阁双层木构，高十五米，重檐歇山，四面回廊
  • 铁保题蓬莱阁巨匾，海市蜃楼皆幻影名联
  • 阁下蓬莱水城为中国现存最完整的古代军港之一
  • 八仙过海传说列入山东省省级非物质文化遗产
  • 崂山太清宫西汉建元元年始建，三官三清三皇三院
  • 建筑不施斗栱彩画，体现道家见素抱朴；汉柏盘龙三树合一

84. 聊城光岳楼与山东楼阁建筑

The Guangyue Tower in Liaocheng and Tower Architecture in Shandong
明洪武七年（1374）建 城市楼阁 全国重点文物保护单位（1988年第三批） 聊城市东昌府区古城中央

光岳楼位于聊城古城中央，始建于明洪武七年（1374年），是中国现存最高大、最古老的木结构楼阁之一，1988年被国务院公布为第三批全国重点文物保护单位。

光岳楼是明代东昌卫指挥佥事陈镛在修筑聊城城墙时，为「严更漏、窥敌望远」而主持建造的，初名「余木楼」——因修城所余木料建成，后称「鼓楼」「东昌楼」，明弘治九年（1496年），吏部考功司员外郎李赞过东昌，与太守金天锡共登此楼，取其「近鲁有光于岱岳」之意，命名「光岳楼」，沿用至今。

光岳楼在形制上承袭了宋元楼阁的遗制，同时也开启了明清楼阁的先声，是中国古代楼阁建筑发展史上的重要环节。楼为四重檐十字脊过街式楼阁，由砖石砌筑的高台基与木结构楼身两部分组成：台基为砖石结构，高九点三八米，边长三十四点五米，占地一千二百三十余平方米，四面各辟一个半圆券拱门，可通行车马，形成「过街楼」的形制；

台基之上为木结构楼身，共四层，通高三十三米（含台基），其中楼身高二十四米。楼身的结构为典型的多层木构楼阁做法：底层设金柱三十二根、檐柱二十根，金柱直通三层，形成「通柱」结构，极大地增强了楼身的整体刚度；各层施斗栱，形制古朴，保持了宋元风格；

屋顶为四重檐十字脊歇山顶，四条正脊在顶部交汇成十字形，上置高大的宝顶，四条垂脊与四条戗脊向八方伸展，轮廓优美，气势恢宏，是中国现存楼阁中等级最高的屋顶形式之一。楼内各层设楼梯可登临，顶层四面设窗，可俯瞰聊城古城全景与东昌湖风光。楼内存有大量匾额、楹联与碑刻，其中最著名的是清康熙皇帝御题的「神光钟暎」匾额，以及乾隆皇帝多次登临留下的诗碑。

光岳楼的价值在于它是中国木构楼阁技术发展的重要标本：其通柱做法、斗栱形制、举折制度、以及砖石台基与木构楼身的结合方式，都保留了宋元向明清过渡时期的技术特征，对研究中国古代高层木结构建筑的抗震、抗风与结构稳定性具有不可替代的科学价值。

山东境内还有其他重要的楼阁建筑：济宁太白楼原名贺兰氏酒楼，因李白常在此饮酒赋诗而得名，是中国历史文化名楼之一，历代文人题咏不绝，见证着诗仙李白文化在济宁的传承；济南的寿康楼由一座三层西式楼房与一座二层中式阁楼组成，为清末《山东官报》旧址；潍坊的奎文门为明代潍坊古城城门，是城市防御与楼阁建筑结合的实例；烟台所城里、即墨雄崖所城的城楼则属于军事防御性楼阁。

这些楼阁虽然功能各异、形态有别，但共同体现了山东古代高层建筑的技术成就与文化内涵。

— ENGLISH —

The Guangyue Tower stands at the centre of the old city of Liaocheng; built in 1374, it is among the tallest and oldest surviving timber towers in China and was listed by the State Council in 1988 in the third batch of National Key Cultural Heritage Sites.It was built by Chen Yong, assistant commander of the Dongchang guard, while he was constructing the city wall, for the purpose of keeping the night watches and observing the enemy; it was first called the Leftover Timber Tower because it was made from wood remaining from the wall works, and later the Drum Tower or Dongchang Tower.In 1496 Li Zan, a vice director in the Ministry of Personnel, passed through Dongchang and climbed the tower with the prefect Jin Tianxi; taking the sense that being near Lu it sheds light on Mount Tai, they named it the Guangyue Tower, the name it still bears.In form the tower inherits the legacy of Song and Yuan towers while heralding those of the Ming and Qing, making it an important link in the development of Chinese tower architecture.It is a four-eave cross-ridge tower over a street crossing, composed of a brick and stone terrace and a timber superstructure.The terrace is of brick and stone, 9.38 metres high and 34.5 metres on a side, covering over one thousand two hundred and thirty square metres, with a semicircular arched opening on each face through which carts and horses may pass - the form known as a street-crossing tower.Above it the timber body rises in four storeys to a total height of thirty-three metres including the terrace, the body itself being twenty-four metres.The structure is a typical multi-storey timber tower: the ground floor has thirty-two inner columns and twenty eave columns, the inner columns running unbroken to the third storey to form through-columns, which greatly increases the rigidity of the whole; dougong are used on each storey in an archaic manner preserving the Song and Yuan style; and the roof is a four-eave cross-ridge hip-gable in which four main ridges meet in a cross at the top beneath a tall finial, while four hanging ridges and four diagonal ridges extend in eight directions - an elegant silhouette of great magnitude and one of the highest-ranking roof forms among surviving Chinese towers.Stairs inside allow ascent, and windows on all four sides at the top give a view over the whole old city and the Dongchang Lake.The tower holds many plaques, couplets and steles, the most famous being the plaque Spirit Light Shines Afar written by the Kangxi emperor and the poem steles left by the Qianlong emperor on his repeated visits.Its value lies in being an important specimen of the development of Chinese timber tower technology: its through-columns, its dougong forms, its roof-pitch system and the way the masonry terrace combines with the timber body all preserve features of the transition from the Song and Yuan to the Ming and Qing, giving it irreplaceable scientific value for the study of earthquake and wind resistance and structural stability in traditional Chinese high-rise timber building.Shandong has other important towers.The Taibai Tower in Jining, originally the Helan wine shop, was renamed because Li Bai often drank and composed there; it is one of the famous historical towers of China, endlessly celebrated in verse, and bears witness to the transmission of Li Bai's culture in Jining.The Shoukang Building in Jinan, consisting of a three-storey western block and a two-storey Chinese pavilion, housed the Shandong Official Gazette in the late Qing.The Kuiwen Gate in Weifang is a Ming city gate of the old town, an example of the combination of defence and tower building; and the gate towers of Suochengli in Yantai and Xiongyasuo in Jimo are military towers.Different in function and form though they are, all these towers embody the technical achievement and cultural content of high-rise building in ancient Shandong.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 明洪武七年建，中国现存最高大最古老的木构楼阁之一
  • 初名余木楼，弘治九年取近鲁有光于岱岳命名光岳楼
  • 台基砖石高九点三八米，边长三十四点五米，四面券门
  • 通高三十三米，四层木构，四重檐十字脊歇山顶
  • 底层金柱三十二根直通三层，通柱结构增强刚度
  • 斗栱形制古朴，保持宋元风格
  • 康熙御题神光钟暎匾，乾隆多次登临留诗碑
  • 宋元向明清过渡的技术标本，抗震抗风研究价值高

第八章 会馆、近代建筑与装饰技艺

Chapter 8 Guild Halls, Modern Architecture and Decorative Crafts

85. 聊城山陕会馆

Shanshan Guild Hall, Liaocheng
清乾隆至嘉庆 会馆建筑·神庙与会馆结合 全国重点文物保护单位 聊城市东昌府区双街55号

山陕会馆俗称关帝庙，坐落于聊城东关古运河西岸，是清代山西、陕西客商集资合建的一处神庙与会馆相结合的古建筑群。京杭大运河贯通后，聊城为沿河九大商埠之一，享有漕挽之咽喉、江北一都会等美誉。清代顺治、康熙年间，山陕商人竞相集资筹建会馆。

会馆始建于清乾隆八年（1743年），历经四年山门、正殿等主体工程竣工，其后逐年扩修，至嘉庆十四年（1809年）方具现在之规模，历时六十六年，累计耗银六万余两。会馆坐西朝东，面河而立，东西长七十七米，南北宽四十三米，占地面积三千三百一十一平方米。

整个建筑群由山门、过楼、戏楼、夹楼、钟楼、鼓楼、南北看楼、南北碑亭、关帝大殿、文昌火神殿、财神殿、春秋阁、望楼、游廊等亭台楼阁一百六十余间组成，布局紧凑，错落有致，装饰华丽。

馆内现存历年重修碑刻十九幢，石雕方檐柱三十根，浮雕、透雕的精密木质额枋四十二方，石雕狮子、大象、麒麟等柱础十二座，照壁、折壁人物、花鸟、山水等石刻画十二幅。上百个柱础上几乎都刻有花草、鸟兽装饰。木柱、石柱、匾额上所刻楹联、文字，正楷行书兼备，为书法家所称颂。

山陕会馆的石雕、木雕、砖雕和绘画工艺堪称中国建筑艺术精品，对于研究中国古代建筑史、商业史、戏剧史、运河文化史以及书法、绘画、雕刻艺术史具有极高价值。一九八八年被国务院列为全国重点文物保护单位。

— ENGLISH —

The Shanshan Guild Hall, popularly known as the Temple of Guandi, is situated on the western bank of the ancient Grand Canal in the eastern suburb of Liaocheng.

It was funded and built jointly by merchants from Shanxi and Shaanxi provinces during the Qing Dynasty as an architectural complex combining a temple dedicated to Guan Yu with a commercial guild hall.

After the Grand Canal was opened, Liaocheng became one of the nine major commercial ports along the canal. Construction began in 1743 and continued for sixty-six years until 1809, costing over sixty thousand taels of silver.

The complex faces east toward the canal, measuring seventy-seven meters east-west and forty-three meters north-south, covering 3,311 square meters.

It comprises over 160 structures including the main gate, opera tower, bell and drum towers, viewing corridors, stele pavilions, the Guandi Hall, Wenchang Hall, God of Wealth Hall, Chunqiu Pavilion, and a watchtower.

The hall preserves nineteen steles, thirty carved stone columns, forty-two intricately carved wooden architraves, and twelve carved stone bases featuring lions, elephants, and qilin. The stone, wood, and brick carvings and paintings are considered masterpieces of Chinese architectural art.

It was designated a National Key Cultural Heritage Site in 1988.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 清代山陕商人集资合建的神庙与会馆结合建筑群
  • 占地3311平方米，160余间亭台楼阁
  • 石雕方檐柱30根、木质额枋42方、柱础石雕12座
  • 碑刻19幢，楹联文字正楷行书兼备
  • 历时66年建造（1743-1809），耗银6万余两
  • 京杭大运河沿线重要商业文化遗产
  • 1988年国务院公布为全国重点文物保护单位

86. 聊城光岳楼

Guangyue Tower, Liaocheng
明洪武七年（1374年） 楼阁建筑·过街式楼阁 全国重点文物保护单位 聊城市东昌府区古城中央

光岳楼位于聊城古城中央，始建于明洪武七年（1374年），是迄今为止全国现存古代建筑中最古老、最雄伟的木构楼阁之一。明洪武二年（1369年），东昌卫守御指挥佥事陈镛为严更漏、窥敌望远、报时报警，利用修城余木建造此楼，故初名余木楼，后又称鼓楼、东昌楼。

明弘治九年（1496年），考功员外郎李赞与东昌府太守金天锡商定命名光岳楼，取其近鲁有光于岱岳之意。光岳楼是一座由宋元向明清过渡的代表建筑，在形式上承袭宋元楼阁遗制，结构上继承唐宋传统风格，同时与明初其他建筑有若干相似之处，开官式建筑先河。

外观为四重檐歇山十字脊过街式楼阁，由墩台和主楼两部分组成。墩台为砖石砌成的正四棱台，高九米，占地一千二百三十六平方米，边长三十四点五米，由交叉相通的四个半圆拱门和直通主楼的五十多级台阶组成。四层主楼筑于墩台之上，全为木结构，高约二十四米，方形外加围廊。全楼有一百一十二个台阶、一百九十二根金柱、二百余朵斗拱。通高和四边长都是三十三米，即古代九丈九尺，九为阳数之极，寓意不可超越。

楼内匾、联、题、刻琳琅满目，其中清康熙帝御笔神光钟暎匾、乾隆帝诗刻以及郭沫若、丰子恺匾额楹联至为珍贵。乾隆皇帝九过东昌，六登光岳楼，将其列为南巡三十六行宫之一。光岳楼是中国现存明代楼阁中最大的一座，一九八八年被列为全国重点文物保护单位。

— ENGLISH —

Guangyue Tower stands at the center of the old city of Liaocheng. Built in 1374, the seventh year of the Hongwu reign of the Ming Dynasty, it is one of the oldest and most magnificent timber-framed tower pavilions surviving in China.

The tower was originally constructed by Chen Yong, the military commander of Dongchang Guard, using leftover timber from city wall repairs, for the purposes of timekeeping, enemy surveillance, and alarm signaling. It was initially named Yumu Lou (Leftover Timber Tower) and later called Dongchang Lou.

In 1496, it received its current name Guangyue Lou, meaning illuminating the sacred Mount Tai from nearby Lu.

The tower represents a transitional architectural style from the Song-Yuan to the Ming-Qing period, inheriting Song-Yuan pavilion forms and Tang-Song structural traditions while pioneering official-style architecture.

It is a four-eaved hip-and-gable cross-ridge over-street pavilion consisting of a brick-stone platform base and a four-story main tower. The base is nine meters high, covering 1,236 square meters. The main tower, entirely of wood, stands approximately twenty-four meters tall.

The entire structure has 112 steps, 192 golden pillars, and over 200 bracket sets. Its total height and width are both thirty-three meters, symbolizing the ancient nine zhang and nine chi, with nine being the supreme yang number.

The tower houses precious inscriptions including Emperor Kangxi calligraphy and poems by Emperor Qianlong, who visited the tower six times. It is the largest surviving Ming Dynasty tower pavilion in China and was designated a National Key Cultural Heritage Site in 1988.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 明洪武七年（1374年）始建，距今650余年
  • 全国现存明代楼阁中最大的一座
  • 四重檐歇山十字脊过街式楼阁
  • 全木结构，192根金柱，200余朵斗拱
  • 通高33米，取九丈九尺阳数之极
  • 康熙御笔神光钟暎匾、乾隆六次登楼
  • 宋元向明清过渡的代表建筑，开官式建筑先河

87. 青岛德国总督府旧址与总督官邸

German Governor Office and Residence, Qingdao
清光绪至宣统（1903-1908年） 近代建筑·德式公共建筑 全国重点文物保护单位 青岛市市南区沂水路11号 / 龙山路26号

青岛德国总督府旧址坐落于观海山南坡，始建于1903年，1906年竣工，位于青岛老城中心，面朝大海，是胶澳殖民地统治机构所在地。建筑面积四千零八十三平方米，高度三十米，采用十九世纪欧洲公共建筑的对称平面，四角和中间稍作突出，整座建筑俯视图呈凹字形。

厚达六十多厘米的外墙以黄色为基调，局部以花岗岩石料作装饰，石面加工粗朴。米红色筒瓦、蓝色鱼鳞瓦、绿色牛舌瓦铺设的折坡屋顶，使大楼精美别致。以青岛路为中轴线，既庄严又肃穆。

德国总督官邸旧址又称青岛迎宾馆，建于1905年，1908年正式竣工，坐落于信号山脚下，背倚风景如画的山坡，依山面海，雄伟壮观。建筑风格为德国威廉时代典型建筑样式与青年风格派相结合，具有欧洲皇家风范，古典而又华丽，是近代中国出现的最具代表性的一幢德国风格建筑。

梁思成曾评价它是融合东西方多种文化理念于一体的建筑艺术巨制。米红色筒瓦、蓝色鱼鳞瓦、绿色牛舌瓦铺设的楼顶，在造型和装饰上令人流连。如今这种风格的房屋在德国乃至欧洲大陆也不多见了。总督府与总督官邸共同构成了青岛德占时期殖民统治建筑的典型代表，见证了青岛从渔村到近代城市的历史变迁。

— ENGLISH —

The former German Governor Office is situated on the southern slope of Guanhai Hill, overlooking the sea. Construction began in 1903 and was completed in 1906. It served as the administrative center of the German Kiautschou Leased Territory.

The building covers 4,083 square meters and stands thirty meters tall, adopting the symmetrical plan of nineteenth-century European public architecture. Its plan forms a concave shape with projecting corners and center. The walls, over sixty centimeters thick, are yellow-toned with granite decorations.

The pitched roof is covered with red, blue fish-scale, and green tongue-shaped tiles. The Governor Residence, also known as the Qingdao Guest House, was built from 1905 to 1908 at the foot of Signal Hill.

It combines the typical German Wilhelmine style with Jugendstil (Art Nouveau) elements, displaying European royal grandeur. Liang Sicheng, China foremost architectural historian, praised it as an architectural masterpiece fusing Eastern and Western cultural concepts.

The red, blue, and green tiled roof is a rare survival of this style, which is now uncommon even in Germany. Together, these two buildings represent the quintessential German colonial architecture of Qingdao and bear witness to the city transformation from a fishing village to a modern city.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 德国总督府1903-1906年建，建筑面积4083平方米
  • 总督官邸1905-1908年建，德国威廉时代与青年风格派结合
  • 梁思成誉为融合东西方多种文化理念于一体的建筑艺术巨制
  • 米红筒瓦、蓝色鱼鳞瓦、绿色牛舌瓦三色屋顶
  • 十九世纪欧洲公共建筑对称平面，凹字形俯视图
  • 青岛德占时期殖民统治建筑的典型代表
  • 全国重点文物保护单位

88. 青岛圣弥厄尔大教堂

St. Michael Cathedral, Qingdao
民国（1932-1934年） 近代建筑·哥特式教堂 山东省文物保护单位 青岛市市南区浙江路15号

青岛圣弥厄尔大教堂本名圣弥厄尔主教座堂，本地人习惯称之为天主教堂，由德国设计师毕娄哈依据哥特式和罗马式建筑风格设计而成，始建于1932年，是青岛地区最大的哥特式建筑，也是中国唯一的祝圣教堂。教堂平面呈拉丁十字形，通体以黄色花岗岩加钢筋混凝土砌造，入口处选用青岛花岗岩。正面两侧各耸立一座钟塔，塔内悬有四口大钟，当钟乐鸣奏，声传数里之外。

大门上方设一巨大玫瑰窗，线条流畅的半圆拱形窗排列有序。教堂坐东面西，占地约二千六百平方米。七只铜制大吊灯悬挂在顶棚，柔和的阳光穿过色彩斑斓的玻璃花窗，在光影映射下，教堂显得神圣又浪漫。彩色玻璃镶嵌而成的窗户，每扇绘制有不同的图案，表现《圣经》中的故事情节，人物形象栩栩如生。阳光透过窗户折射出七彩光斑，洒进室内，映照在整个大厅，产生如梦似幻的美感。

圣弥厄尔大教堂是青岛标志性建筑之一，与江苏路基督教堂、青岛德国警察署旧址的钟楼并称青岛五大钟楼。教堂虽经历史风雨，但至今意气风发，是许多新人拍摄婚纱照的圣地，也是游客心中经典的浪漫地标。

— ENGLISH —

St. Michael Cathedral, officially the Cathedral of St. Michael the Archangel and locally known as the Catholic Church, was designed by German architect Bila combining Gothic and Romanesque styles.

Construction began in 1932, and it is the largest Gothic building in the Qingdao area and the only consecrated cathedral in China. The church follows a Latin cross plan, built with yellow granite and reinforced concrete. Twin bell towers flank the main facade, each housing four bronze bells whose chimes carry for miles.

A large rose window sits above the main entrance. Seven bronze chandeliers hang from the ceiling, and sunlight filtering through the stained glass windows creates a spectrum of colors across the interior. Each stained glass window depicts a different scene from the Bible with lifelike figures.

The cathedral is one of Qingdao most iconic landmarks and is one of the city Five Great Bell Towers, alongside the Jiangsu Road Christian Church and the former German Police Station. It remains a popular site for wedding photography and a beloved romantic landmark for visitors.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 1932年由德国设计师毕娄哈设计
  • 青岛地区最大的哥特式建筑，中国唯一的祝圣教堂
  • 拉丁十字平面，黄色花岗岩加钢筋混凝土砌造
  • 双钟塔各悬四口大钟，声传数里
  • 巨大玫瑰窗与彩色玻璃花窗
  • 青岛五大钟楼之一，标志性建筑
  • 山东省文物保护单位

89. 青岛里院建筑群

Qingdao Liyuan Courtyard Apartment Complex
清末至民国（1900s-1940s） 近代民居·中西合璧 青岛市文物保护单位 青岛市市北区大鲍岛片区

里院是独具青岛特色的一种中西合璧的建筑样式，形成于上世纪二三十年代，鼎盛于四十年代，是青岛特有的民居建筑。里院依据青岛地势而建，将西式洋楼与中国传统四合院巧妙结合，成为青岛城市发展的见证和特殊的文化符号。

里院多为二至三层围合式建筑，中间形成天井式院落，沿街一面为商铺，院内一面为住宅，一楼一院，多边形的一方天地承载了许多老青岛人的情感。里院体现了上世纪中期和谐共济的居住理念，以及诚恳、直接、简约又明了的人生哲学。

广兴里是青岛现存最大的里院建筑之一，位于大鲍岛片区，始建于1906年，分为南院和北院，总建筑面积约一万五千五百平方米，南院呈L型分布，是青岛现存规模最大的里院综合体。

走在高高低低的毛石台阶与木构挑梁之间，老青岛的市井生活气息扑面而来。三江里位于济宁路24号，百年前曾是三江会馆，门前的大戏楼有琴港第一戏楼的美名，洪深、老舍、俞珊等名人都曾前来赏戏。如今在保留原有建筑风貌的基础上，三江里引入了书房、精酿、手作工坊与文创店铺，蜕变成复古摩登的时尚街区。

随着青岛老城更新，以广兴里、三江里为代表的里院建筑重新回到人们视野，成为承载城市记忆与当代生活的活态空间。

— ENGLISH —

Liyuan is a unique Qingdao architectural form that seamlessly blends Western-style buildings with traditional Chinese courtyard houses.

Emerging in the 1920s-1930s and flourishing in the 1940s, Liyuan is a distinctly Qingdao vernacular dwelling type adapted to the city hilly terrain. These structures typically consist of two-to-three-story buildings arranged around a central courtyard, with shops facing the street and residences inside.

Each Liyuan is a self-contained polygonal world that carried the daily life and emotions of generations of Qingdao residents, embodying a mid-twentieth-century philosophy of harmonious coexistence. Guangxing Li, the largest surviving Liyuan, is located in the Dabaodao district.

Built in 1906, it comprises north and south courtyards totaling approximately 15,500 square meters, with the south courtyard forming an L-shape, the largest surviving Liyuan complex in Qingdao.

Sanjiang Li, at 24 Jining Road, was formerly the Sanjiang Guild Hall a century ago, whose grand opera stage was known as Qingdao First Opera Tower and attracted luminaries including Lao She and Hong Shen.

Today, Sanjiang Li has been revitalized with bookshops, craft breweries, workshops, and creative retail spaces while preserving its original architectural character.

As Qingdao old city undergoes renewal, Liyuan buildings have returned to public attention as living spaces that carry urban memory while serving contemporary life.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 青岛特有的中西合璧民居建筑形式
  • 形成于1920-1930年代，鼎盛于1940年代
  • 西式洋楼与四合院巧妙结合
  • 广兴里建筑面积约15500平方米，现存最大里院
  • 三江里原为三江会馆，老舍等名人曾到访
  • 从居住功能向文旅活化转型的典型案例
  • 体现和谐共济的居住理念与市井文化

90. 青岛八大关万国建筑群

Badaguan Multinational Architecture Ensemble, Qingdao
民国（1930s） 近代建筑·别墅群 全国重点文物保护单位 青岛市市南区八大关风景区

八大关因以八大著名关隘命名道路而来，区内有二百多栋建筑，涵盖了俄、英、美、德、法、日、丹麦等二十多个国家的建筑风格，是名副其实的万国建筑博物馆。八大关风景区坐落于青岛市区南部，背倚太平山，面朝黄海，岬角交错，海湾蜿蜒。五步一楼，十步一阁，历史沉淀与异国风情在此交织。花石楼建于1930年，坐落于八大关南端岬角，背倚万国建筑群，三面临海，位置得天独厚。

建筑融合希腊、罗马与哥特式风格，外墙以花岗岩砌筑，塔楼顶部饰有雉堞式女儿墙。内部墙面选用莱州滑石装饰，因滑与花谐音，故得名花石楼。主体共五层，顶层设观海台，登临可俯瞰三面海景。公主楼位于居庸关路10号，建于二十世纪三十年代中期，据传为丹麦公主建设的避暑别墅，虽然公主并未到来，公主楼之名却流传至今。整座建筑由尖塔和不规则斜顶屋组成，是一座典型的丹麦式建筑，周身为梦幻的孔雀蓝，屋顶为红色大坡度教堂塔楼式屋檐。

茂密的落叶松环绕在楼的四周，沿着砖铺的小径，跨过花园式的庭院进入楼内，宛如走进了安徒生童话中的王宫。八大关建筑群每一栋建筑都完美地与环境融合，独树一帜、不可复制，漫步其中，安逸、静谧、古感的气息扑面而来。

— ENGLISH —

Badaguan, named after eight famous mountain passes that give its roads their names, contains over 200 buildings representing more than twenty national architectural styles including Russian, British, American, German, French, Japanese, and Danish. It is aptly called the Museum of World Architecture.

The scenic area is located in southern Qingdao, backed by Taiping Mountain and facing the Yellow Sea. The Huashi Lou (Flower-Stone Tower), built in 1930, sits on the southernmost promontory of Badaguan, surrounded by the sea on three sides.

It blends Greek, Roman, and Gothic styles, with granite exterior walls and a battlemented parapet atop its tower. The interior walls are decorated with Laizhou talc stone; since talc sounds like flower in Chinese, the building became known as Flower-Stone Tower.

The Princess House, at 10 Juyongguan Road, was built in the mid-1930s, allegedly as a summer villa for a Danish princess. Though the princess never came, the name endured. It is a quintessential Danish-style building with a dreamy peacock-blue exterior and a red steep-pitched roof.

Each building in Badaguan is uniquely integrated with its surroundings, making the ensemble irreplaceable and atmospheric.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 200余栋建筑涵盖20多个国家风格
  • 名副其实的万国建筑博物馆
  • 花石楼：融合希腊、罗马与哥特式，三面临海
  • 公主楼：典型丹麦式建筑，孔雀蓝外墙
  • 1930年代建成的别墅群，全国重点文物保护单位
  • 每栋建筑与环境完美融合，独树一帜
  • 青岛历史文化名城的核心组成部分

91. 济南洪家楼天主教堂

Hongjialou Catholic Cathedral, Jinan
清光绪（1902-1905年） 近代建筑·哥特式教堂 全国重点文物保护单位 济南市历城区洪家楼北路

洪家楼天主教堂全称洪家楼耶稣圣心主教座堂，是华北和华东地区规模最大的天主教堂，也是中国三大天主教堂之一，享有东方巴黎圣母院的美称。清同治九年（1870年），天主教济南教区顾立爵神父在洪家楼购地修建教堂。1900年义和团运动中教堂被破坏。

光绪二十七年（1901年）《辛丑条约》签订后，天主教山东教区方济各会荷兰籍主教申永福决定利用庚子赔款在原址兴建新教堂。

光绪二十八年（1902年），奥地利修士庞会襄受命担任总设计师和监工，历城石匠卢立成受聘担任总施工，带领本村一百多名石匠住进工地，历时三年于光绪三十一年（1905年）修建完成。教堂属于典型的欧洲十二至十六世纪初哥特式建筑风格，以巴黎圣母院和科隆大教堂为蓝本。

坐东面西，建筑面积约二千六百平方米。正面有两个高约六十米的尖顶分立式钟楼，塔顶十字架高约三米，后端也有两个高约五十五米的尖塔，前后四塔相互对应，皆为青砖、料石砌垒。教堂主厅中央通廊高大，地面用青条石铺就，堂内设两排方形纵柱，柱头雕刻镂空花卉，圆顶和墙壁上绘满宗教壁画。彩色玻璃镶嵌的窗户在阳光照射下产生强烈光色效果，每扇窗绘制不同的《圣经》故事。

洪家楼教堂虽是比较纯粹的西式建筑，但细节处可见中国传统文化元素：屋顶盖中国传统小黑瓦，有殿宇起脊结构与徽派马头墙，中门两侧石墙雕有石龙头，内部壁画除西方宗教故事外也有中国高山流水、仙鹤延年的内容。2006年被国务院列入第六批全国重点文物保护单位。

— ENGLISH —

Hongjialou Catholic Cathedral, formally the Cathedral of the Sacred Heart of Jesus, is the largest Catholic church in northern and eastern China and one of China three great cathedrals,享有 the reputation of Oriental Notre-Dame de Paris.

The original church on this site, built in 1870, was destroyed during the Boxer Rebellion in 1900. After the Boxer Protocol of 1901, the Dutch Franciscan Bishop of Shandong used indemnity funds to rebuild.

Austrian brother Pang Huixiang served as chief designer, and local stonemason Lu Licheng was appointed chief constructor, leading over one hundred stonemasons in a three-year effort completed in 1905.

The cathedral follows a classic Gothic style modeled on Notre-Dame de Paris and Cologne Cathedral, facing west with approximately 2,600 square meters of floor area. The facade features twin spires reaching about sixty meters, topped with three-meter crosses, with two additional fifty-five-meter spires at the rear.

The nave has a soaring central aisle paved with blue stone, two rows of square columns with carved floral capitals, and walls and vaults covered with religious murals. Stained glass windows depicting Biblical scenes create dramatic light effects.

Though primarily Western in design, the cathedral incorporates Chinese elements: traditional small black tiles on the roof, Chinese-style ridge structures and Anhui-style horse-head walls, dragon-head stone carvings flanking the central portal, and interior murals featuring Chinese landscapes and longevity cranes alongside Western religious scenes.

It was designated a National Key Cultural Heritage Site in 2006.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 华北华东地区最大天主教堂，中国三大天主教堂之一
  • 1902-1905年建，奥地利修士庞会襄设计
  • 哥特式风格，以巴黎圣母院和科隆大教堂为蓝本
  • 双钟楼高约60米，彩色玻璃花窗
  • 中西合璧：中国小黑瓦、马头墙、石龙头雕刻
  • 历城石匠卢立成率百余名石匠历时三年建成
  • 2006年国务院公布为全国重点文物保护单位

92. 济南经四路基督教堂

Jingsi Road Christian Church, Jinan
民国（1924-1926年） 近代建筑·基督教堂 山东省文物保护单位 济南市槐荫区经四路425号

济南经四路基督教堂简称经四路教堂，位于济南市槐荫区经四路425号，是济南现有的七处基督教教堂之一，也是济南最大的基督教堂和商埠地区的标志性建筑之一。

教堂南部立面原为山东中华基督教自立会礼拜堂，1924年始建，1926年落成。经四路教堂的独特之处在于，这是第一座完全由中国人投资、设计、建造的基督教建筑，建成后也一直由中国教牧人员管理。

其设计者为该处原临时礼拜堂第一任中国籍牧师李道辉之子李洪根，施工建造者为桓台籍建筑商杨长利、杨长贞。教堂底层为毛石砌墙，二层以上为清水红砖墙、红瓦顶，色彩鲜艳明快。立面造型质朴庄重，以文艺复兴时期建筑手法为基础，并融合了中国传统建筑部分形式，例如使用了大量的长方形门窗，而不是西方惯用的半圆形拱式门窗，与整个商埠地区西式建筑的设计风格相融合。

经四路教堂的建造，标志着中国基督教自立运动在建筑领域的重要成果，体现了近代济南开埠以后中西建筑文化的交流与融合。

— ENGLISH —

Jingsi Road Christian Church, located at 425 Jingsi Road in Jinan Huaiyin District, is one of seven Christian churches in Jinan and the city largest. Its southern facade was originally the Shandong Chinese Christian Self-Governing Church chapel, constructed from 1924 to 1926.

What makes this church particularly significant is that it was the first Christian building entirely funded, designed, and built by Chinese people, and has been managed by Chinese clergy since completion. The designer was Li Honggen, son of Li Daohui, the first Chinese pastor of the original temporary chapel.

The builders were Yang Changli and Yang Changzhen, contractors from Huantai. The church features a rusticated stone ground floor with red brick walls and red tile roof above, creating a bright appearance.

The facade is dignified and modest, based on Renaissance architectural techniques while incorporating Chinese traditional forms, such as the extensive use of rectangular doors and windows instead of the Western semicircular arches typical of church architecture.

This design harmonizes with the Western-style buildings of Jinan commercial port district. The church represents an important achievement of the Chinese Christian self-governance movement and exemplifies the exchange and fusion of Chinese and Western architectural cultures in modern Jinan.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 第一座完全由中国人投资、设计、建造的基督教建筑
  • 1924-1926年建，济南最大基督教堂
  • 设计者李洪根，施工者桓台杨长利、杨长贞
  • 文艺复兴建筑手法融合中国传统形式
  • 毛石底墙、清水红砖墙、红瓦顶
  • 济南商埠地区标志性建筑
  • 中国基督教自立运动在建筑领域的重要成果

93. 烟台山近代建筑群

Yantaishan Modern Architecture Ensemble
清至民国（1861-1930s） 近代建筑·领事馆群 全国重点文物保护单位 烟台市芝罘区烟台山

烟台山近代建筑群是中国目前现存最完整、最密集的近代领事馆群，是研究中国近代建筑史、中西文化交流史和中国近代社会发展史的珍贵实物。1861年烟台开埠后，英、法、美、德、日、俄、丹麦、挪威等十七个国家先后在烟台山及周围设立领事馆或代理领事。

英国驻登州领事马礼逊实地考察后，认为烟台港拥有向东和东北方向敞开的U型天然港湾，芝罘岛由夹河冲积形成的巨大沙洲横卧北部构成天然屏障，遂选定烟台取代登州为通商口岸。烟台山上的主路被称为领事路，分东西两路，英国、法国、德国、丹麦、日本、俄国等国领事馆设于此。烟台山上还有天主教烟台主教府、联合教堂等。山下朝阳街与海关街之间洋行林立，英国大东电报公司、法国天主教施医院等位于烟台山以东沿海一线。

东海关税务司署旧址始建于1863年，位于海关街，是烟台开埠的标志性建筑。1876年北洋大臣李鸿章与英国公使威妥玛在此签订了《中英烟台条约》。

朝阳街开埠建筑群北起烟台山，占地约四十二公顷，内有全国重点文物保护单位二十处、省级文物保护单位七处、市级文物保护单位十七处、不可移动文物三十三处，是展现开埠后十里洋场中西文化碰撞的代表性区域。近年来相关部门对开埠遗迹实施修缮保护，如2023年启动了挪威领事馆旧址修缮保护工程。

— ENGLISH —

The Yantaishan Modern Architecture Ensemble is the most complete and densely concentrated surviving group of modern consular buildings in China, providing invaluable physical evidence for the study of modern Chinese architectural history, Sino-Western cultural exchange, and social development.

After Yantai was opened as a treaty port in 1861, seventeen countries including Britain, France, the United States, Germany, Japan, Russia, Denmark, and Norway established consulates or proxy consulates on and around Yantaishan (Yantai Hill).

British consul Morrison, after surveying the area, determined that Yantai U-shaped natural harbor, shielded by the sandbar formed by the Jia River deposits behind Zhifu Island, was superior to the originally designated port of Dengzhou.

The Eastern Customs (Donghai) Tax Bureau, built in 1863 on Customs Street, is a landmark building of Yantai opening. There in 1876, Li Hongzhang and the British envoy Thomas Wade signed the Chefoo Convention.

The Chaoyang Street commercial district, covering approximately forty-two hectares, contains twenty National Key Cultural Heritage Sites, seven provincial-level sites, and seventeen municipal-level sites, representing the collision of Chinese and Western cultures in the ten-mile foreign settlement.

Recent conservation efforts include the 2023 restoration of the former Norwegian Consulate.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 中国现存最完整、最密集的近代领事馆群
  • 1861年烟台开埠后17国设立领事馆
  • 东海关税务司署旧址：1863年建，《中英烟台条约》签署地
  • 朝阳街开埠建筑群占地42公顷，20处国保单位
  • 英国领事马礼逊选定烟台取代登州为通商口岸
  • 中西文化碰撞的代表性区域
  • 全国重点文物保护单位

94. 张裕酿酒公司旧址与地下酒窖

Changyu Winery and Underground Cellar, Yantai
清光绪十八年（1892年） 近代工业建筑·酒窖 全国重点文物保护单位 烟台市芝罘区大马路56号

张裕酿酒公司由南洋华侨张弼士于1892年创办，是中国近代首家工业化葡萄酒生产企业，开中国工业化葡萄酒生产之先河。公司位于烟台山东南、东河下游，建厂区的同时购置荒山引进葡萄新品种，并将东河加盖成路，名张裕路（今解放路北段）。

张裕公司的创立与烟台开埠密切相关：开埠后西方势力控制了航运、贸易、金融等行业，客观上也带动了烟台城市的发展，一批民族工商业在外资刺激下开始萌芽。张裕公司地下酒窖建于1905年，是中国现存最早的近代化地下葡萄酒窖，被誉为亚洲第一大酒窖。

酒窖深入地下数十米，采用拱形砖石结构，恒温恒湿，常年保持摄氏十二度左右的温度和八十五度左右的湿度，为葡萄酒的陈酿提供了理想环境。酒窖内分为多个区域，贮藏着不同年份、不同品种的葡萄酒，其中不乏百年老酒。张裕公司酒窖不仅是中国近代工业建筑的珍贵遗存，也是中国民族工业发展史的重要见证。

从1892年创办至今，烟台的葡萄种植业和酿酒业依然受益于张裕公司的早期举措，张裕品牌已成为中国葡萄酒的标志性符号。酒窖于2006年被国务院列为全国重点文物保护单位。

— ENGLISH —

Changyu Winery, founded in 1892 by the Nanyang Chinese entrepreneur Zhang Bishi, was China first industrialized wine producer, pioneering industrial wine production in the country. The company is located southeast of Yantai Hill and along the lower reaches of the East River.

While building the factory, Zhang purchased wasteland to introduce new grape varieties and covered the East River to create Zhangyu Road (today northern Jiefang Road).

The founding of Changyu was closely linked to Yantai opening as a treaty port: while foreign powers controlled shipping, trade, and finance, they also inadvertently stimulated the city development and the emergence of national industries.

The underground cellar, built in 1905, is China earliest surviving modern underground wine cellar, hailed as Asia Largest Cellar.

It extends dozens of meters underground with arched brick-stone construction, maintaining a constant temperature of approximately twelve degrees Celsius and eighty-five percent humidity year-round, providing ideal conditions for wine aging.

The cellar is divided into multiple zones storing wines of different vintages and varieties, including century-old bottles. The Changyu cellar is not only a precious remnant of China modern industrial architecture but also an important witness to the development of China national industry.

Designated a National Key Cultural Heritage Site in 2006, the Changyu brand remains an iconic symbol of Chinese wine.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 1892年南洋华侨张弼士创办，中国首家工业化葡萄酒企业
  • 地下酒窖建于1905年，亚洲第一大酒窖
  • 拱形砖石结构，恒温12°C、恒湿85%
  • 中国现存最早的近代化地下葡萄酒窖
  • 中国民族工业发展史的重要见证
  • 百年老酒陈酿，不同年份品种分区储藏
  • 2006年国务院公布为全国重点文物保护单位

95. 威海英租时期建筑群

British Lease Period Architecture, Weihai
清光绪至民国（1898-1930s） 近代建筑·英式建筑群 全国重点文物保护单位 威海市环翠区北山路、环海路一带

威海英租时期建筑群是1898年至1930年英国租借威海卫期间留下的西式建筑遗存，2013年被国务院列为全国重点文物保护单位。

1898年英国强租威海卫后，在刘公岛及威海城区兴建了领事馆、官邸、学校、教堂、洋行等各类建筑，形成了较为完整的英式建筑群。威海英国领事馆旧址位于北山路1号和6-2号，建于1899至1902年，是英租时期的核心建筑。

英国工程师住宅旧址位于东山路6号，泰茂洋行旧址位于育华路26号，均属省级文物保护单位。英海军司令避暑房旧址俗称四眼楼，位于环海路7-1号，建于1904年，因四面皆有窗户可观海景而得名，是威海英租建筑的标志性建筑之一。

环海路沿线还分布着英商别墅旧址、意人别墅旧址、美国牙医别墅旧址等多国侨民住宅，展现了英租时期威海作为国际避暑胜地的多元文化面貌。海星学校旧址位于文化路5号，建于1921至1934年，是英租时期教育建筑的代表。

宽仁院旧址位于新威附路，建于1934至1937年。收回威海卫纪念塔位于海滨北路，建于1931年，是纪念1930年收回威海卫主权的重要历史建筑。

威海英租建筑群集中体现了英国殖民建筑风格在中国沿海的传播与适应，是研究中国近代建筑史和中西文化交流的重要实物资料。

— ENGLISH —

The British Lease Period Architecture in Weihai comprises Western-style buildings constructed between 1898 and 1930 during the British lease of Weihaiwei, designated a National Key Cultural Heritage Site in 2013.

After Britain leased Weihaiwei in 1898, consulates, residences, schools, churches, and trading houses were built on Liugong Island and in the Weihai urban area, forming a relatively complete ensemble of British-style buildings.

The former British Consulate, at 1 and 6-2 Beishan Road, was built from 1899 to 1902 and served as the core building of the lease period.

The Four-Eye Tower (Siyan Lou), at 7-1 Huanhai Road, built in 1904, earned its nickname from windows on all four sides offering sea views, and is one of the most iconic British lease-period buildings.

Huanhai Road features villas of British, Italian, and American residents, reflecting Weihai cosmopolitan character as an international summer resort during the lease period. The former Haixing School, at 5 Wenhua Road, built from 1921 to 1934, represents educational architecture of the period.

The Weihaiwei Recovery Memorial Tower, built in 1931 on Haibin North Road, commemorates the 1930 return of Weihaiwei to Chinese sovereignty.

This architectural ensemble demonstrates the spread and adaptation of British colonial architecture along the Chinese coast and provides important physical evidence for studying modern Chinese architectural history and Sino-Western cultural exchange.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 1898-1930年英国租借威海卫时期建筑遗存
  • 英国领事馆旧址：1899-1902年建，核心建筑
  • 四眼楼：四面观海，英租建筑标志性建筑
  • 环海路沿线多国侨民别墅，多元文化面貌
  • 海星学校旧址：1921-1934年，教育建筑代表
  • 收回威海卫纪念塔：1931年建，纪念主权收回
  • 2013年国务院公布为全国重点文物保护单位

第九章 保护、活化与传承

Chapter 9 Conservation, Revitalisation and Transmission

96. 山东省历史文化名城名镇名村保护体系

Shandong Historic Cities, Towns and Villages Protection System
当代 文化遗产保护·制度体系 省级保护体系 山东省全域

山东省现有国家和省级历史文化名城二十座、名镇四十九个、名村七十一个，历史文化街区六十片，传统村落五百五十七个，历史建筑二千二百三十六处。潍坊于近年跻身国家历史文化名城，至此山东国家历史文化名城达到十一座，数量位居全国第二。山东省坚持空间全覆盖、要素全囊括，构建大保护格局。《山东省十五五规划纲要》明确系统性保护文物古迹、名城名镇、传统村落、非遗等各类遗产。

从省级《山东省历史文化名城名镇名村保护条例》到《山东省历史建筑保护办法》，多地出台配套法规，所有名城名镇、街区全部编制保护规划，依法保护、依规管控的制度框架基本成型。山东坚持真实保护原则，把历史文化遗产保护全面融入城乡建设，留住不同时期的城乡记忆。持续开展资源专项调查，新挖掘各类潜在保护要素千余处。加大资金投入，推进街区整治、历史建筑修缮，完善基础设施。

连续六年开展省级专项评估，建立问题整改销号机制，各地落实日常巡查、网格化管理，以常态化监管守住遗产安全底线。山东拥有中国传统村落一百六十八个、省级传统村落三百八十四个、国家传统村落集中连片保护利用示范县五个，初步形成了以威海荣成市为代表的胶东地区、以枣庄山亭区、济宁嘉祥县、临沂沂水县为代表的鲁西南地区、以济南章丘区为代表的鲁中地区三大传统村落保护利用集中连片示范区。

— ENGLISH —

Shandong Province currently has twenty national and provincial-level historic cities, forty-nine historic towns, seventy-one historic villages, sixty historic districts, 557 traditional villages, and 2,236 historic buildings.

Weifang was recently designated a National Historic City, bringing Shandong total to eleven National Historic Cities, ranking second nationally. The province has built a comprehensive protection framework with full spatial coverage.

The Shandong Fifteenth Five-Year Plan explicitly mandates systematic protection of cultural relics, historic cities, traditional villages, and intangible cultural heritage. From provincial regulations to historic building protection measures, all historic cities, towns, and districts have compiled protection plans.

Shandong conducts continuous resource surveys, having newly identified over a thousand potential protection elements. The province has conducted provincial-level special assessments for six consecutive years.

Shandong boasts 168 Chinese Traditional Villages, 384 provincial-level traditional villages, and five national demonstration counties for cluster protection, forming three major concentrated protection zones: the Jiaodong region, the southwestern Shandong region, and the central Shandong region.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 20座名城、49个名镇、71个名村、60片街区
  • 557个传统村落，2236处历史建筑
  • 11座国家历史文化名城，数量全国第二
  • 省级保护条例与历史建筑保护办法构成制度框架
  • 168个中国传统村落，5个国家级集中连片示范县
  • 连续六年省级专项评估，问题整改销号机制
  • 胶东、鲁西南、鲁中三大集中连片保护区

97. 传统村落集中连片保护利用

Cluster Protection and Utilization of Traditional Villages
当代 文化遗产保护·集中连片 国家级示范 嘉祥县、沂水县、招远市、青州市、蒙阴县等

山东省积极推进传统村落从单一保护向集中连片保护转变，探索传统村落加融合发展模式。嘉祥县、沂水县两个国家级传统村落集中连片保护示范县，以及招远市、青州市、蒙阴县三个省级传统村落集中连片保护利用县市，分别获得国家级三千万元和省级一千万元的定额奖补资金。嘉祥县以武氏墓群石刻为代表的汉画像石数量位居全国首位，传统村落保护利用项目分两期实施：一期涉及石头民居修缮保护、村落步道提升、数字博物馆及非遗展馆建设；

二期为武氏祠文化展馆、汉儒林研学基地、非遗文化体验区建设。沂水县秦代置县、隋初定名，已有一千四百余年历史，是千年古县和沂蒙精神主要发祥地。沂水传统村落集中连片保护锚定沂蒙精神发源地、红色宜居古村落发展定位，保护春秋遗风，重启千年商埠铁路驿站。村内常见的干插石头房颇具特色：干插即不使用砂浆或黏合剂，依靠石块的形状、重量和摩擦力，以错缝相挤、层层叠压并在缝隙间填充碎石加固的形式构筑房屋墙体。

部分民居采用薄石板鱼鳞瓦屋顶，选用当地页岩、石灰岩薄石板沿坡顶交错叠压铺设，屋脊处用整块长条形石板压顶固定。青州市形成一圈一带集中连片保护利用模式：西部以凤凰新村为核心辐射五个传统村落，南部以井塘村等五个传统村落为节点串联美丽村居。蒙阴县在岱崮镇打造中国军工文化园，将传统村落保护与旅游开发结合。

— ENGLISH —

Shandong is transforming traditional village protection from individual site preservation to cluster-based conservation, exploring a traditional village plus integrated development model.

Jiaxiang and Yishui counties, as national demonstration counties, receive thirty million yuan each in national funding, while Zhaoyuan, Qingzhou, and Mengyin receive ten million yuan each in provincial funding.

Jiaxiang County leads the nation in Han dynasty stone reliefs, with projects divided into two phases. Yishui, a millennium ancient county with over 1,400 years of history, anchors its protection in Yimeng Spirit birthplace positioning.

The distinctive dry-stack stone houses use no mortar: stones are held by shape, weight, and friction, with gravel filling gaps. Some dwellings feature fish-scale tile roofs made of local shale and limestone slabs. Qingzhou has formed a one circle, one belt cluster model.

Mengyin County is developing a Chinese military industry cultural park in Daigu Town, integrating village protection with tourism.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 国家级示范县获3000万元，省级获1000万元奖补
  • 嘉祥县汉画像石数量全国首位，干插石头房
  • 沂水县千年古县，鱼鳞瓦屋顶
  • 青州市一圈一带集中连片保护利用模式
  • 蒙阴县岱崮镇军工文化园，保护与旅游结合
  • 从静态保护到活态利用的转变
  • 探索传统村落加融合发展模式

98. 济南明府城保护与更新

Jinan Ming Prefecture City Protection and Renewal
当代 文化遗产保护·城市更新 省级示范IP 济南市历下区明府城片区

济南明府城是济南历史文化名城的核心片区，依托城市更新打造为山东文化遗产保护的示范IP。明府城片区涵盖趵突泉、大明湖、芙蓉街、曲水亭街、府学文庙等核心历史文化区域，承载着济南泉水之城的历史文脉与城市记忆。山东秉持保护优先、以用促保的原则，推动历史文化与现代生活相融共生，出台多项技术导则，建立街区活化利用正负清单。

明府城的保护更新坚持真实保护原则，留住不同时期的城乡记忆，持续开展资源专项调查，加大资金投入，推进街区整治和历史建筑修缮。在保护历史风貌的前提下，引入文创店铺、书房、手作工坊等新业态，使老街区焕发新活力。芙蓉街作为济南最著名的历史商业街，在保留传统建筑风貌的基础上引入特色餐饮与文化创意产业；曲水亭街保留家家泉水、户户垂杨的泉水人居格局，成为济南泉水文化的活态展示窗口。

明府城的保护更新体现了山东保护优先、以用促保的理念，在守住遗产安全底线的同时实现历史街区的活态传承。2025年以来，全省历史文化街区、名镇名村接待游客突破三千七百万人次，文化遗产赋能文旅消费、拉动地方发展的作用持续显现。

— ENGLISH —

The Jinan Ming Prefecture City is the core area of Jinan Historic Cultural City, developed through urban renewal as a demonstration IP for Shandong cultural heritage protection.

The area encompasses key historic sites including Baotu Spring, Daming Lake, Furong Street, Qushuiting Street, and the Prefectural Confucian Temple, carrying Jinan historical identity as the City of Springs.

Shandong adheres to the principle of protection first, promotion through utilization, issuing technical guidelines and establishing positive and negative lists for district revitalization. The renewal preserves authentic historical character while introducing creative retail, bookshops, and workshops.

Furong Street maintains its traditional architectural character while hosting specialty food and cultural creative businesses. Qushuiting Street preserves the springs in every courtyard, willows at every door residential pattern, serving as a living display of Jinan spring culture.

Since 2025, historic districts and villages across the province have received over thirty-seven million visitors.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 济南历史文化名城核心片区，省级示范IP
  • 涵盖趵突泉、大明湖、芙蓉街、府学文庙
  • 保护优先、以用促保原则
  • 街区活化利用正负清单制度
  • 芙蓉街：传统风貌与文创产业结合
  • 曲水亭街：泉水人居格局活态展示
  • 2025年全省历史街区接待游客超3700万人次

99. 青岛中山路历史街区活化与里院更新

Qingdao Zhongshan Road Historic District and Liyuan Renewal
当代 文化遗产保护·街区活化 省级示范IP 青岛市市南区中山路片区

青岛中山路历史街区是青岛历史文化名城的核心商业街区，依托城市更新打造为山东文化遗产保护的示范IP。中山路始建于德占时期，是青岛最早的现代商业街，沿街分布着青岛俱乐部旧址、胶澳帝国法院旧址、德国警察署旧址等近代建筑，见证了青岛从渔村到近代城市的历史变迁。青岛以中山路为主线推进老城更新，在保留历史建筑风貌的基础上引入新业态，使百年老街焕发新活力。里院更新是青岛历史街区活化的核心内容。

广兴里作为青岛最大的里院建筑群，在修缮后引入文创、餐饮、展览等业态，成为大鲍岛文化休闲街区的重要组成部分。三江里在保留原有建筑风貌的基础上引入书房、精酿、手作工坊与文创店铺，旧时的青石板路配上新潮的花墙与店铺，蜕变成复古摩登的时尚街区。太兴里同样在保护修缮后实现了从传统居住功能向文旅综合体的转型。

青岛中山路与里院的活化更新体现了保护优先、以用促保的理念，在守住历史建筑真实性的同时注入当代功能，使老建筑成为承载城市记忆与当代生活的活态空间。青岛依托城市更新打造中山路示范IP，盘活坊茨小镇、烟台张裕等工业遗产，赋能城市文化名片。

— ENGLISH —

Qingdao Zhongshan Road Historic District is the core commercial street of Qingdao Historic Cultural City, developed through urban renewal as a demonstration IP for heritage protection.

Originally built during the German lease period, Zhongshan Road is Qingdao earliest modern commercial street, lined with landmarks including the former Qingdao Club, Kiautschou Imperial Court, and German Police Station. Liyuan renewal is central to the district revitalization.

Guangxing Li, Qingdao largest Liyuan complex, has been repaired and repurposed with creative retail, dining, and exhibition spaces. Sanjiang Li preserves its original architectural character while introducing bookshops, craft breweries, workshops, and creative retail.

Qingdao approach exemplifies protection first, promotion through utilization: preserving historical authenticity while injecting contemporary functions. The city has also revitalized industrial heritage sites such as Fangzi Town and the Changyu Winery.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 青岛最早现代商业街，省级示范IP
  • 沿街分布青岛俱乐部、胶澳法院、德国警察署等近代建筑
  • 广兴里：最大里院引入文创餐饮展览
  • 三江里：书房精酿手作工坊，复古摩登
  • 保护优先、以用促保理念实践
  • 盘活坊茨小镇、张裕等工业遗产
  • 老建筑成为承载城市记忆与当代生活的活态空间

100. 荣成烟墩角海草房生态旅游活化

Yandunjiao Seaweed House Eco-Tourism Revitalization, Rongcheng
当代 文化遗产保护·生态旅游 国家级传统村落 威海市荣成市烟墩角村

荣成市烟墩角村位于胶东半岛东端，是国家级传统村落，以独特的海草房建筑和冬季天鹅栖息地闻名。海草房是胶东沿海地区特有的民居形式，以石材砌墙、海草苫顶，厚实的海草屋顶可达四五十厘米，具有良好的保温隔热性能，适应胶东沿海冬寒夏湿、海风强劲的气候特点。

烟墩角村的海草房保存较为完整，村内现存海草房百余栋，多为清末至民国时期建造。近年来，烟墩角村开发了生态旅游模式：冬季游客观赏来此越冬的大天鹅，全年可入住传统海草房民宿体验胶东渔村生活。

这一模式将传统建筑保护与生态旅游有机结合，实现了文化遗产的活态利用。山东以荣成市为代表的胶东地区传统村落保护利用集中连片示范区，探索了海草房修缮保护与传统渔村活化利用的有效路径。

在保护海草房建筑真实性的前提下，完善基础设施和公共服务设施，提升人居环境质量，同时挖掘海草苫顶技艺等非物质文化遗产，培育传统技艺工匠，为古建修缮筑牢人才根基。烟墩角村的活化实践表明，传统建筑保护与当代生活可以实现共生共赢：海草房不仅是历史遗产，更是活态的居住空间和文化载体。

— ENGLISH —

Yandunjiao Village in Rongcheng, located at the eastern tip of the Jiaodong Peninsula, is a national-level traditional village famous for its unique seaweed houses and winter swan habitat.

Seaweed houses are a vernacular dwelling type unique to the Jiaodong coast, featuring stone walls and thick thatched roofs of seaweed reaching forty to fifty centimeters, providing excellent insulation against the region cold winters, humid summers, and strong sea winds.

Yandunjiao preserves over one hundred seaweed houses, mostly built from the late Qing to the Republican period.

In recent years, the village has developed an eco-tourism model: winter visitors observe overwintering whooper swans, while year-round guests stay in traditional seaweed house guesthouses. This model organically combines traditional architecture protection with eco-tourism.

Rongcheng represents the Jiaodong region concentrated traditional village protection zone, exploring effective paths for seaweed house restoration and fishing village revitalization.

Yandunjiao demonstrates that traditional architecture and contemporary life can coexist: seaweed houses are not merely historical relics but living spaces and cultural carriers.

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 国家级传统村落，海草房百余栋
  • 海草屋顶厚40-50厘米，保温隔热性能优良
  • 冬季天鹅栖息地，生态旅游模式
  • 海草房民宿体验胶东渔村生活
  • 胶东地区传统村落保护利用集中连片示范区
  • 海草苫顶技艺等非物质文化遗产传承
  • 传统建筑保护与生态旅游有机结合

101. 山东传统建筑工匠技艺传承与数字化保护

Craftsmanship Inheritance and Digital Preservation of Traditional Architecture
当代 文化遗产保护·技艺传承 省级保护体系 山东省全域

山东省高度重视传统建筑工匠技艺的传承与数字化保护，为古建修缮筑牢人才根基。山东省已培育千余名传统技艺工匠，涵盖木作、石作、砖作、瓦作、彩画、雕刻等多个工种，形成了较为完整的传统建筑工匠培养体系。

枣庄市创新开展三防抢救工程，鼓励引导土专家、土工匠参与传统建筑保护利用，采取传统技术进行修旧如旧，留住传统村落风貌，既保存了青砖黛瓦的村落肌理，又让传统建筑成为承载乡村文化、寄托乡愁记忆的活态载体。在数字化保护方面，山东省搭建数字资源平台，对新列入的中国传统村落全部实施挂牌赋码保护，统一保护标志牌设计样式，并融入二维码标识，通过扫码可查看村落信息。

济南章丘区石子口村位于古齐长城脚下，通过挖掘长城文化和石屋遗产，开发了研学旅游、乡愁集市等特色文旅IP，蒸汽小火车穿行山间谷地，游客可沿线观赏石屋和春秋时期齐长城遗址。山东持续挖掘遗产蕴含的文化基因，推出系列丛书，潍坊、泰安等六座名城登上《文脉春秋》纪录片。

丰富多彩的名城名镇特色活动持续举办，文化遗产赋能文旅消费、拉动地方发展的作用持续显现。山东省以文润城，讲好齐风鲁韵故事，让传统文化在保护中传承、在传承中发展。

— ENGLISH —

Shandong places great emphasis on the inheritance of traditional architectural craftsmanship and digital preservation, building a talent foundation for ancient building restoration.

The province has cultivated over one thousand traditional craftspeople covering carpentry, stonework, brickwork, tilework, polychrome painting, and carving.

Zaozhuang City has innovated a Three-Prevention Rescue Project that encourages local experts and craftsmen to participate in traditional building protection, using traditional techniques for restoring the old as the old.

In digital preservation, Shandong has built a digital resource platform and implemented tagging and coding protection for all newly listed Chinese Traditional Villages, with unified signage incorporating QR codes.

Shizikou Village in Jinan Zhangqiu District, at the foot of the ancient Qi Great Wall, has developed study tours and nostalgia markets as cultural tourism IPs, with a miniature steam train carrying tourists through mountain gullies.

Shandong continues to mine cultural genes embedded in heritage, publishing book series and featuring six cities in the documentary Wenmai Chunqiu (Cultural Lineage Chronicles).

关键特征 / Key Features

  • 培育千余名传统技艺工匠，涵盖多工种
  • 枣庄三防抢救工程，修旧如旧传统技术
  • 中国传统村落挂牌赋码保护，二维码标识
  • 石子口村蒸汽小火车、研学旅游、乡愁集市
  • 数字资源平台搭建，文化遗产数字化
  • 《文脉春秋》纪录片，潍坊泰安等六座名城
  • 以文润城，讲好齐风鲁韵故事
